A cleaner sees only whether an inspection of her own job passed or failed, and the date, never the score, the inspector's notes or the item list. *Why:* the board and the cleaner's job page sent scores (BACKEND-SCHEMA 6.1).
> - **Photos on damage and lost-item records are for the person who filed them,** for staff as well as cleaners; owners and managers see them all. Staff can still add a photo to a report they filed themselves.
> - **The cleaning board shows a cleaner the guest only in units where she has a job.**
> - **My pay is for cleaners only, on the page and in the data behind it** (`/api/my-pay`). Other roles get "My pay is for cleaners." (403). A clean a cleaner finished that is paid to someone else shows without that person's name or any money. *This replaces the round-5 default in BLOCKERS question 13 ("leave it as it is"), because the data still carried other cleaners' names.*
> - **Archiving an owner turns portal access off** and ends their sessions and unused sign-in links.
> - **Workspace modules decide what the screens show, not what the server answers.** An owner can switch modules at any time, and only ever reads their own workspace. *Why:* recorded so later checks don't file it as a leak.
>
> *Money*
> - **Longer stays never get a smaller discount.** Among the discounts that are on, weekly is at most the 14+ night discount, which is at most the monthly one. The listing form and the server both refuse an inverted ladder, with the reason at the field of the longer stay. The server checks an edit only when the edit sends a discount field, so an old listing that already has an inverted ladder can still change its name or price. *Why:* the price engine applies one discount, the longest that fits (PRD), so an inverted ladder made a month cost more per night than two weeks.
> - **A payout must say which statement month it is for, and the month must be between 2000 and 2100.** Without a month, the "owner owes you" and over-payment checks (D-76, D-81) could be skipped. The same year range applies to the statement screens.
> - **"Channel fees" means channel commission, and the fees inside revenue are "cleaning and extras".** Stay reports, Home and profit and loss use these words; Stay reports shows cleaning and extras as its own column.
> - **Previews never show a figure the server refuses** (a negative charge price shows no total).
> - **Amount filters work like date filters:** an amount that can't be used (negative, or more than 10 digits) or a backwards range ("from" above "to") says so at the field and the filter is off, with no filter chip. Payments and Expenses show one "Amount (₱)" chip.
>
> *Cleaning and operations*
> - **Copy for cleaner is not offered on a cancelled job,** so a cancelled job can never read as live work.
> - **Inspect is not offered on a paid clean,** the same as Reopen (D-81).
> - **Reopening an older clean, or failing its inspection, doesn't mark the unit dirty when a newer clean of the same unit is already done.** Closes the gap noted in BLOCKERS round-5 question 2.
> - **Quantities above 100,000 are refused before the "Not enough on hand" question.**
> - **Status chips with no matches say "No results found"** and Clear filters returns to the default status (maintenance, tenants, properties). With no filter set, a chip keeps its own empty text.
>
> *Reviews, notifications and support*
> - **Reviews ignore a filter id that is not a real id** (D-77: unknown ids in filters are ignored), so a bad `unit_id` or `property_id` answers the full list instead of a 404. The "matched" count and average cover all matches, not only the 200 rows sent back.
> - **A support rating can be given once** (409 `ALREADY_RATED` the second time).
> - **A notification setting can't be saved with an empty property list.** The server refuses it with a message at the field ("Pick at least one property, or choose all properties."). *Why:* an empty list silently muted the account.
>
> *Screens*
> - **A failed portal invite says so,** and the owner card offers "Send the invite again" while access is on.
> - **Owners with no units are left out of a month's statements** unless a statement was already saved for them.
> - **Each problem is said once, at its field.** Banners and price summaries don't repeat a field message. This includes a taken email on Get started: one message at the email field, "This email is already registered. Log in or reset your password instead." (with the Taglish version on the landing page).
> - **A blank or badly written id in a page address shows "Not found"** (for example `#/payments/%20`), never a raw error.
> - **The launchpad counts skipped steps separately:** "N of 11 steps done · K skipped", and the bar shows done steps only.
> - **The settings activity log shows "Changed: <labels>"** for edits, with the names of the fields and no values. *Why:* values can be private.
>
> *Why:* each call takes the narrowest reading of the access matrix, keeps money rules in one place, and stops the app offering anything it can't do.
>
> *Addendum 2026-10-14 (re-verify, fix C22):* The re-verify pass found staff were still getting data the access table (6.2) denies, so the narrowest reading was applied to the rest of the reservation data.
> - **Staff no longer receive the house rules text or the time the guest accepted them** on reservations (matrix 6.2).
> - **The payment-recording response no longer carries the guest's contact details or flags to staff.**
> - **Internal payment idempotency keys are never returned**, to any role.
> - **Hidden guest details now apply to every role except owner and manager.** The check is an allow-list, so a role added later sees nothing extra by default.
> - **Empty-state rule:** a status tab with nothing in it keeps its own text (for example "No archived tenants"). "No results found" with Clear filters shows only when a search or another filter is set. In Maintenance, a non-default status chip counts as a filter. This is the rule, not a bug (it was reported as one in round 6 and closed).

> **D-83 · 2026-10-14 · QA round 7 calls (2026-10-14 sweep, fixed the same day).** The seventh QA sweep (12 lanes, 88 entries including items carried from round 6) found problems that needed a product decision. These are the calls made, with the default we are using until the founder says otherwise. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 7". Each call was checked against the code after the fixes landed.
>
> *Money*
> - **Recording a payment above the balance asks first.** "This is ₱X more than the balance of ₱Y. Record it?" Cancel keeps the form and records nothing ("Payment not saved."). The guest's receipt is emailed only after the host says yes. This is a question on the screen only: the server still accepts an overpayment, because a guest can overpay (D-32) and the refund flow handles it. *Why:* rent payments and owner payouts already ask (D-76); a receipt for a wrong amount went to a guest. *(Superseded in part by D-84, 2026-10-14: Cancel on an over-amount question is now quiet everywhere, with no "Payment not saved." message. The question itself stays.)*
> - **A cancelled group shows the money it holds.** The Groups list shows a cancelled group as "Cancelled" with "₱X held" when its rooms had payments, and the group page adds "Held from cancelled rooms". Only owners and managers get this figure; staff never see "held" (staff see groups without money). *Why:* the list showed ₱0.00 while the page showed ₱1,000 paid.
> - **A quote never offers ₱0 from your rates.** When a listing has no nightly rate for some nights, the quote says "This listing has no nightly rate for some nights. Enter the total yourself.", leaves the amount empty and shows no "Use ₱0" button.
> - **Previews follow the server's rules first:** a deposit deduction above the deposit, a booking-window rule out of range, a negative base rate and a next-due date more than a year back are refused at the field before any question or preview figure. Bulk "Set a price" warns about nights below the lowest price, as the percentage change does.
> - **Gap-night rules run from 1 to 7 days** (`RULE_DAYS` in `src/routes/pricing.js`: last-minute 0 to 30, far-out 31 to 540, gap night 1 to 7). The rule form checks the days before it asks "Change the existing rule?".
> - **The price preview labels a night "Adjusted"** when a booking-window rule or discount changed it from the base rate. A night that is already booked still reads "Booked".
> - **"1,5" is refused everywhere,** as D-79 says. APP-FLOW said New reservation read it as 1.5; that line was wrong and is corrected.
> - **Both workspaces need a nightly rate on every new unit.** Add unit asks for a base nightly rate in short-term workspaces and in Both workspaces, even when the unit is only rented monthly (founder question 5). Units already saved without a rate show "No nightly rate yet".
>
> *Cleaning*
> - **An outside cleaner can't have an active team member's name.** Typing a name that matches someone on the team (spaces and capitals ignored), for a rate or a job, is refused with "Pick <name> from the team list instead." The check covers adding or changing a rate and adding or editing a cleaning task. *Why:* the clean was paid at a second rate and wasn't linked to her login. Rates already saved that way are left alone (read-only check for the founder in BLOCKERS). The remove-rate dialog now names the amount and the person.
> - **My pay shows cleans paid to someone else as "Paid to another cleaner",** with no amount, as BACKEND-SCHEMA 6.1 says (answers BLOCKERS round 6 "My pay: cleans paid to someone else" with its default).
>
> *Access*
> - **Staff reservation data is an allow-list.** Staff and cleaners get a fixed set of reservation fields (`STAFF_RESERVATION_KEYS` in `src/routes/stays.js`); a new booking column stays hidden from them until someone adds it. The booking `source` is not in the set (matrix 6.2 denies booking sources to staff; founder question 7).
> - **A failed receipt email gives staff a fixed plain sentence,** not the email provider's text; owners and managers still see the details.
> - **Non-owners see the plan's status, not its price or listing limit.** Managers, staff and cleaners still receive `plan`, `name`, `ai`, `read_only`, `reason`, `is_demo` and the dates they need to read the status: `trial_ends_at`, `trial_days_left`, `paid_until` and `deletion_scheduled_at`. Only `price` and `listing_limit` are removed.
> - **Staff and cleaners don't get a lease's document id** (the document itself was already refused).
> - **Managers may archive and restore units and listings in the properties assigned to them,** from the property page, as BACKEND-SCHEMA 6 ("Same, assigned") and APP-FLOW section 2 ("add/archive units") say and the server does. The Listings page's own Archive button stays owner-only. *Why:* the Listings page note in APP-FLOW read as a rule; the access table is the rule. Founder question 6.
> - **Template language stays owner-only (D-28).** The access table row that said managers could switch it is corrected.
> - **Every team member sees the workspace owner's name and email** on their account page, so they know whom to ask. Recorded in the matrix (founder question 4).
> - **Team invites say the same as sign-up** when an email already has an account ("This email already has a BalayHub account. Use a different email for each workspace."), once at the field plus a generic banner. This follows the round 1 sign-up default (keep the friendly message).
>
> *Activity log*
> - **The activity log records only what really changed,** by field name, never values, for every edit: listings and properties, the guide, the gate pass, guest experience, automatic messages, cleaning tasks, recurring jobs and supplies. A save that changes nothing writes no row. "14:00" and "14:00:00", or "4200.00" and "4200", count as the same. Numbers and yes/no values are compared as numbers or yes/no only when one side really is a number or a yes/no; two pieces of text are always compared as text, so a door code or Wi-Fi password change ("0123" to "123", "1234" to "1234.0") is never hidden. Resuming a recurring job reads "Resumed a maintenance schedule", and a cleaning task edit reads "Edited a cleaning task". *Why:* round 6 fixed how rows are shown; the rows themselves still listed every field sent, and this class had come back three rounds running. One shared check (`src/lib/auditdiff.js`, `changedKeys`) now decides for all of them. The security check found one gap in the first version (digits-only text compared as numbers, which hid door code changes); it was fixed in the same round.
>
> *Screens*
> - **Filter values in a link that aren't real options are dropped** (calendar tag, availability and cleaning; inbox listing and unknown listing or property ids; maintenance priority), so the list and the dropdowns always agree (D-77). The inbox Listing dropdown names the unit, with the property only when two units share a name.
> - **Guest pages show the "link not valid" and "not available" sentences in Taglish** (check-in, review, guidebook, quote, and two booking page sentences).
> - **The booking page keeps "At least 1 guest."** *(Wording changed 2026-10-14, D-85: "Enter a whole number of guests, 1 or more.")* at the field (only the server's guest-limit text is cleared when a new quote is requested), and no quote is asked for fewer than 1 guest.
> - **The More menu on phones has a Close button, listed first so it takes focus, and keeps focus inside** while open.
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* a long-term owner reaching stay APIs (D-82 modules rule); add-on descriptions in one language (D-81); a manager's all-listings season answering 400 with the message (round 6); past nights' prices and blocks can be edited (founder question 1); staff can still read the Reviews page and booking group notes (open round 6 questions). D-82's "no review card" wording for staff applies to guest profiles and reservations, not to the Reviews page. The cleaner redirect with no toast is on purpose (APP-FLOW route guard).
>
> *Why:* each call follows the access table and the existing money rules, asks before money moves, and stops the screens offering what the server refuses.

> **D-84 · 2026-10-14 · QA round 8 calls (2026-10-14 sweep, fixed the same day).** The eighth QA sweep (12 lanes, 123 entries including items carried from round 7) found problems that needed a product decision. These are the calls made, with the default we are using until the founder says otherwise. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 8". Each call was checked against the code after the fixes landed (34 planned fix lanes C01 to C34, plus four follow-up lanes C35 to C38).
>
> *Privacy*
> - **Get started answers belong to the email they were typed for.** The setup page fills its form with them only for that email (after a Google sign-up, where the email is not known yet, for 30 minutes), and waits for you to press Create; they are never posted on their own. Log out, "Use a different account" and a signed-in person who already has a workspace all delete them. *Why:* on a shared browser a second account got the first account's business name and mobile number. *Carried to round 9:* the Google path should use a one-time token instead of the 30-minute window.
>
> *Money*
> - **A rent row's Record button starts at that month's remaining amount;** the dialog still shows the whole balance, and the tenant's own Record still starts at the whole balance (founder question 2).
> - **An amount above ₱99,999,999.99 is refused at its field ("Amount received is too large." and its siblings) before any money question.**
> - **Cancel on an over-amount question ("Record more than the balance?", "Record more than is owed?", the owner payout question) just returns to the form, with no message,** in reservations, rent and owner payouts. *Supersedes D-83's "(Payment not saved.)" detail; the question itself is unchanged.*
> - **Every date typed into BalayHub is between 2000 and 2100** (payments, expenses, refunds, deposits, payouts, cleaner pay, stays, leases; the check is in `validate.js`). Extends D-82's payout month range to every date. *Known exception, recorded so it isn't filed:* the report and list filters that use `isValidDate` still accept 1900 to 2200; to be tightened in a later round.
> - **Expense answers never carry the internal idempotency key,** for any role (completes the D-82 addendum).
> - **A quote or booking can't have more guests than the listing allows** (`max_guests`), the same as reservations and the booking page: "This listing allows up to N guests."
> - **"Any free room of a type" quotes the listing BalayHub would pick now** (free and not on a lease) and names it; with none free the answer is 409 `NO_FREE_ROOM`. D-81's empty price boxes stay.
> - **A percentage-model unit owner needs a management fee:** a blank fee box is refused ("Management fee is required.") on add and on edit; it is not kept as the old fee or replaced by the 20% default. A flat-fee owner has no percentage.
>
> *Cleaning and supplies*
> - **A job's Supplies used asks owners and managers first,** as the Supplies page does (D-77): nothing on hand is refused on that row; more than on hand asks "Record what is on hand" and only then sends `record_on_hand`. The server refuses the same cases without it. Cleaners and staff keep the skip-and-name behaviour, unchanged; only cleaners are told to tell their manager. The Supplies page's record-use keeps its cap.
> - **Cleaner pay acts only on what you can see:** changing a filter drops ticks on rows that are no longer shown.
> - **An outside cleaner's name can't be renamed on an existing rate** (past pay is filed under that name). Remove the rate and add it again.
> - **The team-name refusal no longer repeats the person's name** and says how to add them to the property (closes the round 7 security note).
> - **Owners and managers can edit a cleaning task's notes** on the job page.
>
> *Access*
> - **Unit-level damage reports (`POST /units/:id/damage`) are for owners and managers;** staff are refused (403), and report damage from a job or a stay (BACKEND-SCHEMA 6.1).
> - **A manager hitting the plan's listing limit is told to ask the owner,** without the number (follows D-83's plan rule).
> - **The deposit receipt answer names the guest's email address only to owners and managers;** everyone else reads "the guest".
>
> *Activity log*
> - **Every edit records only what really changed, by field name, never values, and a save that changes nothing writes no row.** Round 8 finished the list: workspace modules and default language, the booking page settings (including Welcome text, and the PayMongo key by name only), manual payment details, team access, your profile, notification settings on the Account page and in Settings (which now logs at all), unit owners, guests, booking groups, tenants, leases, repair requests, pricing rules, add-ons, message templates and the cleaning checklist. Unit owner edits write `unit_owner.updated` with `{ fields }`. *Exception, recorded so it isn't filed:* "Edited a reservation" (`reservation.updated`) keeps the old and new amounts of the fields that changed, because it is a money record; the screen shows the field names only.
>
> *Screens and links*
> - **Filter values from a link or a saved filter that aren't real options are dropped by the shared filter bar,** on every list page, and a search of spaces is no search. A page whose options arrive late sets `keepUnknown` so a valid value is not dropped (maintenance "Assigned to"; cleaner pay "Cleaner" until its list loads). The reviews, unit owners and inbox lists ignore ids that aren't in your workspace. Other lists answer an empty list to a direct request with such an id (no data either way); the pages never send one. *Makes D-77's "unknown ids are ignored" precise.*
> - **Sort and view words from a link are checked against the page's own list** (`own()` in `src/lib/pick.js`), so `?sort=constructor` or `__proto__` is just an unknown word and no longer a server error.
> - **Tab lists work with the keyboard everywhere:** one shared handler gives arrow keys, Home and End on every `role="tablist"` (D-81). The inbox composer mode switches are toggle buttons (`aria-pressed`), not tabs.
> - **Repeated buttons and links name their row everywhere** (D-81, now swept file by file), and a dialog that redraws after an action keeps focus on a sensible control.
> - **A guest link with its code missing shows the page's own "not valid" card,** in the guest's language, never the marketing home. A signed-in page with a missing id says "This link is incomplete".
> - **Switching language on the booking and quote pages keeps everything the guest typed or picked.** Tab titles follow the language and keep the host's name.
> - **After a form error the keyboard focus stays in the form.** An explicit Cancel discards a saved draft; closing a dialog any other way keeps it (founder question 3).
> - **A field shows its error instead of its hint while the error stands** (D-82 "said once").
> - **A listing keeps its type** (Bedspace and Commercial space are offered on the listing form and filter).
> - **A listing's public page address must start and end with a letter or number** (3 to 60 characters, lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens inside).
> - **The share link source starts on "Other"** (founder question 4).
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the setup page is English only (host page); add-on quantities are capped in the box (D-81); a past next-due date asks again on later edits (D-81); a long-term owner reaching stay APIs (D-82); a payout to an owner with no units skips the owes check (founder question 8); the Wi-Fi password box has no Show/Hide (founder question 7); other server lists answer an empty list, not a dropped filter, to a direct request with an unknown id; re-archiving an add-on writes a second row; `GET /api/units/:id/channels` still creates the iCal token on read; the admin support reply is still a toast.
>
> *Carried to round 9 (from the lane reports and the security check):* the Google sign-up path should use a one-time token instead of the 30-minute window; several compare-then-write saves are not yet locked (booking page settings `directbooking.js`, workspace edit in `auth.js`, pricing rule edit, cleaning checklist, inbox template and automation edit, stays guest and group edit, maintenance schedule) and should read inside a transaction with `FOR UPDATE`; the router's empty-parameter check covers only the last path segment; focus after a redraw in pricing, rent, refunds and cleaner pay (Mark paid, Undo, rate save); the account page has one unnamed Save; the report and list date filters still accept 1900 to 2200.
>
> *Why:* each call follows the access table and the money rules, asks before money or stock moves, acts only on what the person sees, and keeps one account's data away from another.

> **D-85 · 2026-10-14 · Alpha with small hosts, and QA round 9 calls (2026-10-14 sweep, fixed the same day).** Founder direction: "test it in alpha first to check how beneficial it is for the 1-3 property owners." The Guesty feature recommendations (https://claude.ai/artifact/NU1YwskX8n4jyGM8BoX59h) are parked as reference only; nothing from them is being built. From now on, QA puts friction for an owner of 1 to 3 properties first: anything that blocks, confuses or misleads a small host, and anything touching money or guests. Screens that show too much for a small host are grouped or collapsed, never cut. The ninth QA sweep (8 lanes, 94 entries including carried items) was fixed in 18 code lanes (C01 to C18). These are the calls made, with the default in use until the founder says otherwise. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 9". Each call was checked against the code after the fixes landed. *Amended 2026-10-15 (D-90): the founder asked for a Guesty-style top bar; that one item is built, the rest stays parked.*
>
> *Money*
> - **One shared balance rule: total, less the channel fee (channel bookings only), less payments, plus refunds** (voided payments and refunds left out). It is written once in `src/lib/balance.js` and used everywhere a balance shows: reservation, list and CSV, pay link, receipts, daily close, owner portal, message templates, the assistant and the inbox. A refund past the overpayment on a live stay now shows the money owed again. *Supersedes the balance clause of D-34 and its repeats in D-77, D-82 and D-83 (each annotated above). The refund cap, never more than was paid, stands.* *Why:* a ₱1,000 refund on a ₱600 stay paid ₱1,000 said "Nothing more is owed" while ₱600 was owed. This was the round's one severe bug.
> - **A refund that would leave money owed on a live stay asks first** (founder question 1). Default: allowed up to what was paid, after the question.
> - **Every peso amount the server writes shows two decimals** (₱1,500.50), through one function, `fmtPeso` in `src/lib/messaging.js`. *Guest pages refined 2026-10-15 by D-86 (advertised prices may be short).* This includes messages, emails, labels, the discount list and the public `/stay` pages ("From ₱2,500.00"). The two-decimal "From" price on `/stay` is a call the founder may want to change (BLOCKERS).
> - **A rent payment goes to the oldest unpaid month first** (as PRD says). The Record form now says which month gets the money. "Partly paid" counts every month with some money on it, overdue or not. The round 8 question stays open.
> - **A clean you do yourself, with no pay rate for you, is "Done by you, no pay"** and isn't counted as unpaid. A clean with a rate stays unpaid with its amount. It is a computed label (`pay_status: 'self'`), not a stored status.
> - **Discount codes can't contain spaces** when an owner creates or edits one; the message suggests dashes ("Try QA-R9-BAD."). Guests typing a code are still forgiven a space.
> - **A new unit owner's management fee starts empty;** you type it. The Unit owners page tells an owner-operator they don't need one.
> - **Profit and loss counts finished stays by check-out date;** Reports counts by check-in. Each page says so (D-44 stands).
> - **A unit that belongs to another owner moves only after you confirm,** naming both owners ("Move this unit?"). Statements and payouts follow the unit from then on.
>
> *Guests*
> - **The check-in form can't be saved with an empty guest row:** "Enter the name of each guest, or remove the empty row." The host note says "booked for N" when fewer names than booked guests were given.
> - **New automatic messages start turned off,** as the PRD always said; you turn one on when the wording is right. The Add form's "Turned on" box starts empty. Two automatic messages can't share a name.
> - **The pay page's add-on boxes say "Select";** the button says "Add selected to my bill".
> - **After a guest picks dates, the booking page price line shows this stay's average nightly price,** before any discount, not the lowest "from" price.
> - **A payment-provider failure tells the guest "Online payment isn't available right now."** and does not blame the host. The real reason goes to the server log only.
>
> *Small-host screens (alpha)*
> - **The menu is grouped: Every day, Money, More tools, Setup,** on the sidebar and the phone's More sheet. More tools starts folded. Nothing was removed.
> - **Add and Edit listing show Basics first;** Price extras, Guest info, Booking page and payments, and Booking rules are collapsed sections that open on a tap (and open themselves when an error is inside). The pricing explainer is folded under "How pricing works". The section formerly named "Pricing helpers" is now "Price extras" everywhere. Reservation channel and property filters are under More filters.
> - **New reservation asks "Where did they book?"** for direct bookings (Facebook, Messenger and the other D-61 sources). There is no Walk-in source: a walk-in is saved as Other, and a source on a channel booking is refused. Editing dates offers a new price from your rates.
> - **The Unit-count card reads "1–3 units".** The Launchpad can bring back the Home reminder; the calendar step ticks only when a sync worked; the guest-info step counts house rules. **The trial banner shows only in the last 3 days.**
> - **Cleaning photos show a small preview** instead of a file icon.
>
> *Screens and keyboard*
> - **Focus comes back after every save or redraw:** a dialog returns focus to the button that opened it (or its replacement), and a tab row that is drawn again keeps focus on the same tab (completes D-81's tab rule).  *Replaced 2026-10-15 by D-89's single focus guard.*
> - **No question is asked about a save that can't succeed:** dates outside 2000 to 2100 are refused at the field first, and while a question is open the form's button shows its own label, not "Saving…".
> - **Filter links drop bad values from the address too,** and date filters use 2000 to 2100 like every other date (completes D-84).
> - **A guest count must be a whole number from 1:** "Enter a whole number of guests, 1 or more." (D-84's "At least 1 guest." is annotated above.)
>
> *Decision conflicts settled (the code now behaves as written here)*
> - **D-34 against live-stay refunds:** D-85 wins; see the balance rule.
> - **PRD "automatic messages are created off" against the Add form's "Turned on" box:** the PRD wins; the box starts empty.
> - **PRD "payments go to the oldest charge first" against APP-FLOW "Record starts at that month's amount":** not a real conflict. One is where the money goes, the other is what the box is filled with. The form now names the month that gets the money.
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the refund cap is what was paid (D-34); rent is applied oldest month first (round 8 question); P&L and Reports keep their different bases (D-44); `reservation.updated` keeps changed amounts (D-84); the admin support reply toast (round 8 question); internal ids on staff deposit answers and the unlocked compare-then-write saves are round 10 items.
>
> *Why:* the alpha host has one to three properties and little time. Money must never read as settled when it isn't, guests must never be messaged or registered wrongly, and the first screens must show what a small host needs first.

> **D-86 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 10 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The tenth QA sweep (8 lanes, 71 entries including carried items) was fixed in 14 code lanes (C01 to C14, plus two small follow-up lanes for tests). The calls below follow D-85: friction for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 10". Suite result at the merge: 2101 of 2103 passed; the two that did not were one inbox test that depends on the time of day around midnight in Manila (already on the carry list) and one old test whose pattern was fixed straight after. Security review: PASS.
>
> *Privacy*
> - **An unsent internal note stays a note after a reload.** The inbox saves whether a draft is a note or a message, next to the draft text; an older draft with no saved mode opens as an internal note, so sending to the guest is always a deliberate switch. *Why:* a reloaded note came back as a guest message, one click from being emailed to the guest. This was the round's one severe bug.
>
> *Money and data*
> - **A lease can't start over booked short stays.** Creating a lease, or moving its end later or clearing it, is refused with 409 `LEASE_OVER_STAYS` while a stay that is not cancelled falls inside it. The message names up to 5 stays and the first day the lease could start, and nothing is written. *Why:* completes the rule that a unit can't be both leased and let by the night (it was only enforced from the stay side).
> - **Changing a reservation's dates asks "Use the new price?"** on Save when the accommodation amount still matches the old dates and your rates give a different price, as APP-FLOW always said. A host who typed a new amount is not asked.
> - **The refund question appears only when a refund turns an overpayment into money owed,** and names both numbers. A refund above what is left is refused at the Amount field first, with the server's own sentence and no question. *Refined 2026-10-15 by D-87: any refund that leaves money owed asks.*
> - **A receipt's "Remaining balance" is the balance at that payment:** refunds recorded after that payment are left out. (Closes the round 9 carry item.) *(Extended in round 24: see D-106. Every money line on a receipt is now as of that payment, not only the balance.)*
> - **The guest pay page lists refunds** ("Refunded to you") so its lines add up to the balance. The bill carries only the refund amount and date, never the reason or who recorded it.
> - **Money on guest pages:** any amount owed, charged, paid, refunded or totalled shows two decimals. Advertised prices (per night, "from") show whole pesos when exact, and calendar tiles may show a short "₱2.8k" with the exact price in the tile's accessible name. *Refines D-85's "two decimals everywhere" for the guest pages.*
>
> *Guests*
> - **Switching English and Tagalog keeps what the guest typed** on every guest page that redraws itself (check-in, review, pay), through one shared helper in `i18n.js`. Photos chosen but not yet uploaded cannot be kept by a browser, so the check-in page asks the guest to choose them again.
> - **The pay page's failure text is the agreed sentence** (D-85), like the quote and booking pages. If the host has turned online payment off, the guest still sees "Online payment is not available for this booking. Please contact your host." (409 `NOT_ACCEPTING`).
> - **"Dates" is "Petsa" in Taglish; "Guests" stays "Guests"** (everyday Taglish).
>
> *Small-host screens (alpha)*
> - **The booking page can't be turned on with no listing ticked,** on screen. Turn off is never gated. *The server does not refuse yet; carried to round 11.* *Server refusal added 2026-10-15 (D-87).*
> - **Add listing's first screen asks only for what a stay needs** (building, listing name, type, nightly rate, cleaning fee, maximum guests). Address, property type, room type and tags are under "More details"; the deposit is under "Booking page and payments" and isn't required (blank saves 50%).
> - **Cleaner pay and Unit owners are under More tools** until the founder says otherwise.
> - **The plan card counts "Short-term listings"** and says monthly units are not counted.
> - **Lost and found has "+ Add item"** for owners and managers (a listing, what it is, where, date found).
> - **Supplies used with no supplies set up** says so and points to the Supplies page (owners and managers) or to the manager (cleaners), instead of a Save that can't work.
> - **Plain wording:** the inbox banner no longer sends the host to "whoever set up BalayHub"; the rent roll caption, the recurring-job date range, the cleaner-rate messages, the cancelled group header and the support ticket tracker say what they mean. The search box on Reservations reads "Guest, code or listing".
> - **Long-term owners get a monthly-rent reports guide;** the short-term reports guide is hidden for them.
> - **Printing a rent payment prints an acknowledgement receipt** (not a BIR official receipt), with no receipt number yet.
> - **Report tiles say how they count** (for example "Rooms counted by night; cleaning and extras by check-in"). The ways of counting themselves are unchanged (D-44, D-85).
>
> *Screens and keyboard*
> - **Focus is kept after every save or redraw, even a slow one:** BalayHub watches for up to 2 seconds and puts focus back on the same control, its renamed replacement in the same place (Mark clean to Mark dirty), or the page heading; never on something the person already moved to. Buttons inside dialogs are covered, and tab rows wait for the new row to be drawn. *Completes D-85's focus rule, which stopped watching before the redraw arrived.* Views add no focus code of their own for redraws. *Extended to 10 seconds and to dialogs with no opener 2026-10-15 (D-87).* *Extended to navigation, in-place reloads, re-enabled buttons and error targets 2026-10-15 (D-88). A view's own redraw after a dialog save still lost focus; carried to round 13.*  *Replaced 2026-10-15 by D-89's single focus guard.*
> - **A form's own field checks run with the required check,** so "Accommodation is required" no longer hides "Check-out must be after check-in" (New quote, New group).
> - **Link-style buttons are at least 24px tall** (the minimum for inline text links); buttons keep the 44px house size. Chart "Show as table" toggles are 32px tall on phones.
> - **Every date box in reports uses 2000 to 2100,** custom report dates and a month in a Profit and loss link included. A bad month in the address shows "That month isn't valid, so this is <this month>." and the address is rewritten.
> - **A past day's close lists damage reports reported by that day and still open,** under "Damage reports still open".
> - **The calendar-link error is announced** as an alert, marks the link field invalid and moves focus to it.
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the platform Admin menu shows only to addresses in `PLATFORM_ADMIN_EMAILS` (local dev includes stays@); automatic message names stay app-checked with no database index (same reason as add-on names); Reopen from Done goes to In progress; staff report access waits on the open founder question; managers view and use discount codes but only the owner creates or changes them (D-28; APP-FLOW corrected).
>
> *Why:* a small host must never send a private note to a guest by accident, never let a unit twice, never under-bill a changed stay, and never lose a keyboard user's place.

---

> **D-87 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 11 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The eleventh QA sweep (8 lanes, 74 entries including carried items) was fixed in 16 code lanes (C01 to C16), plus two small follow-up lanes (C10b for the pay ceiling on a single clean, C12b for the reopen audit row and test fixtures). The calls below follow D-85: friction for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 11". No severe bug this round. Suite result at the merge: 2214 of 2214 passed after the C12b follow-up (the first full run was 2211 of 2213, both failures were fixed). Security review: PASS.
>
> *Money*
> - **A receipt shows an overpayment** as "Overpaid by ₱800.00" instead of "Remaining balance ₱0.00", on the page and in the email. *Why:* the receipt has to add up and agree with the reservation screen.
> - **A refund that leaves the guest owing asks first,** whether or not the guest was overpaid before ("After this ₱100.00 refund the guest will owe ₱100.00. Record it?"). A refund on a cancelled booking never asks. *Refines D-86's refund question.* The cap sentence shows once, at the Amount field.
> - **An occupied unit shows its lease rent,** and the Rent roll adds up lease rents ("Monthly rent from current leases"). The list rent shows beside it when different.
> - **A voided rent payment can't be printed as a receipt;** its printed block says VOID.
> - **A cleaner rate is at most ₱10,000.00 per clean or ₱1,000.00 per hour,** and the pay typed on a single clean is at most ₱10,000.00. *Why:* an extra zero must not become a payout. Rates saved before this change are not touched (founder questions in BLOCKERS).
> - **Returning a security deposit before check-out asks first.** It is still allowed (cancellations and short stays need it). *Annotates PRD F14.*
>
> *Guests*
> - **Room charge in Taglish is "Tuluyan";** the deposit line is "{pct}% na deposit"; the online-payment failure sentences are translated; the pay page's refund line reads "Refunded to you, added back to the balance". A native-speaker check is in BLOCKERS.
> - **The booking page uses D-85's guest wording** ("Enter a whole number of guests, 1 or more."), and a count above the listing's limit names that limit.
> - **A bad listing link says the listing isn't available,** not that the host stopped taking bookings.
> - **The check-in form starts with no blank guest row;** "+ Add guest" adds one. The empty-row rule (D-85) stands.
> - **The review page's "choose a star rating" message shows under the stars** and goes away when the review is sent.
> - **The booking page can't be turned on with no bookable listing, on the server too** (409 `NO_LISTING`; the listing needs a nightly rate). *Completes D-86.*
>
> *Small-host screens (alpha)*
> - **A day closed by mistake can be reopened** ("Reopen day"; owners any close, managers only a close that covers just their own properties, never a whole-workspace close). A past day's close lists arrivals, departures and cleanings as they stood at the end of that day. The reopen is written to the activity log with the old note, who closed it, when, which properties and the saved summary.
> - **Maintenance "Open & in progress" shows only open and in-progress requests.** A resolved request offers only Reopen; an in-progress one offers "Back to open".
> - **An owner's own finished clean reads "Done by you, no pay"** on the task too (D-85 wording). *Did not show on the task page until 2026-10-15 (D-88): the server now says whether the viewer finished the clean.*
> - **"Something broken?" for an owner or manager logs a repair** ("Log repair"); cleaners still send it to the host.
> - **A cleaner who uses more than is on hand is told** how much was recorded (the server answers with `short`).
> - **Inbox:** a second template replaces the first template's subject (a typed subject stays, as APP-FLOW says); the AI draft button is hidden in Internal note mode; Snooze 1 week wakes at 9 AM; template fields say their limits.
> - **Wording and messages:** "Item" in Lost and found; "Enter a quantity of at least 1." for a use or restock; a quote's guest count uses D-85's wording; a group names the room with no listing; a report link outside 2000 to 2100 says so; a single date filter flags a bad year; long-term owners' delete and download texts name tenants and leases; "Monthly units are not counted" only when there are monthly units; the new-building fields on Add listing are marked required and shown first when there is no building yet (the name box reads "Building / property name"); "Not verified yet" on the Check your email page, which now follows the chosen language.
> - **Charts on an empty account show one 0 label,** not 1% or ₱1 ticks. "Show as table" sits at the left of the chart, clear of the Ask button. The sidebar shows a cue when more is below.
>
> *Screens and keyboard*
> - **Focus is kept after every dialog and redraw, even with no opener or a slow network:** a dialog opened by a link (`?new=1`, `?assign=1`) hands focus to the page heading when it closes, and BalayHub keeps watching for up to 10 seconds while focus is on the page. *Completes D-86's focus rule (2 seconds was too short on a slow network).*  *Replaced 2026-10-15 by D-89's single focus guard.*
> - **Every wrong field in a new or edited reservation is reported at once;** the stay quote and the calendar refuse dates outside 2000 to 2100.
> - **A lease and a short stay saved at the same instant can't both pass:** both lock the unit first (round 10 security note).
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* Housekeeping stays under More on phones (founder question); a rent acknowledgement has no balance-after line yet (founder question); the booking calendar's day buttons are reached by Tab, not arrow keys; 22px date links rely on the spacing exception; the refund cap is what was paid (D-34).
>
> *Why:* a small host must see true numbers on every receipt and rent roll, must not pay a typo, and must be able to undo a mistake.

> **D-88 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 12 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twelfth QA sweep (8 lanes, 84 entries including carried items) was fixed in 14 code lanes (C01 to C14), plus follow-up lanes for test lines and for the sign-up language (C06b). The calls below follow D-85: friction for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 12". No severe bug this round. Suite result at the merge: 2,315/2,315 (final run after C06b). Security review: PASS, with two info notes (`inbox.js` line 578 should use `?.archived`; `reservations.js` and `listings.js` pass an extra argument to `apiFilters`). **The round was not clean:** the re-verify found the focus problem again after in-place saves (SEV2) plus minor items; all are carried to round 13 (see IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN session notes).
>
> *Money*
> - **Every list sends its default tab to the server.** The Rent page's "All unpaid" shows only unpaid charges. One shared rule (`apiFilters`) and a test that checks every list page. *Second time for this class (round 11 fixed Maintenance by hand).*
> - **Record payment on a direct booking starts with no method chosen;** the host picks one. Channel bookings still start on "Channel payout". *(Airbnb bookings start on Cash since 2026-10-15, D-99 O4.)*
> - **The payment card says "Overpaid ₱800.00",** the same word as the alert and the receipt. **"You keep" shows only once the channel fee is known;** until then the card asks for the fee and shows the total.
> - **Ending a lease moves an unpaid move-in charge's due date to the move-out day,** and editing the lease's end date does the same. The full month is still billed (PRD).
> - **The Rent cards say they count all unpaid rent** when the list is narrowed. **P&L and Reports each say what they count.**
> - **A nightly rate above ₱50,000.00 asks "Save anyway?"** before saving (listing, calendar price, bulk "Set a price"). Nothing is refused.
>
> *Guests*
> - **An automatic message can't run on a deleted template:** the template can't be deleted while any automatic message uses it, and a message can't be turned on with a deleted one. The page says when all messages are off and links to the guest language setting.
> - **Snoozed conversations are hidden from every working list** until they wake.
> - **The booking summary names the price rule** that changed the nightly price ("Includes: Booked-early price.").
> - **Review stars meet 3:1 contrast in both themes;** the star message clears when a star is picked; the check-in thank-you screen says "Thank you! You're registered."; the door code card says "Door code" once (Taglish "Code ng pinto").
>
> *Small-host screens (alpha)*
> - **A new reservation saved from the list opens it** (APP-FLOW 7.5); the calendar stays. Edit can change "Where did they book?", which is now marked optional. A date clash is reported before the price question.
> - **With 3 or fewer units, the Rent page keeps Month and Property under More filters.** Each supply row shows Add stock and Record use, with the rest (Count, History, Edit, Archive) under "More".
> - **The Calendar is called "Calendar";** the Launchpad uses the form's words and the Add listing form asks for the name, type, rate and cleaning fee before the building.
> - **Daily close:** a year outside 2000 to 2100 is "That date isn't valid."; a manager no longer sees Reopen or Update on a close only an owner can change.
> - **Housekeeping:** an owner's own clean reads "Done by you, no pay" on the task page too (*completes D-87*); "1 pc"; "Copy for cleaner" is not offered on a finished or cancelled clean.
> - **Reports:** switching the period clears the old one from the link; a date link without a period is read as custom dates; Export is off until both dates are set; By month shows every column; the By property total shows occupied units. Archived add-ons are listed only on the Archived tab.
>
> *Screens and keyboard*
> - **Focus goes to the new page's heading after any navigation,** a page reloading in place stays on screen until the new one is ready, a button disabled during its own request gets focus back, and a refused form focuses the field that needs fixing (the stars, the blank guest row). *Completes D-86 and D-87's focus rule for navigation; a view's own redraw after a dialog save still loses focus (round 13 top priority).*  *Replaced 2026-10-15 by D-89's single focus guard.*
> - **Every page-level error has a heading;** the void reason box is labelled.
>
> *Access*
> - **Staff no longer receive the building admin email or gate-pass settings** (section 6: gate passes, staff None). *Settles BLOCKERS round 4 "Staff and property money details" for these fields.* **Staff see a listing's cancellation policy and deposit %** (the section 6 rows now agree).
>
> *Calls made by the orchestrator this round (2026-10-15):*
> - **The owner portal is English throughout for now.** A mixed Taglish heading over an English body (seen in the re-verify) is worse than plain English. Founder question open: should the owner portal be Taglish at all? (BLOCKERS.)
> - **The host-only daily-close line stays English.** The line that explains who can change a close is on a host app screen, and the host app is English (see "Not changed" below and the host app language question in BLOCKERS). The follow-up lane correctly left it in English.
> - **A nightly rate above ₱50,000.00 asks "Save anyway?"; bulk percent changes are not questioned yet.** A typed rate is one clear number to check. A "Change prices by %" can push many nights over the limit at once, and the right question there is not clear; the founder is asked (BLOCKERS). *Default: no question on bulk percent changes.*
> - **Automatic messages list in a fixed order:** created time, then trigger order, then name, then id. Before this, messages created at the same instant came back in a random order.
> - **A button that looks disabled but must stay reachable uses `aria-disabled="true"`, drawn dimmed with the not-allowed cursor** (`.btn[aria-disabled]`). Real `:disabled` buttons keep their own look, which also shows progress. *Not while the control waits for its own request: aria-disabled (D-97, D-120).* *Why:* a keyboard or screen reader user can still land on the button and read the hint beside it (for example Export on Reports before both dates are set).
> - **The supplies button stays labelled "Count"** (inside the "More" list with History, Edit and Archive). Renaming it was considered and not done: the label is already in use across the supplies screens and the sweep found nothing wrong with it.
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the host app stays English (founder question), apart from the verify screens, which follow the chosen language; the job sheet, Record use and Count keep their own quantity messages because their rules differ; the 101x32 theme buttons pass the 24px minimum (*superseded 2026-10-15 by D-95: theme buttons are 44px on phones*); rates saved above the round 11 ceiling still price cleans (round 11 question); the staff quote route has no role guard (pre-existing; the clash text shows staff nothing they can't already see).
>
> *Why:* a small host must see true money on every list and card, must not save a wrong method or rate with one tap, and must be able to use every page with a keyboard.

> **D-89 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 13 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirteenth QA sweep (8 lanes, 77 entries including carried items and the founder's own report) was fixed in 13 code lanes (F01 to F13), plus follow-up lanes for old tests and for gaps the re-verify found. The calls below follow D-85. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 13". No severe bug this round. Suite result at the merge: 2,430/2,430. Security review: PASS, with info notes (the template check in `inbox.js` line 563, fixed in a follow-up; a stale current page on the phone More button, fixed in a follow-up; the season double-submit race, carried). **Not a clean round:** the sweep filed 55 findings and the re-verify found more small ones (below), all carried to round 14. Clean-round streak: 0/3.
>
> *Founder report: the sidebar jumped to the top on every click.* Cause: every page change rebuilt the whole menu (sidebar, top bar, bottom bar), which resets the sidebar's own scroll; the page was then scrolled a second time and the heading was focused in a way that scrolled again. Fix: **the menu is kept across page changes** and only the highlighted link moves; the sidebar keeps its scroll position and the More tools fold keeps its state; a new page scrolls to the top once, when the click happens; the page heading takes focus **without scrolling** (`preventScroll`). The phone More sheet folds More tools the same way (closed on Home, open when the current page is in it). The re-verify held at 1280x900 and 1366x768: the sidebar never rebuilt, its scroll never moved, the page started at the top and never moved again, and the heading had focus. *Founder report 2026-10-15; answered in BLOCKERS.*
>
> *One focus rule, kept by one piece of code.* After any save, confirm or redraw, focus goes back to the control you used, the control that took its place, or the page heading (without scrolling the page). **A single focus guard in `ui.js` replaces the three older watchers** (the click watcher, the dialog-close handler and the tab watcher); they were removed and a scan test fails if any other file manages redraw focus. The guard waits while a dialog is open and acts when it closes; it never moves focus after you chose something else, and never after a page change (the router focuses the new page's heading). *Why seven rounds kept finding the same bug:* each old watcher gave up before the screen was redrawn, or tried while the page under a dialog could not take focus. The guard reacts to the screen change itself. Views add only a `data-focus-key` on controls whose label changes when used (the keys are listed in DESIGN-BRIEF section 22). *Completes D-85 to D-88's focus rule.* Two gaps the re-verify found were fixed in follow-ups: the guard now also searches the whole app (not only the main area) for a control recorded outside it, such as the guest-page language toggle in the header; and Daily close has separate keys, `dc-close` (Close the day, Update close) and `dc-reopen` (Reopen day), so focus no longer lands on Reopen day after Close the day. The first owner-portal invite got its own key, `owner-invite-<id>`.
>
> *Screens and keyboard*
> - **"Skip to main content"** is the first Tab stop on every app page and moves focus to the page heading.
> - **On phones (under 960px), every button (including small ones), tab, filter chip, back link, standalone link and checklist row is at least 44px tall;** the top bar brand and account link are too, and the assistant button is 44x44. **Calendar booking tiles are at least 24px** (a dense grid; the WCAG minimum). **Checkboxes in the cleaner pay rows are 24px** on phones. Small buttons stay 38px on wider screens, links 24px. Theme buttons unchanged (D-88). *Superseded 2026-10-15 by D-95: theme buttons are 44px on phones.* *Settles the DESIGN-BRIEF `.btn--sm` 38px "only in dense toolbars" conflict: the 44px phone rule wins.*
> - **A form error shows its sentence once,** at the field; the box at the top says "Please check the highlighted fields."
> - **Confirm dialogs start on Cancel** (the safe choice). A dialog with a reason box still starts in the box.
>
> *Money: questions before saving*
> - **"Save anyway?" above ₱50,000** now also covers a season's fixed nightly price, an add-on's price (above ₱50,000) and a booking total above ₱50,000 a night ("This is ₱60,000.00 a night (₱60,000.00 for 1 night). Save anyway?"; ₱60,000 for 2 nights does not ask). Nothing is refused. Bulk percent changes still don't ask (round 12 question).
> - **Duplicates.** An identical season (same listing, name ignoring capitals and spaces, dates and price settings) is refused with "You already saved this season." A unit owner email that another active owner already uses asks "This email already belongs to <name>. Save anyway?" and saves only on yes. Archived owners and the owner's own row never count. Founder question.
> - **Checks before questions:** a deposit deduction above the deposit, and a cleaner rate above the ceiling, are refused at the field before any "return early" or "Change this rate?" question.
> - **Wording:** "Voided only: 3 entries, not counted in totals."; "Fix the dates to export." for an inverted range; "Whole-property expenses" for expenses with no unit on the profit and loss screen and in both CSVs (the Reports screen row still adds "(dues etc.)", carried); a cleared P&L month refills with this month and says "Showing this month."; the season preview names the winner last; a Fixed price with no rate says so; "so 1 pc was recorded"; a cleaner rate added again with different capitals keeps the saved spelling.
>
> *Guests*
> - **A template you renamed keeps its name** when you switch guest language (the wording still follows the language when you never edited it).
> - **An automatic message whose template was deleted can't be turned on from the list** (the button is dimmed with "Choose a template first."), and the template row is locked while either side changes. The dimmed state is not reachable today because a template in use can't be deleted; the guard is the safety net.
> - **Gate pass:** one guest is shown without a number; the Set up buttons are named "Set up, <listing>". The review form sits in its own card with room around it. The verify screen's toast is English when you go on to the host app.
>
> *Small-host screens (alpha)*
> - Assigned to shows only when there is someone to assign to; the inbox email alert no longer names server files.
> - Calendar on 3 or fewer listings: the filters and the colour legend fold away.
> - A new owner Home with no reservations leads with "Connect your calendar"; the charts fold under "Trends and revenue". (Managers and staff with no reservations don't get the card yet; carried.)
> - Add listing: a cleaning fee hint ("e.g. 500"; a card with none says "No cleaning fee"), a "Building: <name> (saved)" line after a partial save, one plain line for what a guest pays now and later (when the guest chooses: "A guest pays 50% or the full amount when booking, and any balance 3 days before check-in."), the calendar link label ending "(ends in .ics)" with a hint, a shorter description example. Settings: how to get a PayMongo account. Setup: what "Both" adds.
>
> *Decision conflicts settled:* APP-FLOW 5.2 and 5.8 against D-84 (D-84 wins: the saved answers fill the setup form and nothing is created until Create workspace; docs changed); DESIGN-BRIEF `.btn--sm` 38px against the 44px phone rule (44px on phones); D-88's theme buttons (stand); D-86 and D-87's "views add no focus code of their own" (stands, now enforced by a scan test).
>
> *Starter plan default unchanged.* Starter still covers 2 listings (D-03); raised again in round 13 (#37) and left open in BLOCKERS.
>
> *Not changed (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the gate pass contact is the owner's account email (founder question); publish, send and record payout stay three steps (founder question); paying online after check-out stays unavailable (founder question); bulk percent changes don't ask; the host app stays English; theme buttons (about 33px high, pass the 24px minimum); `charts.js` colours (low); managers and staff with no reservations see the usual Home.
>
> *Re-verify (10 lanes, 2026-10-15):* the sidebar, focus (reservations, money, operations, guest pages), phone sizes, roles and a 20-burst race all held after the follow-ups. Found and carried: saving on a long list page (Supplies) moves the page by about 62px because the list is replaced and Chrome keeps its own anchor (SEV3, shared fix in round 14); the deposit Return dialog says "The deduction can't be more than the deposit" twice (field and Return to guest line); the Reports screen row still says "Whole-property expenses (dues etc.)"; two small phone targets (landing page small buttons and links, the cleaner pay select-all box).
>
> *Why:* seven rounds of the same keyboard bug came from watchers that each gave up too early, and the founder's own navigation was jarring on every click; one guard and a kept menu end both.

> **D-90 · 2026-10-15 · FOUNDER DECISION: build a Guesty-style top bar.** The founder wrote: "add like guesty: on the middle part a search bar to search everything within BalayHub; on the upper right icons for bookmarks, + for add new, notifications, help for support, then the profile." (screenshots in the QA round 14 carry notes). **This overrides D-85's "Guesty features parked as reference, nothing built" for this one item only;** every other Guesty idea stays parked. Adapt to BalayHub's own brand and to small hosts; do not copy Guesty's look or names. Scheduled for QA round 14 / the next build sprint (it is a feature, not a bug fix). What is wanted:
> - **Global search** in the centre of the top bar ("Type / to search"; the `/` key focuses it from anywhere except while typing in a field). It searches only what the person may see (scoped by role on the server): reservations (guest name, confirmation code), guests, listings, properties, tenants, unit owners, quotes, maintenance requests, plus pages and settings by name and help articles. Grouped results, arrow keys and Enter, Escape closes, recent searches.
> - **Bookmarks:** star the current page and list starred pages; saved per person on the server so they follow the person across devices.
> - **"+" Add new menu:** Reservation, Listing, Property, Owner, Cleaning task, Expense, Invite teammate (and for long-term: Tenant, Rent payment), filtered by role and by workspace, each opening the existing "new" dialog.
> - **Notifications bell:** a panel with filters (Reservations, Payments, Cleaning, Messages, Owners), an unread count and mark as read, built on the existing events and activity log, respecting each person's notification settings.
> - **Help icon:** help-centre search, suggested articles for the current page, and "Contact us" opening a support ticket.
> - **Profile avatar** (initials): My account, Settings, theme, Log out.
> - **On phones:** the top bar keeps search (an icon opening full-screen search), "+" and the bell; bookmarks and help move into the profile menu or the More sheet. 44px targets and the D-89 focus rules apply. The host app stays English.
>
> *Why:* the founder asked for it directly after comparing with Guesty; one search box and one "+" menu cut the number of clicks for a small host. *Docs:* PRD feature (planned), APP-FLOW and BACKEND-SCHEMA (a bookmarks table, a search endpoint and a notifications feed) and DESIGN-BRIEF get their sections when it is built.

> **D-91 · 2026-10-15 · The top bar built (F53).** The Guesty-style top bar the founder asked for in D-90 is built. **D-90 said it overrides D-85's "Guesty features parked, nothing built" for this one item only, and that is still the whole of the override:** every other Guesty idea stays parked as reference, and the look and names are BalayHub's own.
> - **What is in it (desktop, 960px and wider):** a search box in the middle ("Type / to search"), then on the right Bookmarks, "+" Add new, the Notifications bell, Help and the profile avatar. The sidebar keeps the brand and its own account box (name, theme, Log out); the avatar menu repeats them, and the sidebar copy stays (D-85: group, never cut).
> - **Search:** pages and settings by name (found in the browser from the menu the person already has), plus records from the server: reservations (guest name or confirmation code), guests, listings, properties, tenants, unit owners, quotes, maintenance requests and help articles. Grouped, arrow keys and Enter, Escape closes, recent searches kept on that device only. It matches names and codes, never phone numbers, emails, notes or money. Active records only; cancelled reservations show marked "Cancelled".
> - **Bookmarks:** star the current page; up to 20 per person, saved on the server so they follow the person across devices. A bookmark to a page the person's role can no longer open is hidden, not deleted.
> - **Add new:** Reservation, Listing, Property, Unit owner, Cleaning task, Expense, Tenant, Rent payment, Invite a teammate, each opening the page's existing "new" dialog through a `?new=` or `?invite=1` link, filtered by role and workspace.
> - **Bell:** a panel with an unread count, mark as read, and filters (Reservations, Payments, Cleaning, Messages, Owners).
> - **Help:** help-centre search, suggested articles for the current page, and "Contact us" (a new support ticket).
> - **Profile menu:** My account, Settings, theme, Log out.
> - **Role rules:** owners and managers see everything above. Staff see all of it but the "+" menu holds only Expense (and Rent payment in a long-term or Both workspace). Cleaners get search (pages and help only, the server refuses them), bookmarks, the bell, help and the profile, with no "+" menu. Read-only and demo workspaces have no "+" menu. Long-term-only workspaces have no bell (no rent alert type exists yet). Owners, managers and staff only ever see records of their own workspace; managers and staff only those of their assigned properties.
> - **Phones (under 960px):** the bar holds the logo (no word under 560px), Ask, Search (an icon that opens full-screen search), "+", the bell and the avatar. **Bookmarks and Help move into the avatar menu.** Every control is 44px; panels open full width under the bar and never sit under the bottom bar. The host app stays English.
> - **Calls made with the build (each has a default in use; questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 14"):**
>   - The bell is fed by a new `notifications` table written at the same place that already sends phone alerts (`push.notify`), one row per person who is meant to get that alert. It is not built on the audit log: the audit log has no "who should see this" and no read state.
>   - Alert settings in My account also hide items from the bell, and so does a property filter. Rows are re-checked against role, property assignment and settings when they are read, so someone who loses access to a property stops seeing its alerts.
>   - A new alert type, "Unit owner activity" (`owner_activity`), when a unit owner blocks or unblocks dates in the owner portal. It fills the Owners filter, and owners get it on the phone too unless they turn it off.
>   - Notifications are kept 90 days.
>   - The Ask button stays at the bottom right on desktop.
>   - "Property" is in the "+" menu for long-term and Both workspaces only (a short-term host adds buildings through Add listing).
> - **Cost:** two new tables (`bookmarks`, `notifications`, migration 033) and three new endpoints (`GET /api/search`, `/api/account/bookmarks`, `/api/account/notifications`). No change to existing access rules.
>
> *Why:* the founder asked for it directly after comparing with Guesty; one search box and one "+" menu cut the number of clicks for a small host. *Docs:* PRD F53 (built), APP-FLOW 27, BACKEND-SCHEMA 2.17, 6.4 and 20, TRD 3.3 and 4, DESIGN-BRIEF 23.

> **D-92 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 14 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The fourteenth QA sweep (8 lanes, 60 entries including carried items) was fixed in the same wave as the top bar (19 code lanes). Calls follow D-85. Open questions are in BLOCKERS.MD under "Questions from QA round 14". One severe bug (the booking summary). Suite result at the merge: 2640 of 2640 passing. Security review: PASS. Round 14 re-verify result: pending re-verify.
>
> *Money and trust*
> - **The booking page shows what the guest pays now for the option they picked.** "Pay in full now" shows one "Pay now" line with the total; the deposit line shows only for "Pay a deposit". Changing the choice updates it at once. *Severe: the page showed the deposit when the guest would be charged in full. The server was right all along.*
> - **No money method is pre-chosen for a refund.** Record refund starts on "Choose…" and asks "Choose how the money was refunded." (the same as Record payment on a direct booking), so a one-tap save can no longer record cash as GCash. *Applied to cleaner pay on 2026-10-19 by D-100: Mark paid's "How you paid" also starts on "Choose…" (it had been defaulting to Cash).* *Refined 2026-10-15 by D-99 (O4): a payment on an Airbnb booking now starts on Cash; direct bookings still start empty, other channels still start on Channel payout.* A zero or negative refund is refused at the field before any question.
> - **A PayMongo key is checked with PayMongo before it is called Connected.** On save BalayHub asks PayMongo whether the key works. Accepted: "Connected". Refused: nothing is saved and the field says so. **If PayMongo can't be reached, the key is saved and reads "Saved, not checked yet".** Turning the booking page on then tries the check again; if PayMongo still can't be reached, the owner is asked "Turn on anyway?". A key PayMongo refuses can't be used to turn the page on. A guest checkout that PayMongo refuses as unauthorised marks the key unchecked again. *Default for a key that can't be checked: allow, after a clear warning (the safer alternative, refusing until PayMongo confirms, would block the host whenever PayMongo is briefly down).*
> - **Check-in needs at least one ID photo per registration,** because the page already tells guests the building requires one. A guest who already sent a photo can edit their answers without sending another. *Founder default; the alternative is softer wording with photos optional.*
> - **A unit owner email that is a teammate's login asks first:** "This email is a login for <name>. Save anyway?", the same way the round 13 duplicate-owner question works.
> - **Recording rent from a later month's row keeps the amount of the row clicked,** and the form now says in words that the money goes to the oldest unpaid month first (D-85), then to this one. Only the wording changed.
>
> *Smooth screens (founder priority)*
> - **Saving, searching, filtering or switching a tab keeps the page where you were.** One shared piece of code in `ui.js` (the scroll keeper) notes the window position when the person acts, holds the page height while the redraw runs, and undoes any scroll the redraw caused; scroll anchoring is off for the page content. Views never move the window themselves (a scan test enforces it). Only a page change scrolls to the top, once (D-89).
> - **Home draws its cards in one go,** so nothing jumps while it loads.
> - **Focus (completes D-89).** When the control you used is gone and nothing took its place, **focus goes to the page heading at once** (no pause on the page body), and moves to the control if it comes back within 10 seconds. The sweep's "page body" readings were the guard's old 800 ms pause, not a lost guard. **Matching "the same place in a group" now applies to tabs and radio buttons only,** so focus never lands on another row's Remove or Archive. New keys: automatic message Turn on/off, Pause/Resume, Skip for now/Show again. After Reopen, the Daily close page lands on the heading (accepted; carried).
>
> *Phones*
> - **Also at least 44px tall under 960px:** checkbox rows (cleaning checklists, notification settings), dialog close buttons, the guest language toggle, link-style buttons, "Show as table", small inputs (Go to date, Month), the guest Privacy link and the landing page's small buttons. The cleaner pay select-all box is 24px *(superseded 2026-10-15 by D-99 (O3): the box may stay 24px, but its label is the 44px tap target on phones)*. Unchanged: calendar booking tiles (24px). *Theme buttons: superseded 2026-10-15 by D-95, they are 44px on phones.* Plain links use a shared `link-tap` class. On desktop the page keeps room under the last row so the Ask button never covers it.
>
> *Small-host screens and wording*
> - **The Listings filter bar scales with the number of listings:** none until there is a listing; 1 to 3 listings show only search with the rest under "More filters"; 4 or more show the full bar. *Known side effect:* with 0 active listings (all archived) the Archived listings view is reachable only by link (BLOCKERS).
> - **The "Cancel lease" button names keep their visible text**, so the spoken name always contains the words on screen (WCAG 2.5.3). When a tenant has more than one lease, the lease buttons also say which lease they belong to.
> - New reservation: Channel fee only when booked through a channel; "Where did they book?" and "Other fees" under "More details". The guest limit follows the listing's own limit.
> - Inbox: the email note is one line; Property and Listing filters show only when there is more than one.
> - "Connect calendar" on Home opens the first listing's Channels dialog.
> - Reports: a short-term workspace opening the long-term Reports goes to Stay reports; "Billed" names its dates; the CSV "Generated" line reads like the screen; Stay reports CSV is disabled for a bad date range; the Listing filter follows the Property; the Reports row reads "Whole-property expenses".
> - Copy: "No resolved tickets."; cleaner pay labels follow the chosen month; seasons column "Dates"; discount codes "Until …"; the deposit cap sentence shows once; an inbox error clears when you switch tabs.
>
> *Decision conflicts settled:* the template row in APP-FLOW (managers edit templates) against D-28 (owner only): D-28 wins and the doc changed; the gate pass has no language toggle and stays English (doc changed to match the code); the check-in copy against the code that accepted no ID (founder default: require one); D-85 against D-90 (D-90 wins for the top bar only, recorded in D-91).
>
> *Intended (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* add-on descriptions show as typed in both languages (a hint now says so); "Please fill in…" for empty required fields and "Please check…" for invalid ones; calendar bars as D-88/D-89 (*theme buttons: superseded 2026-10-15 by D-95, 44px on phones*).
>
> *Carried:* identical seasons saved at the same instant (no unique index); managers and staff with no reservations get the usual Home; the server trusts the client's ID photo count (the upload is a second request).
>
> *Why:* a made-up PayMongo key showed "Connected" and let the booking page go live, the booking page showed the wrong amount, and the founder's own navigation was jarring; these are money and guest trust first (D-85), then friction.

> **D-93 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 14 re-verify calls.** Nine browser lanes re-checked round 14. Most fixes held; the new findings were fixed in follow-ups (suite 2662 of 2662; browser check in round 15). The round is not clean, so the clean-round streak stays 0 of 3. This adds to D-92 and D-91 and changes none of their calls except where it says so.
> - **The booking page's add-on line moves the rows below it, by design.** The summary has to list the add-ons the guest picked; the checkbox and the scroll position do not move. Recorded so it is not filed again.
> - **The scroll keeper holds a short page's height until the person scrolls or changes page.** The quiet timer no longer lets go (and never clamps the scroll); a search that makes the page shorter no longer leaves the window about 50px out of place. Refines D-92's scroll keeper.
> - **Bell rows with no property are owner-only.** Managers and staff see only rows for properties they are assigned to, so a person who loses a property stops seeing its alerts. Every alert call site was checked; the housekeeping assignment alert now carries its property. Refines D-91's read-time scoping. *Annotation 2026-10-15 (D-97, S3): owners always get alerts that have no property, whatever their property filter. A type the owner turned off stays off.*
> - **The theme switch sits below the profile menu list, not inside it.** Arrow keys move through the menu items; Tab reaches the theme buttons (a menu cannot hold a radio group). Refines D-91's profile menu.
> - **The `?new=` and `?invite=` openers fire once per navigation.** Once a page has opened its dialog it drops the opener from its own state and the address, so saving does not reopen it.
> - **Tab counts as the person's own move.** For 300 ms after a Tab the focus guard leaves focus alone, so the search list can close without focus being pulled back.
> - **The first listing, for "Connect your calendar" and the Launchpad calendar step, is the first active listing in Listings order;** with none, the Listings page opens.
> - **Group room buttons name the room's dates** (and "room N" when two are identical), so Remove cannot be confused with another row's.
> - **Open question (BLOCKERS):** should staff on a long-term workspace see tenant rent amounts, due day and outstanding? Default: yes, they need them to record payments.
>
> *Why:* the re-verify exists to catch what the sweep and the unit tests cannot: keyboard order, focus and scroll timing. Each finding got a test first.
