> **D-94 · 2026-10-15 · Platform plan coupons.** Founder request, for alpha and beta testers. A platform admin makes coupons for BalayHub's own plans on a **Plan coupons** page; a workspace owner types a code under **Have a coupon code?** on Plan & billing. These are not the hosts' guest discount codes (F17), which keep that name. The full contract is `docs/contracts/platform-coupons.md`. *(Amended in practice 2026-10-21 by D-104: on a plan change, free days from a coupon or a trial move across day for day and never grow; only paid days convert at the price ratio.)*
>
> *What billing really is (so the coupon is honest).* BalayHub charges one prepaid period at a time. It never keeps a card and never charges anyone automatically *(superseded in part 2026-10-15 by D-98: BalayHub keeps a card reference at PayMongo, and charges automatically only in Stage 2)*; renewal is manual and the usual 7 grace days then read-only rules still apply. So a coupon covers a number of **billed months**, not calendar months: while months remain, every checkout the owner starts is discounted automatically (no code typed again); when they run out, checkouts are full price and the box says so.
>
> *Behaviour and the founder defaults (each is built in; nothing waits on an answer, see BLOCKERS "Questions from the founder requests, round 15").*
> - **Three kinds:** percent (1 to 99% off), fixed (a peso amount off each covered month) and free (an explicit choice). **Length:** the first billed month only, the first N billed months (2 to 36), or every billed month. A 3-month coupon on a 12-month purchase covers 3 of the 12. Fixed means per covered month.
> - **The ₱20 floor is one constant.** `MIN_CHARGE_C` (2000 centavos) in `src/lib/coupons.js`. If a discount would leave a total above zero but under ₱20, the total is raised to ₱20. *Minimum ₱20 is BalayHub's own floor. PayMongo's documented minimum is ₱1 for cards, GCash and Maya (research 2026-10-15, medium-high confidence; BDO and BillEase ₱100), so lowering the floor means changing that one constant.*
> - **"Require card" means a one-time ₱20 card payment.** BalayHub cannot keep a card or charge it later *(superseded in part 2026-10-15 by D-98: it now keeps a PayMongo card reference; the ₱20 coupon rule itself is unchanged)*, so a free coupon that requires a card asks for ₱20 once, card only (the PayMongo checkout is opened with `payment_method_types: ['card']`), and then gives the free months. Nothing is charged automatically afterwards. If card payments are off in `PAYMONGO_METHODS` the code answers `CARD_OFF`.
> - **Free with no card needs no payment.** A free coupon without "require card" makes no PayMongo call: a `billing_payments` row with `provider = 'coupon'` and amount 0 is written, and the plan switches on at once ("Activate free plan"). During a trial the paid period starts when the trial ends; a coupon never lengthens the trial.
> - **One coupon live per workspace.** A workspace can use one coupon at a time (a different code answers `COUPON_OTHER`). "Once per workspace" is on by default for every new coupon, so a used-up code cannot be used again by the same workspace.
> - **The 45-day rule (`COUPON_AHEAD`).** A discounted checkout is refused with a 409 while the workspace is already paid more than 45 days ahead: "Your plan is already paid ahead. Your coupon applies at your next renewal." The preview uses the same check, so the two cannot disagree. *Why:* the security review (Medium) found that coupon checkouts could stack paid time far into the future, and a later plan change converts that time into another plan at the old price.
> - **A used coupon is locked.** Once any non-released redemption exists, the code, kind, amount, length, plans and start cannot change (409 `COUPON_LOCKED`); name, note, end date, limit (never below the times used), once per workspace, require card and on/off can. There is no delete; switch it off. Ending or switching off a coupon stops **new** workspaces only; workspaces already using it keep their remaining months.
> - **Demo workspaces get 403 `DEMO_READ_ONLY`.** The read-only guard refuses the write before any coupon code runs (the coupon library also refuses the demo, as a second line).
> - **Failed code tries are limited:** 10 per workspace and 20 per IP, per 15 minutes (only failed checks count; *the IP cap was raised to 60 on 2026-10-15 by D-96*). *The lock also blocks valid codes for the rest of the window* (accepted; BLOCKERS asks the founder). Unknown, off, not started, ended and full codes all answer the same generic 400 `COUPON_INVALID`, so codes cannot be probed. Codes are not case-sensitive (trimmed, inner spaces removed, upper-cased; letters, digits and dashes, 3 to 32).
> - **Preview order:** the preview checks that online billing is on (`BILLING_OFF`) before anything else, then the code, then the 45-day rule.
> - **Other defaults:** a coupon box shows only while online payment is on; codes are entered on Plan & billing, not at sign-up *(annotated 2026-10-15 by D-98: sign-up is now followed by the Confirm your payment card step; codes are still entered on Plan & billing, and a coupon never discounts the ₱20 card check)*; deleting a workspace deletes its coupon use, which frees a slot under the limit; the coupon box offers no way to stop a coupon for workspaces already using it.
> - **True monthly auto-renew is not built.** *(Built but switched off 2026-10-15 by D-98, Stage 2: `PAYMONGO_SUBSCRIPTIONS=on` after PayMongo approves Subscriptions.)* It needs PayMongo's Subscriptions feature, which is gated (an account review and approval; cards and Maya; plain saved cards work only while the customer is present). Until then the prepaid checkout stays, and the coupon discounts each renewal automatically. Whether to ask PayMongo to turn Subscriptions on is a BLOCKERS question; if yes it becomes its own project (saved card, a monthly charge job, retries and emails).
> - **Audit:** `platform.coupon_created`, `platform.coupon_updated` (field names only), `platform.coupon_switched_off` and `_on`, and `billing.coupon_applied` (written when the payment is marked paid). Migration 035 holds the schema (BACKEND-SCHEMA 2.18).
>
> *Why:* the founder needs to give alpha and beta testers free or cheaper access without editing each workspace by hand, in a way that fits how BalayHub really bills. The money is stored as whole centavos and the discount is applied by lowering the single line item sent to PayMongo, because PayMongo has no coupon feature of its own.

> **D-95 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 15 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The fifteenth QA sweep (8 browser lanes, 67 entries including carried items and lane notes, plus the founder's three requests) was fixed the same day in 26 code lanes. Suite 2860 of 2860 at the merge. Security review PASS (manual); its one Medium (coupon stacking) is fixed as the 45-day rule in D-94 and the Lows are fixed too; the browser re-verify is pending, so the clean-round streak stays 0 of 3. This adds to D-94, D-93 and D-92 and changes only what it says.
>
> *Phones and the 44px rule*
> - **The theme buttons are 44px tall on phones (under 960px).** *Supersedes the D-88 exemption.* The same 44px now covers the guest footer links, the landing footer links, review stars (44x44 with a gap), the housekeeping calendar unit link, the filter check pill, and small buttons (now 44px wide as well as tall).
> - **Links inside a sentence are exempt from the 44px rule** (the accessibility standard's inline exception): the consent "privacy notice", "Ask BalayHub Support" inside a sentence. Standalone links get 44px. Calendar booking tiles and cleaner-pay row boxes stay 24px. *Superseded 2026-10-15 by D-99 (O3) for the cleaner-pay boxes: the box may stay 24px, but its label is the 44px tap target on phones. Calendar tiles are unchanged.*
> - **The theme switch is one radio group:** one Tab stop, the arrow keys (and Home and End) move and select, and its buttons are updated in place, never rebuilt, so focus stays on them.
> - **Bottom-bar labels read "Groups" and "Help"** on the phone bottom bar (the sidebar keeps the full names, "Group bookings" and "Help & support").
> - **The account page title (h1) reads "My account"**; the person's name is a smaller heading inside the page.
> - **The Reservations Channel column folds under the listing name between 960 and 1439px** (`col-md-hide` and `col-md-show`); wider screens show the column, narrower ones use the card layout.
> - **The scroll keeper never reveals an off-screen control.** After a redraw, focus is put back without scrolling the control into view if it was not visible before. ~~Error toasts are announced at once (role alert); other toasts stay polite.~~ *Superseded by D-109: toasts carry no role of their own, and the toast area is the one live region, so error toasts are read politely with the others.*
>
> *Check-in, calendar links, add-ons, payouts, alerts, supplies*
> - **A check-in stays a draft until an ID photo is stored.** Saving the answers without a stored photo keeps them as a draft: the guest page says "Your answers are saved. Add a photo of your ID to finish registering." and the host card says "Waiting for the guest's ID photo". The first stored photo finishes it and sends the host the usual alert and inbox note. The gate pass, the Reservations "check-in form" filter and the Launchpad step count only finished registrations. Rows registered before today stay finished. (Migration 036 makes `submitted_at` nullable; NULL means draft.) *Why:* the server used to trust the guest's own count of photos.
> - **A calendar link that does not look like a calendar gets a "Save it anyway?" question** (409 `ICAL_NOT_CALENDAR`, resent with `confirm_unusual_link`). A link with `.ics` or `ical` in its path or query passes without a question. Refusing outright could block an unusual channel's real link.
> - **An add-on quantity is never silently changed.** Over the limit, the typed number stays, the field says "Up to N <name> per stay." and Save is blocked.
> - **The payout Method starts on "Choose…"** so a method is always a deliberate pick.
> - **Alerts with no property go to owners only** (bell and phone); a future caller can no longer fan an alert out to every manager.
> - **Cleaners see only the supplies on their own job, through a new route.** `GET /api/cleaning-tasks/:id/supplies` answers the job's property supplies (names, units, on hand); `GET /api/supplies` now refuses cleaners and the cleaner Supplies page is blocked. *Settles the conflict between the founder default ("refuse cleaners") and the job sheet's "Supplies used" picker, which needs a list.*
>
> *Filters, cards and search*
> - **"Filters" replaces "More filters":** a funnel icon, the word, and a count of every active filter except the search box; set quick selects and chips look set; Clear all shows with a single chip; every select has a chevron. Small hosts get search plus Filters instead of a bare "Filter" fold (Calendar included).
> - **A card with one message and an action uses `.cta-card`:** icon, text, then the button, with a 12px gap between the sentence and the button (a defensive rule covers deposits, cleaner pay and the booking result). The Home first-run card is the first user.
> - **Searches name what they show:** a capped group says "5 of 8 shown"; a guest result names the latest stay; dates read "Oct 16, 2026 – Oct 17, 2026". The Launchpad **Skip for now** and **Show again** keep the step open and the page where it was; Home groups several calendar links of one listing ("(2 links)").
>
> *Decision conflicts settled:* theme buttons (D-88 exempt against D-89 and D-93 44px: 44px wins); cleaner supplies (BACKEND-SCHEMA 6.1 against the founder default: a job-only route); BACKEND-SCHEMA section 6 said three roles (the code has four, the doc changed); APP-FLOW 27.1 and the code agree that Property in "+ Add new" is for long-term and Both only; bulk "Set a price" asks on Apply (intended, D-88).
>
> *Intended (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the floating Ask button may pass over content while the page scrolls (D-92's bottom space keeps the last row clear); the first page load leaves focus at the start of the page so "Skip to main content" is the first Tab stop, only a change of page focuses the heading (D-89); the consent link inline in a sentence and the other inline links; a checkbox as D-89; calendar tiles and cleaner-pay row boxes at 24px; the bulk price preview only previews.
>
> *Why:* the money and access findings first (the check-in count, cleaner supplies, coupon stacking), then the rule that kept returning (tap sizes), with the founder's coupon, filter and card requests built in the same wave.

> **D-96 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 15 late calls (orchestrator).** Two calls made after D-95 was written. They change only what they say.
> - **The coupon code limit per IP address is 60 per 15 minutes (was 20).** Owners in one office, or on one mobile-carrier address, share an IP address and could lock each other out of applying a code. The limit per workspace stays at 10 failed tries per 15 minutes and is the main guard against guessing. Refines D-94.
> - **A filter bar with no extra ("more") filters shows a small, non-interactive funnel and a "Filter by" label instead of an empty Filters button.** A button that opens nothing would mislead. Search-only bars are unchanged. *Why:* founder request 3, "filters must look like filters". Refines D-95's Filters button.
> - **Money display in the owner coupon box:** whole-peso amounts in the Total and the "saved" lines show two decimals (for example ₱499.00), matching every other peso amount in the app (D-85). Offers ("50% off your first 3 months") and the "Pay ₱20 by card" button label stay short.

> **D-97 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 16 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The sixteenth QA sweep (8 browser lanes, 41 filed findings plus carried items) was fixed the same day in 20 code lanes and a docs lane. No severe bug: 37 real findings (9 significant, 28 minor); 4 were not bugs. The merge gate, the security pass (PASS) and the browser re-verify are tracked in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN, so the clean-round streak is unchanged. This adds to D-96, D-95 and D-94 and changes only what it says.
>
> *Orchestrator calls (O1 to O10):*
> - **O1 Do not rebook follows the email.** Any reservation (an owner save or an online direct booking) whose guest email matches a guest flagged Do not rebook in the same workspace gets the warning (owner toast; Inbox note and push for online bookings), even when a separate guest record is made. The merge rule for guest records (same email and first name) is unchanged, and the flag stays on the original guest record. The flag is workspace-wide by email on purpose: a manager limited to some properties may see the flag on a guest because another guest record with the same email was flagged. Only the yes or no reaches them.
> - **O2 Back restores the list's scroll.** Going back to a page (browser Back, or the page's own `‹` link to the page you came from) returns to where you left it. A new page still starts at the top. Back waits up to 1.5 seconds for the list to load, then restores the place; any scroll, tap or key press cancels it.
> - **O3 Wordmark contrast** stays the logo exemption (BLOCKERS). The landing hero calendar's Airbnb bar darkens to `#c8344a` (5.19:1 with white text).
> - **O4 Top bar search** gets one muted line, "Search covers guests, reservations, listings and pages."; search is not widened. *Widened 2026-10-15 by D-99 (O1).*
> - **O5 Launchpad** shows the current group open and folds the later groups under "Later (N more steps)"; the count stays the full count.
> - **O6 Phone calendar:** Property, Tag and Availability sit behind Filters below 960px.
> - **O7 Comped workspaces** see no Pay now and no coupon box, only "Your workspace is on a complimentary plan, so there is nothing to pay."
> - **O8 Check-in headcount:** the host sees registered against booked on the reservation's check-in card and on the gate pass; the guest is told when they register more than booked; nothing is blocked (the listing's maximum stays the hard limit).
> - **O9 Owner fee and duplicate names:** "Save anyway?" for a percentage fee over 50% and for an exact duplicate owner name; 0% saves without a question. On Edit, the fee question is asked only when the fee model or the value changed.
> - **O10 Free-with-card coupon wording** states the ₱20.00 card charge in the preview message, the banner and the hint.
>
> *Strategist calls (S1 to S6):*
> - **S1 Quiet redirect.** Opening the other module's home (`#/dashboard` on a stays workspace, `#/home` on a rentals workspace) goes home with no message. Any other refused page still says "That page isn't available for your role or workspace." The Privacy page logo now goes to the workspace's own home.
> - **S2 The Launchpad fold applies to every owner,** not only owners with 1 to 3 listings. The onboarding answer has no listing count and adding one is a server change for no gain; a bigger host still sees every step one click away. (BLOCKERS asks.)
> - **S3 Account-wide alerts reach every owner,** whatever their property filter. A type the owner turned off stays off. Annotates D-93's "Bell rows with no property are owner-only".
> - **S4 Photo picks upload at once** on the cleaning task and on the repair request (one pattern for both pages; the deposits page still has the two-step way, carried).
> - **S5 Inbox composer:** each mode keeps its own text. Switching to Internal note puts the guest text aside; switching back brings it back.
> - **S6 Coupon code nudge:** a non-blocking hint under the code box for codes of 3 to 7 characters; a free coupon with no total limit and once per workspace off asks "Unlimited free months?" before saving.
>
> *Not bugs (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the phone "jumps" when opening Filters on Reservations or a dialog from a scrolled page (#37, #38) were the test tool scrolling an off-screen button into view; a person cannot tap a button that is off screen or under the top bar, and clicking the same buttons without that scroll left the page where it was. Calendar tiles keep their 24px size (D-95, #40). The wordmark stays exempt from the contrast rule (#17, O3).
>
> *Focus rules learned:* (1) a Tab followed by an action inside 300 ms no longer loses focus: once focus lands on a control after a Tab, the Tab window ends; (2) a form's Save button is marked busy (`aria-disabled="true"`) rather than disabled while saving, so focus stays on it, and the busy flag is the double-submit guard; (3) a dialog with no fields starts on its first action button. The re-verify must drive these with a real Tab then Enter inside 300 ms, not by script focus.
>
> *Server rules added:* a PayMongo 401 on the balance-pay refresh clears the host's key check only when the 401 came from the key currently saved (a stale checkout cannot clear a newer key's check). Owner names: two active owners with the same name (trimmed, case-insensitive) ask first (409 `NAME_IN_USE`). *Closed 2026-10-19 by D-100 (K6): a duplicate owner name is allowed after "Save anyway?", so no database unique index is added.* A discount code with both a percent and an amount is refused: "Choose one: a percent off or an amount off, not both."
>
> *Why:* a guest or host should never be told something false (a false red error, a flag that did not follow the email, a "free" offer that hid its charge), the focus class that kept returning since round 13 got its two root causes, and small hosts get less on screen at once (Launchpad, phone calendar).


---

> **D-98 · 2026-10-15 · FOUNDER DECISION: card on file before the free trial.** New hosts must save a payment card with PayMongo before their 14-day free trial starts, so they are serious and so the plan can be charged to that card when the trial ends. Built in two stages behind settings. Adds to D-94 and changes only what it says. The full contract is `docs/contracts/card-on-file.md`.
>
> *Decisions (each is built in; nothing waits on an answer, see BLOCKERS "Card on file and automatic payment").*
> - **Where:** a new step, **Confirm your payment card** (`#/confirm-card`), after sign-up, email verification and workspace setup, before anything else in the app works. The landing Get started sheet and the sign-up form are unchanged apart from one honest line. *Why:* sign-up stays short; the card step needs a workspace (it charges the workspace) and a verified email (stops card-testing bots).
> - **Card only.** The payment uses `payment_method_allowed: ['card']`. GCash, Maya wallet and GrabPay are not offered, whatever `PAYMONGO_METHODS` says. Saving a payment method for later charges works for cards only.
> - **Amount: ₱20, once, not refunded and not credited toward a plan.** It shows in Plan & billing history as "Card check". A real payment is required to save a card (PayMongo has no ₱0 check). ₱20 is the existing `MIN_CHARGE_C` floor (D-94). Crediting it would touch coupon maths, the minimum charge and Stage 2 invoices. The host is told before paying.
> - **The trial clock starts when the card check succeeds.** Workspace creation leaves `trial_ends_at` empty; success sets it to now plus `TRIAL_DAYS` (14).
> - **Before the check** the host sees only Confirm your card, Help, Account and Log out. Every other page sends them back. On the server, every write except auth, account, support, the card check routes, accepting invitations and workspace deletion answers 402 `CARD_CHECK_REQUIRED` ("Confirm your payment card to start your free trial."). *As built:* a support ticket screenshot upload (`POST /files` with `record_type=support`) is also allowed while gated, so Help works fully.
> - **Who is exempt (`card_check` stays empty):** every workspace that existed before migration 037; workspaces created while the check is off (online billing off, no public key, or `CARD_CHECK=off`), whose trial starts at creation as before; the demo; workspaces created by a platform admin; any workspace set to Complimentary (the gate applies only while `plan = 'trial'`). **Not exempt:** long-term-only hosts. **Team members** never do the check; until the owner does, they see "Waiting for your workspace owner".
> - **Platform-admin override: yes.** "Skip card check" on the admin workspace panel sets `card_check = 'waived'` and starts the trial now. Audited (`platform.card_check_waived`). The founder invites alpha owners by hand and must not make them pay ₱20.
> - **What the host chooses:** the plan they want after the trial (Starter, Growth or Pro, default Growth), stored as `renewal_plan`, changeable in Plan & billing. Automatic payment is always 1 month; 3 or 12 months can still be bought by hand.
> - **Stage 1 (on now): no automatic charge.** Reminder emails 3 days and 1 day before trial end. In Plan & billing, **Pay ₱999 with Visa ending 4242** charges the saved card through a new payment with the host present (one tap; the bank may ask for 3-D Secure). If that fails, **Pay another way** opens the existing hosted checkout. The copy says plainly that nothing is charged automatically. *Why:* PayMongo saved cards work on-session only; saying "we will charge you" in Stage 1 would be false.
> - **Stage 2 (built, off): `PAYMONGO_SUBSCRIPTIONS=on`.** At trial end (and at each later `paid_until` for owners who turned automatic payment on) BalayHub creates a PayMongo Subscription on the saved card for `renewal_plan` and PayMongo charges monthly. Subscription webhooks keep `paid_until` in step. "Cancel automatic payment" cancels it. The 7 grace days then read-only rule still applies when a charge fails. Only the gated Subscriptions API may charge off-session.
> - **Consent for automatic payment is explicit.** In Stage 2 the card step has a required tick box; its time is stored in `auto_renew_consented_at`. Workspaces that saved a card under Stage 1 copy are not silently enrolled when Stage 2 switches on; they see a "Turn on automatic payment" offer.
> - **Coupons still apply.** The one-tap pay and the hosted checkout use the same coupon rules (`coupons.resolve`, the 45-day rule); a free coupon period activates with no charge. A coupon never discounts the ₱20 card check. Stage 2: free coupon months are activated by the job and the subscription starts after them; a limited-months coupon delays the subscription until its months are used; a forever coupon puts the subscription on a PayMongo plan at the discounted price.
> - **No double charge.** One pending card check per workspace (unique partial index). Starting again reuses it. Applying a success locks the workspace row and does nothing if the check already passed. Webhooks and refreshes never trust the event body: they re-read the payment with the secret key and check the amount is 2000 centavos and the metadata names this workspace. Stage 2 invoices are unique on `invoice_id`.
> - **Honest landing copy.** "No credit card needed." becomes "₱20 card check, then 14 days free." (Taglish: "₱20 card check, tapos 14 araw na libre.").
> - **Stale checks.** A pending card check older than 24 hours is marked expired by the reconcile job after one last look. The workspace stays gated; the owner can start again. Never-checked workspaces are not deleted (the admin list can filter them as "Waiting for card").
> - **Removing the card.** Plan & billing has "Remove card": BalayHub forgets the ids (and tries to detach at PayMongo), and cancels automatic payment. The workspace keeps its trial or plan.
> - **Kill switches.** `CARD_CHECK=off` stops new workspaces needing the check (workspaces already waiting keep the gate until waived). `PAYMONGO_SUBSCRIPTIONS` defaults off. Both are settings in `deploy/.env`, no code change.
> - **Security and PCI.** The card number goes from the browser straight to PayMongo with the public key; BalayHub's server never receives, logs or stores it, only PayMongo ids, brand and last 4. Because the card fields sit on BalayHub's own page, the PCI scope is SAQ A-EP rather than SAQ A, until PayMongo confirms a hosted page can save cards (research R1). The CSP gains `connect-src https://api.paymongo.com` and nothing else.
> - **Audit:** `billing.card_check_passed`, `billing.card_removed`, `billing.renewal_changed`, `billing.trial_reminder_sent`, `platform.card_check_waived`.
>
> *Not changed:* the ₱20 floor constant (D-94); the 7 grace days; the read-only rules for ended trials and overdue plans; how a coupon covers billed months; the hosted checkout for 3 and 12 months.
>
> *Built differently from the plan (recorded so the docs match the code):* a declined card on attach answers HTTP 402 with `status: "failed"` (so the failed-only limiters count it); the Plan & billing buttons (Remove card, Change, automatic payment) are `btn--secondary btn--sm`, not link-style buttons, so they meet the 44px phone rule; the admin status filter value is `card_pending`; the Stage 2 "Next charge" amount ignores a coupon discount (carried to round 18).
>
> *Why:* the founder wants serious sign-ups and a card ready to charge when the trial ends, and does not want to charge anyone without saying so. The research questions that could change the build (R1 to R6) have defaults coded and are listed for PayMongo in BLOCKERS.

---

> **D-99 · 2026-10-15 · QA round 17 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The seventeenth QA sweep (8 browser lanes, 38 distinct findings plus 12 carried items) was fixed the same day in 21 code lanes and a docs lane. No severe bug: 33 real findings (5 significant, 28 minor), 5 not bugs. #26 and #28 and four carried items went to the card-on-file work (D-98) and were taken there (below). The merge gate, the security pass and the browser re-verify are tracked in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN. This adds to D-98, D-97 and D-95 and changes only what it says.
>
> *Orchestrator calls (O1 to O11):*
> - **O1 Top bar search covers what D-90 asked for.** Besides reservations, guests, listings, properties, tenants, unit owners, quotes and repair requests, it now finds cleaning tasks, supplies, expenses, discount codes and add-ons. Each group is scoped by role exactly as its page is (staff get no Discount codes or Add-ons; long-term owners get no Supplies); results never show money, contact details or notes; groups stay 5 per group. The scope line names what the person can search. The "Maintenance" group is renamed "Repair requests". An expense result's title is the vendor, then the category, never the free-text description (staff could see money typed there). *Widens D-97 O4.*
> - **O2 The wordmark is a logotype.** "Hub" keeps `--sage`; WCAG 1.4.3 exempts logotypes. Written into DESIGN-BRIEF; sweep lanes stop filing it.
> - **O3 Cleaner pay tick boxes:** on phones the row's label (and the "Select all unpaid" label) is the 44px tap target; the box itself may stay 24px. *Supersedes the 24px cleaner-pay exemption in D-92 and D-95.*
> - **O4 Payment method on Airbnb bookings defaults to Cash;** Channel payout stays a choice. *Refines the round 14 rule "never pre-select a money method" (D-92): Airbnb now starts on Cash instead of Channel payout; direct bookings still start empty; other channels still start on Channel payout.*
> - **O5 Reason fields** (void, refund, cancel, remove room, reject a proof, void a charge, payout or withdrawal) take up to 500 characters, checked in the form and on the server with "Reason must be at most 500 characters." No `maxlength` cut on those boxes. Migration 038 (`038_refund_reason_500.sql`) widens the one database limit that was lower: `booking_refunds.reason` was CHECK-limited to 300 and is now 500. The other void reasons are plain text columns with no length CHECK. The block reason stays at 200 (S3).
> - **O6 Long-term Reports has no two-year limit.** APP-FLOW 29.11 is corrected: the limit is for stay reports only.
> - **O7 Gate pass headcount:** when a registration exists the pass shows the registered count, with the booked count when they differ ("1 (booked for 2)"), on the page and in the email, matching the host's check-in card.
> - **O8 Group bookings warn on a do-not-rebook email,** like single reservations (owner or manager toast after save).
> - **O9 "Save anyway?" above a ceiling** for expenses (above ₱50,000, the add-on ceiling) and for fixed-amount plan coupons (above the highest plan price, today Pro ₱1,799.00).
> - **O10 The mention list never covers the composer's mode tabs;** a click on a tab never inserts a mention.
> - **O11 Small-host listings:** with 3 or fewer listings the bar shows Occupancy and Sort and folds the rest under Filters; listing photos upload when picked; the Home first-run card stops asking to connect once a calendar is connected; one failing calendar link is reported in the Channels dialog, the listing card, the bell and the Home banner, with no toast on top.
>
> *Strategist calls (S1 to S7):*
> - **S1 A typed name joins an existing guest (#5).** The merge is the existing rule (same email and same first name). The save now says so: "Saved under the existing guest {name} (same email and first name)." The typed name is not stored. BLOCKERS asks whether to keep the typed name instead.
> - **S2 Minimal sync-problem set:** Channels dialog line, listing card warning, bell row, Home banner (kept because Home has no listing cards). Dropped: the "Connected, but the first sync failed" toast.
> - **S3 Deposit deduction reason stays at 1,000 characters** (guest-facing explanation); block reasons stay at 200 (calendar labels). O5 covers audit reasons only.
> - **S4 Deposit photos upload when picked** (the third page on the D-97 S4 pattern; the "Add photo" button is gone).
> - **S5 Expense ceiling ₱50,000** on add and on a correction whose amount changed.
> - **S6 The coupon ceiling is checked on the server** (409 `AMOUNT_ABOVE_PLAN`, resend with `confirm_high_amount: true`), so plan prices live in one place (`src/lib/plans.js`).
> - **S7 The `.disabled` audit is closed:** every view checked kept focus or returned it at 300 ms, 2.5 s and 8 s (Daily close Reopen day drops to BODY for about 30 ms, then the heading; watch it). No view changes. *Reopened and closed again by D-120: the audit used fast saves; with a slow save every `disabled` busy button dropped focus.*
>
> *Also decided or learned this round:*
> - **Balance pay and PayMongo 401:** a PayMongo 401 when closing an old checkout, or during the scheduled balance check, clears the host's "connected" check, only when that checkout used the key still saved.
> - **Coupon codes are up to 32 characters** ("Codes are up to 32 characters.", box `maxlength` 32). A live forever coupon shows its offer once (the limited-months coupon still shows it twice; carried).
> - **Plan & billing dates use the normal date format** ("Nov 14, 2026") (#26).
> - **Group room errors** show on the room that caused them (the server adds `room_index` to a per-room 400); a one-room group reads "The 1 room will be cancelled…".
> - **Refund checks** report Amount, Method and Reason in one pass; a negative deposit deduction is flagged at the field with no question.
> - **Admin coupons:** "Total discount given" counts every coupon with two decimals (the server sends `summary.discount_total`); offers show thousands separators.
>
> *Not bugs (recorded so later sweeps don't file them):* the wordmark (#13, #23, #27; logotype, O2); the Ask button passing over a row while scrolling (#18, D-95); the two links inside a sentence (#38, D-95).
>
> *Focus learned:* a panel's Done returns focus to the button that opened it (the Filters button); buttons that change status carry a focus key per target status (`mt-status-<status>`), so focus never lands on the action that undoes the change.
>
> *Why:* a person should never be told something false or have their words cut without warning (the 300-character silent cut), small hosts should find everything from one search box, and the persistent focus class got its newest member fixed.

> *Security review 2026-10-15 (D-98; result PASS, six fixes, re-checked PASS).* (1) Every attach is rate limited, not only declined ones (20 per day per workspace, 60 per 15 minutes per IP), on top of the declined-attach limits, against card testing (the one Medium). (2) The 3-D Secure link from one-tap pay must be https, like the card check's. (3) One-tap pay refuses with 409 `PAYMENT_IN_PROGRESS` while a payment from the last 15 minutes is pending. (4) Card-check and plan refresh are limited to 60 per 10 minutes per workspace each. (5) The platform-admin exemption from the card check needs a verified email. (6) Deleting a workspace cancels its PayMongo subscription (the purge waits and retries if the cancel fails, so the row is kept), detaches the card and deletes the PayMongo customer (name, email, phone); the last two are best-effort. The deletion-cancel job runs whenever the PayMongo secret key is set, even with Subscriptions off.

> *Note 2026-10-19 (D-98, re-verify).* `start()` now looks at an earlier pending Payment Intent before it charges: if it passed, the trial starts; if it is still at the bank, no new charge is made; if it failed, one new charge is made; if PayMongo cannot be reached, the answer is 503 and nothing is charged. *Decision:* an owner cannot invite team members until the card is confirmed (the gate blocks invitations), so the "Waiting for your workspace owner" page is a safety net only. Also fixed after the re-verify: the 3-D Secure return now waits for the payment, a declined card shows its message, and the gated menu is flat. *Updated 2026-10-19 by D-100: a bank "Fail" on 3-D Secure shows the declined message, and a declined retry expires the old ₱20 intent and creates one new intent (the earlier wording that a declined card "keeps the same payment" is superseded). The attach step now locks the billing row.*

> **D-100 · 2026-10-19 · QA round 18 calls and admin workspace delete (alpha with small hosts).** The eighteenth QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone, card) was fixed the same day in about 23 code lanes plus the founder's request for an admin workspace delete. **One severe bug:** SEV1 on the live card check, the 3-D Secure return looped (the card page navigated before it refreshed the signed-in user); hotfixed and pushed alone as commit `1d2268d` before the rest. One SEV2 (cleaner pay defaulted to Cash); everything else SEV3. The merge gate, the security pass on the rest and the browser re-verify are tracked in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN. This adds to D-99, D-98 and D-97 and changes only what it says.
>
> *Founder request: platform admin can delete a workspace (AD-B, AD-F, AD-P, AD-A).* In Admin, open a workspace and use **Delete workspace** in its edit panel. The rules:
> - **Two modes.** **Delete in 14 days** schedules the deletion: the workspace goes view-only, the owner is locked out of changes and emailed, and a nightly job purges it when the date passes. **Delete now** purges at once. Both need the workspace name typed exactly; the box is trimmed on both sides and the match is otherwise exact (case counts). The server checks the same way (400 on `confirm_name`).
> - **Refused (409):** the demo workspace (`DEMO`), the admin's own workspace (`OWN_WORKSPACE`), and any workspace that has a platform admin as a member (`PLATFORM_ADMIN_WORKSPACE`, "This workspace belongs to a BalayHub admin and can't be deleted here."). A workspace already scheduled answers `ALREADY_SCHEDULED`; the panel then shows **Cancel deletion** and only **Delete now**.
> - **A lasting record.** Every delete writes a platform audit row with `organization_id` NULL (`platform.workspace_deletion_scheduled`, `platform.workspace_deletion_cancelled`, `platform.workspace_deleted`), carrying the workspace id and name and the mode, so the record outlives the purge. Delete now writes it before the purge. Scheduling and cancelling also write a row in the workspace's own log.
> - **The owner may cancel an admin-scheduled deletion** from Settings (the same **Cancel deletion** as for a deletion they asked for). *Why kept:* the intent is cleanup, not enforcement; a mistake should be easy to undo. If enforcement is ever needed it is a new decision.
> - **Purge order (`purgeOrganization` in `src/lib/account.js`):** (1) cancel the PayMongo subscription first (if that fails the purge stops, nothing is deleted, and the scheduler retries); (2) open a transaction and lock the workspace row (`FOR UPDATE`; a second purge of the same workspace finds it gone); (3) with the scheduler's `scheduledOnly` flag, re-check the deletion date under the lock, so a restored workspace is never purged; (4) delete every table row and the files; (5) after the commit, detach the saved card and delete the PayMongo customer (best effort, ids only logged). Admin "Delete now" answers 502 "The workspace could not be deleted. Nothing was deleted. Please try again in a minute." (`PURGE_FAILED`) when the purge fails or is not done; a workspace already gone answers 200. *Why this order:* the security check found the card was released before the transaction, so a failed purge could say "nothing changed" after the card was gone (M1), and a "Delete now" left no lasting record (M2).
> - Workspace badge **Deleting** in the Admin table while a deletion is scheduled; the Settings audit list names the actions "Workspace deletion scheduled by BalayHub", "Workspace deletion cancelled by BalayHub" and "Workspace deleted by BalayHub".
>
> *Calls from the sweep and the strategy:*
> - **K6 closed as by design.** A duplicate owner name is allowed after "Save anyway?" (D-97), so a database unique index would contradict the rule. No migration 039 this round. *Supersedes the round 17 carry item "Duplicate owner name has no database guard".*
> - **Coupon check order.** When a code is sent, the code is looked up first: an unknown or unusable code answers `COUPON_INVALID` (or the specific plan, used or other-coupon reason) even on a comped workspace. `COMPED` is answered only for a usable code or no code. This is what `docs/contracts/platform-coupons.md` section 1 always said; the code had checked comped first. *Refines the D-95 order of checks.*
> - **Limited-months coupon line.** The line under the total no longer repeats the offer. It reads "This payment covers N of your M coupon months." where M is the coupon's **total** months (not the months left); for a free coupon too. The offer stays in the coupon box. *Supersedes the round 17 carry item about the offer showing twice, and the D-95 wording "…This payment covers 1 of them."* The question of total months versus months left is in BLOCKERS.
> - **Channel-fee note.** When Airbnb, Booking.com or other channel stays in the period have no channel fee entered, Reports and Profit and loss show "N Airbnb stays have no channel fee entered." per channel, with a link to those reservations. It shows counts only, never money. Reports counts by check-in; Profit and loss by check-out (stays already ended). New route `GET /api/stay-reports/missing-fees`.
> - **Cleaner pay has no default method.** Mark paid's "How you paid" starts on "Choose…" and asks "Choose how you paid the cleaner." (D-92: never pre-select a money method). *It had defaulted to Cash (SEV2).* The server already required a method.
> - **Saved view renamed.** Reservations **Upcoming unpaid** is now **Unpaid, not yet checked out**: it keeps in-house unpaid stays, so the old word was wrong. The help article and the short-term guide use the new name.
> - **Card on file.** A bank "Fail" on 3-D Secure shows the declined message ("Your card was declined. Try another card, or ask your bank."). A declined retry expires the old ₱20 intent and creates one new intent; never two charges. `attach()` locks the billing row (row first, then workspace) and needs status pending. `DELETE /billing/card` sets `auto_renew` off in the database before it cancels at PayMongo, and a repeat removal with no card writes no second audit row. The Stage 1 coupon note keeps only "BalayHub never charges your card on its own; you renew here."; the "applied automatically" sentence shows only when automatic payment is on.
> - **Gated owners keep the top bar.** The assistant answers 402 while the card check gates the workspace (the server gate); no change.
> - **Commission advice.** The founder asked about a "free plan plus 2% on paid direct bookings" option. Advice was given; nothing is built. The decision (and what it would need: PayMongo split payments or monthly invoicing) is in BLOCKERS.
>
> *Small fixes (one line each; details in APP-FLOW section 31):* filters on phones (search and Filters share one row, quick filters move into the panel below 560px); **Clear all** and **Reset filters** keep the saved view; the theme switch labels stay on one line; Record payment reports amount and method errors in one pass; the Channels dialog shows "Connecting…" only after "Save it anyway?" is answered; the bank-return success panel takes focus on its heading; the focused button on Maintenance status and Daily close Reopen is no longer disabled (aria-busy and a repeat-click flag instead), so there is no focus drop to the page body; the inbox composer is named "Internal note" or "Message to {guest}"; Tenants "Owes at least (₱)" uses Rent's amount rule; shorter search placeholders; top search page keywords match word starts only; an expense search result's second line drops the category when the title already is the category; the host check-in card reads "5 (booked for 3)" like the gate pass; Reports date boxes are flagged invalid (aria-invalid and aria-describedby) for every error.
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the wordmark (logotype, D-99 O2); the Ask button passing over a row while scrolling (D-95); links inside a sentence (D-95).
>
> *Why:* a person should be told only what is true (the coupon line, the missing channel fees, the declined card), a money choice should never be made for them (cleaner pay), a cleanup tool must leave a record that outlives what it deleted, and the one live bug (the 3-D Secure loop) was fixed and shipped before anything else.

> **D-101 · 2026-10-20 · QA round 19 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The nineteenth QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone, card) found one severe bug (SEV1: a coupon was dropped after an unfinished bank step and the owner was charged full price), seven significant ones (SEV2) and the rest minor (SEV3). Fixed in about 25 code lanes; the calls are below. No migration.
>
> *Plan payments and coupons (the SEV1).*
> - **A failed or abandoned plan payment gives its coupon back.** When a one-tap payment's PayMongo intent is found failed (awaiting a new payment method with an error, cancelled, or failed), the payment row becomes **failed** and its coupon claim is released (usable again, months used unchanged). A pending row that has sat for more than 15 minutes is **not** let go on the clock alone (a security review found that a payment still going through at the bank could lose its coupon): the row is expired and its coupon released **only when PayMongo shows the payment dead**; otherwise it keeps blocking (see the in-progress bullet). The 2-day cleanup asks PayMongo first in the same way. **A payment that lands after a release takes the coupon back** (it may then go over the coupon's use limit, because the money was already taken; accepted). *Calls made while landing:* the coupon is released, **not re-applied for the owner**: after a failed payment the owner **types the code again**, and the price then shown and the price charged come from the same check (tested: ₱999 becomes ₱499.50 with the coupon). *Why:* before, the coupon stayed reserved by a payment that never happened, so the line said "1 month left" while the next charge was the full ₱999.
> - **A bank "Fail" on a saved-card payment is a failed payment.** The bank return and status checks report **failed** (not "pending") and Plan & billing shows "Your saved card couldn't be charged. Pay another way, or use a different card." as an alert; a paid return shows "Payment received. Thank you! Your plan is active." Either notice takes focus when it appears (the focus move is the point of the action).
> - **The in-progress wait names the real time.** "A payment is already in progress. Try again in N minutes." (N rounds up, at least 1; "1 minute"). Before refusing, the earlier intent is refreshed: if it failed, it is released and the new payment goes ahead. **After 15 minutes with PayMongo still showing it alive,** the message becomes "We're still waiting for your bank to finish an earlier payment. Pay another way, or try again later." and it keeps blocking.
> - **Plan & billing shows a payment that is waiting.** `GET /billing` returns `pending_payment`; while it is set, the page reads "A payment of ₱<amount> is waiting for your bank…" and hides the Pay button and the coupon box (before, it showed the full price). *Found in the round 19 re-verify (SEV2).*
> - **The pay picker follows "Plan after your trial".** It starts on the plan chosen there, else the current paid plan, else Growth; a free coupon uses the picker's plan too. *Why:* an owner who chose Pro was paying for Growth.
> - **Trial end for a workspace with no saved end date = created date + 14 days** (one helper, `trialEndsAt`). The days-left count and "covered months start when the trial ends" both use it. *Side effect, see BLOCKERS:* an older trial workspace with no saved end date may now show "0 days left" while still not locked out (view-only needs a saved end date). *Supersedes nothing; closes the round 18 "trial end" carry.*
> - The saved-card pay button shows only when a card is on file (a hidden "Pay ₱… with Card ending undefined" button is gone).
> - **A second browser tab on "Confirm your payment card"** after the card already passed goes home with no message (the server answers CARD_CHECK_NOT_REQUIRED), instead of a red "already confirmed".
> - **PayMongo key check:** a key with the right start but too short says "This key is too short. Paste the full Secret key from PayMongo." A wrong start keeps the old "starts with sk_" message. A key that has a symbol in it also keeps the start message (not changed).
>
> *Money and records.*
> - **Rent Record payment has no default method** (it had pre-chosen Cash, so the required check never fired). It starts on "Choose…" and says "Choose how the money was received." *D-92 rule: never pre-select a money method.*
> - **A voided rent printout** is titled "Voided payment record" (it said "Acknowledgement receipt" under a "not a receipt" line).
> - **The ₱60,000 expense was not a bug.** A ₱50,000-plus expense asks first; the save path ignores a second submit while the question is open. No change.
> - **Profit and loss "no channel fee entered" note:** the link opens the bookings that check out **before today** so the list matches the note's count (12 and 12, it was 13 and 12). The missing-fees count now reads stays that have already checked out. Reports still count by check-in date. *Round 41 (D-123): the Revenue tile's count reads 'check-ins'.*
> - **Plan coupons (admin):** the "Ended or off" card counts ended and off only (limit reached has its own filter). A missing start date says "Choose the day the coupon starts."
> - **Add-on price question:** "Change it" returns focus to the Price box.
>
> *Staff, bookings and guests.*
> - **Staff may see whether a guest has an email, never the address.** Staff reservation rows carry `guest_has_email` (true or false); this was intended (it lets staff tick "Email a receipt") and the matrix in BACKEND-SCHEMA section 6 now says so.
> - **Check-in needs a photo picked** (D-95; the doc had said a draft is kept). A draft is kept **only after a failed upload**. After a successful registration the tab title follows the new heading.
> - **"Do not rebook" names the flagged guest.** The create-reservation and create-group answers carry `guest_do_not_rebook_name` for owners and managers (the booking's own guest if flagged, else the flagged guest with the same email; empty if none). **Owners see the flagged record's name; a manager sees it only when that guest is in the manager's property scope, otherwise the booking's own guest name** (the flag itself is still true; D-97 keeps the other record hidden). The warning reads: Heads up: {flagged guest} is marked "Do not rebook" in Guests. *It named the new booking's guest before.* Group room table reads "1 night".
> - **Block dates that clash** use the same sentence as reservations ("These dates overlap a block on … on this listing." or the other guest's stay with code and dates) and the answer names the block form's boxes (start and end date), so the message shows in the banner instead of "Dates not available".
> - **Closed as not a bug:** a double-click on the confirm "Reopen day" leaving focus on the page body is **not a bug** (a click on empty page always moves focus there). Carry closed. The pending payment row and coupon claim the card lane left in the dev database were test data (cleared by the snapshot restore).
>
> *Inbox and notifications.*
> - **Send later times are Philippine time.** The label says "Send later (Philippine time, optional)", the chosen time is read as Manila time (+08:00), not the device zone, and the shown scheduled time is Manila time. *Why:* a host travelling abroad would have sent messages at the wrong hour.
> - **Notification settings list every type the person can receive,** each on or off, saved per person: owner 9, manager 8 (the seven stay alerts plus Cleaning tasks), staff the cleaning alerts. (My account already listed all nine; the Settings list had been missing four: mentions, assigned conversations, calendar sync problems and unit-owner activity.) Sending was already controlled by the switch.
>
> *Admin and layout.*
> - **Delete workspace panel stays open after "Delete in 14 days" and "Cancel deletion"** and redraws in place with the new state (Cancel deletion and Delete now, or the form again); focus goes to its "Delete workspace" heading. *Changes D-100, where the panel closed.* On phones the section has a gap and a divider above it.
> - **Phone sizes:** landing Log in and the language switch are at least 44px tall; the compact tab bars show **three equal columns for a group of 3 and two by two for a group of 4** (no orphan tab; Home and Housekeeping; the group fills the width below 560px); the landing section links are hidden below **768px** (they pushed Log in off screen at 390px; 12px gap from 768 to 959); the Assistant's long-term placeholder is "Ask anything, e.g. how do I record rent? (English or Tagalog)" and the Help Ask boxes read "e.g. Paano mag-record?" and "e.g. I-connect Airbnb?" so they fit at 390px; the More sheet's Close stays pinned (Log out still needs a scroll; carried); listing card actions share one boxed button style; lost-and-found guest link is a tap-size link; the pay page has a gap above "Add selected to my bill". Search hints are shortened to fit 390px (Inbox, Rent due, Payments, Expenses, Properties, Help, Listings).
> - **Phone Filters panel on quick-only bars** (Cleaner pay, Plan coupons) like the other pages; every quick select moved into the panel shows its label; the screen-size listener is removed when the bar leaves the page.
> - **Calendar on phones:** listing names that did not fit are cut **in the middle** (for example "QA r19…Unit 1") so units stay different. Accepted for now; a two-line wrap is carried.
> - **Rental Reports:** an empty custom date box is marked invalid with the message "Pick both dates to export."
>
> *Round 18 security Lows, closed:* the purge locks the workspace's payment rows before the workspace (same order as paying); the automatic monthly start re-checks auto-renew, a saved card and no deletion; marking a card check passed reads on the same connection. Tests: failed 3-D Secure then a new card; succeeded 3-D Secure then a second attach. A card attached during 3-D Secure is marked `billing_payments.payment_method = 'attached'` (documented, no new column).
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the wordmark (D-99 O2); the Ask button passing over a row (D-95); links inside a sentence (D-95); gated owners seeing the top bar (D-100).
>
> *Why:* a coupon must never be lost to a payment that did not happen, a person should be told the real wait and the real reason a payment failed, a money method is never chosen for them, and a warning should name the right person.

---

> **D-102 · 2026-10-20 · QA round 20 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twentieth QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone, card) found one severe bug (SEV1: the hosted "Pay another way" button was not guarded while a one-tap payment was waiting at the bank, so an owner could be charged twice), two significant ones (SEV2: a coupon with "Limit total uses" ticked and no number saved as unlimited; Reports and Stay reports lost keyboard focus after a page change) and the rest minor (SEV3). Fixed in about 15 code lanes; the calls are below. **One migration (039).**
>
> *Plan payments (the SEV1).*
> - **Only one plan payment may be open at a time for a workspace, one-tap or hosted.** A new charge does not start while another plan payment for the workspace is open. *Why:* two open payments meant the owner could pay twice for the same period.
> - **Hosted "Pay another way" while a one-tap payment is waiting:** the older payment is **ended at PayMongo first** (cancel the intent, then read it again, the same rule as the 15-minute expiry). The hosted checkout goes ahead **only when PayMongo shows the older payment dead**. If it is still alive: 409 `PAYMENT_IN_PROGRESS`, "A payment is still waiting for your bank. Finish it there, or try again later." Plan & billing also says, under "Pay another way" while a payment waits: "This cancels the payment waiting at your bank."
> - **One-tap while a hosted checkout is open:** 409, "A payment is already open in another window. Finish it there, or try again later." A hosted checkout open against a hosted request is guarded the same way (the older checkout session is expired at PayMongo and the new one goes ahead only when the old one is dead).
> - **The older payment turns out to have been paid:** 409 `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`, so the owner is told the money already arrived instead of being asked to pay again. This also covers an earlier payment found paid during the check itself. The last check is made under the workspace lock. *A security review's first BLOCK (High) found the paid-just-before path still charged again; fixed and re-reviewed as PASS.*
> - **A stale page cannot start a second full-price charge.** The page sends `seen_paid_until` (the paid-until date it last saw, echoed from the new top-level `paid_until` in `GET /billing`) with checkout and pay-with-saved-card. If it differs from the stored date, the answer is `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`; if the field is absent, there is no check. After the refusal the page refreshes the plan summary and history and moves focus. **Paying ahead from a fresh page stays allowed.** *Round 40 (D-122): a one-tap payment waits one minute after a paid plan payment.* *Found in the re-verify (SEV1: a second tab started a new full-price checkout after an approved payment).*
> - **Payment history keeps the card.** Migration 039 adds `billing_payments.card_brand` and `card_last4`, filled when a card payment is paid. The history reads them from the payment row (older rows fall back to the workspace's card), so **Remove card does not wipe the card from past payments.** *Found in the sweep (SEV3).*
> - **A declined card attach keeps answering 402,** on purpose (it was deliberate in the card check work: the same answer as the card gate, and the page treats both as "card not accepted"). Written down in BACKEND-SCHEMA. *Closes the review question from the card lane.*
> - **An applied coupon states its discount once** on Plan & billing (the green box with the code and Remove; the second line under the picker is dropped only when it says the same thing).
>
> *Plan coupons (admin).*
> - **A limit needs a number.** With "Limit total uses" ticked, an empty box says "Enter how many times the coupon can be used." (also refused by the server when the form says the limit is on; API callers that leave the field out are not affected). *The SEV2: it saved as unlimited.*
> - **A taken code marks the code box** (409 `CODE_TAKEN` carries the field). **Codes may be up to 32 characters,** and a longer one says "Codes can be up to 32 characters." instead of being cut silently.
>
> *Listings.*
> - **A host with 1 to 3 listings gets the compact page (D-85):** status tabs with a count of 0 are hidden (All stays), and Occupancy and Sort by move into the Filters panel, like the calendar.
> - **Technical details are folded.** The iCal row keeps its plain sentence; the raw error sits in a closed "Technical details" box. *The nightly rate of 0 was already refused in the form; not a bug.*
> - **Card buttons:** Edit, Archive and Bookings sit in a two-column grid on phones (an odd last button spans both), so none stands alone.
>
> *Keyboard focus.*
> - **After a redirect, focus lands on the page heading** (Reports and Stay reports; the page was being redrawn after the heading took focus). A tab click keeps its focus.
> - **Removing a filter chip or Clear all with the keyboard** moves focus to the **Filters button** (else the search box, else the heading), not the heading while the panel is open. On phones the chips row sits **above** the open panel.
>
> *Support.* **Cleaners may list their own support tickets** (the code already allowed it; the documentation follows the code). Own tickets only.
>
> *Smaller changes.* Block dates that clash name the clash once in the banner (the two date boxes are marked, no repeated text); New reservation shows every field problem in one pass (guest count, dates, email); Add listing does the same (building and nightly rate together); Inbox Subject placeholder "Subject (optional)"; Help new ticket example "e.g. Airbnb not syncing" and a shorter What happened? hint; Platform admin search hint "Workspace or owner", Tenants "Name, phone or unit"; gate pass has a Notes row and the Unit row matches the others; Housekeeping main tabs show two by two under 380px; theme switch labels wrap if needed at 320px (the rule had been overridden by a later phone block, fixed in the re-verify); the Delete workspace divider uses a stronger line in dark mode (see BLOCKERS, for information).
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* a double-click on **Reopen day** leaving focus on the page body (D-101 stands); an applied **coupon is not kept on reload** (it is checked again when the owner pays); **check-in consent must be ticked again** when a guest edits; **gap-night price tiles**; the **gate pass is English only** (founder questions only, not built); the **bell badge may lag up to 15 seconds** (the check is throttled); Log out below the fold on the phone More sheet for owners with many items (Close stays pinned; accepted).
>
> *Why:* one plan can only be paid once at a time, the history should show the card that was really used, a coupon limit should never silently become unlimited, and keyboard users should never lose their place.

---

> **D-103 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 21 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-first QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone, card, plus a PayMongo research lane) found no severe bug (no SEV1). It found three significant ones (SEV2: Clear all lost the first click; focus fell to the page body after a calendar save and after Cancel quote; a route change from the phone More sheet left focus on the More button) and the rest minor (SEV3). Fixed in about 14 code lanes; the calls are below. No migration.
>
> *Keyboard focus.*
> - **The first wrong field, in the order the form shows it, gets focus** (all forms). Before, the server's error order decided (Add tenant with a bad phone and a bad email focused Email). `showErrors` in ui.js now picks the first invalid field by position in the page.
> - **A route change chosen from the phone More sheet puts focus on the new page heading,** even when the old and new pages share the same heading text. The sheet gives focus back to the More button only when the page is still the one it opened on. *Round 47 (D-129): until the heading is drawn, focus stays on More, never the page body.*
> - **After the calendar grid reloads** (Block dates, Set price, New reservation from a cell, Remove block, drag-move), focus returns to the redrawn cell that opened the menu, else the page heading. **After Cancel quote,** focus lands on the page heading. *Round 38 (D-120): after a drag-move, focus is on the moved booking's bar (the control used).* *Why:* a redraw must never leave a keyboard user on the page body.
> - **A quote that does not exist shows a "Not found" heading** and focuses it (the same as a missing reservation).
> - **Clear all and the filter chip buttons are stable across redraws.** The buttons are built once and relabelled in place, so a click is never lost when a number box finishes editing under the pointer. *The SEV2: the first click on Clear all did nothing after a typed, unapplied number.*
> - **Empty coupon code:** pressing Apply with an empty box puts focus in the box.
>
> *Assistant panel.* **The open assistant panel closes when the page changes** (the floating button stays), so it never covers a submit button such as Send to support. Focus does not move.
>
> *Notifications.* **"Notifications on this device" means this browser's own push subscription.** The server says on only when the user has at least one push subscription; the page narrows that to this browser (service worker registration, a live subscription, and permission granted). Before, it read the saved notification choices, so it said "On" with nothing subscribed.
>
> *Forms.*
> - **Public booking form:** a field's error clears as soon as the guest changes that field (typing or choosing), a checkbox error clears when ticked, and the form banner goes when no field is marked. The guest-count box keeps its own live message.
> - **Reservation form:** a date clash is named once in the banner; both date boxes are marked with no repeated text (like the Block dates form), and focus goes to Check-in. **"Email a booking confirmation" is unticked and disabled while there is no guest email;** it ticks when an email is typed, unless the host unticked it.
> - **Quote form:** one pass shows every problem together (dates, guest limit, email, negative amounts), in the server's wording, like New reservation.
>
> *Plan & billing.*
> - **A coupon that is free and card-only says "once by card" once,** not twice.
> - **Plan cards sit one per row under 560px** (the labels are too long for three columns, which left Pro alone on a second row). The **Pay button wraps its label** inside its box, so the page no longer scrolls sideways at 320px.
> - **PayMongo cancelled intent is accepted in both spellings** ("cancelled" and "canceled"). When an intent is still alive after a cancel call, one line is logged with the billing id and the status (no secrets). The cancel endpoint path is still unconfirmed against the live API (see BLOCKERS).
>
> *Cleaner pay.* **The bulk bar on Cleaner pay counts unpaid cleans only** ("N unpaid", or "Nothing unpaid").
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the calendar toolbar wrapping to three rows at 390px; save buttons without a busy marker (a second press is already refused); the Cleaner menu saying "Cleaning" while the heading differs; the dev host's PayMongo key not decrypting in the dev snapshot (a dev environment mismatch; the known carry that the owner gets no warning stands). Not built, asked in BLOCKERS: folding rarely used More tools for 1 to 3 property owners, a shorter first Add listing, whether the Rent Filters panel should survive a tab switch, the "already used" wording for a used-up coupon.
>
> *Why:* a keyboard user should always land on the thing that needs them, a button should never move out from under a click, a switch that says "On" must be on, and a form should say each problem once and stop saying it when it is fixed.

---

> **D-104 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 22 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-second QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone) found no severe bug (no SEV1). It found 22 findings plus one unfiled page error: 5 significant (SEV2: focus left on the page body after a redirect or a first load; the dark-mode price on picked calendar days was too faint; Escape from a ?new=1 dialog on Unit owners and Tenants; free days growing on a plan change) and the rest minor (SEV3). 20 were real bugs, fixed in 17 code lanes. Two were product choices and are asked in BLOCKERS; one was a QA tooling gap. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-94, D-98, D-102 and D-103. No migration.
>
> *Keyboard focus.*
> - **After the router draws a page, it focuses that page's heading when (1) the page changed, (2) it is the first page drawn since the app loaded, or (3) the draw came from a redirect, even onto the page already open.** It does this only when focus is still free (on the body, nowhere, or on a removed element). A redraw of the same page that the person started (filters, saves) stays with the focus guard in ui.js (D-89); the router does not touch it. *Why not "focus the heading whenever focus is on the body":* it would race the guard, which may be waiting for a disabled control to come back. Rules (1) to (3) never overlap with a guard record. *Accessibility note:* on a first load, focus on the heading (no scroll) means the first Tab goes into the page content and Shift+Tab reaches the menu; the skip link stays first in the tab order. This matches every in-app page change. It is reversible (BLOCKERS d).
> - **Dialogs opened with `?new=1` open after the page has drawn,** not while the page is still a detached element. Unit owners, Tenants and Properties now open them in the next tick, only if the page is still connected, the same as Reservations, Listings, Housekeeping, Groups and Quotes. The dialog's opener is then the page heading, so Escape returns focus there. `openModal` is unchanged.
> - **Escape closes the Filters panel** and puts focus on the Filters button, when focus is inside the panel or on the button. It does nothing while a modal dialog is open or when the bar has no visible Filters button. An open native select list takes the Escape itself. *Why:* the More sheet and the assistant already close on Escape; the Filters panel was the odd one out (noted in four lanes over two rounds).
> - **Chip and Clear all focus order extended (amends D-102):** the Filters button if visible, else the search box, else the removed filter's own control (for a chip) or the first visible quick control (for Clear all). Focus uses no scroll, so Cleaner pay, which has quick filters only, no longer jumps to the top.
> - **Voiding one of two charges with the same description** labels the buttons "Void <description> (1 of 2)" and "(2 of 2)", counting voided rows too so the place never changes, and gives each a stable focus key. Unique descriptions keep the plain label.
> - **Settings and Listings:** after "Save anyway" on an odd calendar link, focus lands on the new row's Sync now button (else the dialog heading).
> - **A bar rebuild never throws.** On Rent, changing the Property filter while a typed, uncommitted Amount is in the box no longer raises a page error: the remembered property is updated before the bar is redrawn, and a redraw asked for during another one runs once afterwards.
>
> *Forms.*
> - **One pass shows every problem together, on every form.** A filled email box that is not an email now adds "Enter a valid email address." in the same pass as the required fields (Add owner showed it only after the fee was fixed). Add season checks the price mode and the date order in the same pass: "The last night must be on or after the first night."
> - **Guest forms clear a field's error as soon as that field changes** (typing or choosing), a checkbox's error when it is ticked, and the form banner when no field is still marked. This was the public booking form's rule (D-103); the Quote and Check-in forms now follow it.
> - **Edit reservation with a date clash** marks both date boxes, states the clash once in the banner and focuses Check-in, the same as New reservation.
> - **New support ticket checks the file before sending.** A file that is not a PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP, or is over 10 MB, is refused on the field with "Upload a PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP file up to 10 MB." and no ticket is created; the subject and body stay filled.
>
> *Layout and colour.*
> - **The small price on a picked check-in or check-out day is white in every theme** (it was 4.32:1 in dark mode; 4.5:1 is needed).
> - **On phones the open assistant panel ends just above the bottom bar** (its bottom is the bar's height plus the safe area), so the bar's tabs stay visible and tappable and a tab tap still changes the page and closes the panel. *Rejected:* raising the bar above the panel, which would cover the panel's message box.
> - **The inbox search hint is "Guest or code"** so it is not cut at 390px with a filter count; the screen-reader name keeps the full meaning.
>
> *Channels and booking page.*
> - **The Channel box on Connect calendar starts empty ("Choose the channel").** A pasted link from a known site picks that site (airbnb.* Airbnb, booking.com Booking.com, agoda.* Agoda, vrbo.* Vrbo, expedia.* Expedia, trip.com Trip.com). Any other link leaves the choice to the host, and Connect calendar with no channel says "Choose which site this calendar link is from." on the Channel field. A choice the host made by hand is never overwritten by the link. *Why:* a link from another site was saved and labelled Airbnb without the host noticing.
> - **Settings, Direct booking, with no listing on the booking page,** says "None yet" with a **Go to Listings** link. The "Booking page and payments" section of Edit listing opens by itself while no listing in the workspace is on the booking page. A switch on each listing card is not built (BLOCKERS c). *Round 46 (D-128): Add listing keeps it folded; whether it should open there too is BLOCKERS round 22 (c).*
>
> *Plan & billing.*
> - **A second tab refused with PAYMENT_IN_PROGRESS refreshes** to the waiting view, the same as after PAYMENT_RECEIVED. The Pay button goes and the "Try again in 15 minutes" alert does not show, because the waiting view already says it. When there is no waiting payment (the hosted "already open in another window" case), the message shows as before. The server text and the 409 contract (D-98, D-102) are unchanged.
> - **Free days never grow on a plan change (amends D-94 in practice).** When the plan changes at payment, *paid days* (days to the end of the plan that fall inside a paid, non-zero plan payment) convert at the price ratio as before: `floor(paidDays x old / new)`. *Free days* (coupon months and trial days) move day for day to a cheaper plan and shrink by the ratio to a dearer one: `floor(freeDays x min(1, old / new))`. *Why:* converting at the price ratio exists so that money already paid is not lost; free days carry no money, and multiplying them (44 free days became 88 Starter days, one 499-peso payment gave about 3.9 months) went against D-94, where a free coupon covers a set number of billed months. Unchanged: renewing the same plan early, paying during a trial, the 20-peso card check, and used-up coupon months. No migration. *Open default:* days bought with a partly discounted coupon still convert at list prices (BLOCKERS a).
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the new-reservation draft restoring an untick of the confirmation box was not reproduced and is closed unless seen again; booking bars starting mid-cell on the calendar was not tested and is carried; the dev host's PayMongo key not decrypting is a known dev environment mismatch; PayMongo's spelling of "cancelled" stays open (round 21 BLOCKERS d). The QA harness `mark.sh unreachable` not blocking the card-check calls is a tooling gap, fixed before round 23's card lane. *(See D-105: it was not fixed; it was fixed in round 23.)* Not built, asked in BLOCKERS: fewer buttons on each unit card on phones, a booking-page switch on each listing card, and a note on the Templates page saying which guest language the templates are in.
>
> *Why:* a keyboard user should never be left on the page body, a form should say every problem once and stop saying it when it is fixed, a free coupon should give exactly the free time it promises, and a wrongly labelled calendar link is data the host trusts and cannot see.

> **D-105 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 23 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-third QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone) found no severe bug (no SEV1). It found 17 findings: 3 significant (SEV2: the phone More sheet put Log out on top of Supplies so a tap signed the owner out; focus left on the page body after an address change on the same page; Add photo on a housekeeping task left focus on the body) and the rest minor (SEV3, one raised to SEV2 because it shared a cause with a SEV2). 16 were real bugs, fixed in 16 code lanes plus a tooling lane. One was ruled "works as decided" (ONB-3, below). The guest lane was clean, and every round 22 fix held except the QA harness gap that D-104 said was fixed and was not. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-98, D-103 and D-104. No migration.
>
> *Fixes that landed at the start of the round (before the sweep reports were adjudicated).*
> - **Plan change, paid days weighted by what was actually paid (BILL23; closes the older Medium that D-104 left open).** Each remaining day counts by the share of the list price that was actually paid for it: `min(1, amount / list_amount)` of the plan payment that covers that day. Where two payments overlap, the highest share wins for that day. Days that no paid share covers are the free remainder, and they move at most day for day (`min(1, old / new)`, as D-104). *Why:* a coupon-discounted payment (for example 50% off) was counted as fully paid and so converted at list prices, giving a little extra on a change to a cheaper plan. This closes the "smaller" part of BLOCKERS round 22 (a); the server needs a restart to pick it up. *Amends D-104 and D-94 in practice.*
> - **Plan & billing refreshes when the tab comes back (SET23).** The page listens for the tab being shown again and for the window getting focus. It re-reads the billing state and redraws only if it changed (pending payment, paid-until date, renewal plan or plan). It checks at most once every 5 seconds and stops when the person leaves Settings. *Follow-up in this round (CARD-2):* a return inside the 5-second window is not dropped; one trailing check is scheduled for when the window ends, there is only ever one pending timer, and leaving Settings clears it.
> - **Rent keeps a typed Amount when the Property filter changes (RENT23).** The filter box that has focus is committed before the bar is rebuilt, the count request is not sent twice, and the Amount box and the filter chips keep what the person typed (the earlier symptom: the box emptied and the amount was applied twice).
>
> *Keyboard focus.*
> - **Address changes count as page changes for focus (SEV2, BKG-1, MNY-3, N-2).** A draw caused by an address change the person made (the browser's `hashchange`: a typed or edited address, Back or Forward, a plain `#` link) focuses the page heading even when the path is the same, for example Reservations `?view=all` to `?view=upcoming`, Profit and loss `?month=`, Expenses `&category=`. It does this only when focus was free at the moment the address changed (on the body, nowhere, on a removed element, or in the menu, top bar, bottom bar, skip link or assistant button: the same test the heading focus already uses). If focus was on a control inside the page (a tab link, the Daily close date picker, which sets the address itself), the router leaves it to the focus guard as before. Redraws from in-page controls (filters, saves, `rerender()`, `navigate()`) fire no `hashchange` and are unchanged. *Why check when the address changes, not after the draw:* the router can then never race the focus guard, which only holds a record when a control inside the page had focus. The router's truth table (amends D-104) is now: first draw yes; new path yes; drawn under a skeleton yes; redirect yes; same path with the page kept, no redirect and no address change no; **same path with an address change yes.** The `history.replaceState` correction of a bad Reservations view fires no event and is not the cause.
> - **A read taken before the page's heading exists does not count (N-1).** On a fresh full page load the heading must have focus by the 2.5-second read; a 300 ms read of the body on a fresh load is not a finding. Re-verify keeps the 300 ms read for every other event.
