> - **Add photo on a housekeeping task keeps focus (SEV2, OPS-2).** The file input carries a focus key so the focus guard finds its replacement after the redraw, and the double-upload block uses a busy flag with `aria-disabled` instead of `disabled` (a disabled control drops focus to the body), the same as `makeForm`. "Photo uploaded." stays as the toast.
>
> *Phone More sheet (SEV2, OPS-1).*
> - **Every row in the More sheet can be tapped at 390 x 844 at every scroll position, and Log out never overlaps another row.** Log out sits after a clear gap (a divider and at least 16 px), so a mis-tap near the last link cannot sign anyone out. Log out may still be below the fold (round 21's do-not-file stands); it just cannot sit on another row. NAV2 (round 21) is unchanged: a link to the page already open closes the sheet and focuses its heading, a different page lets the router focus, Close or Escape returns to More. One lane owns both the script and the style file because the cause was not pinned to a line when the round was planned.
>
> *Forms.*
> - **Every form reports all its problems in one save (a finding in four reports: LT-1, MNY-1, MNY-2, ONB-1).** The cause: the pass on the device already collected everything it knew, but it did not know the server's field-type rules (money format, negative money, "greater than zero", whole numbers and the min and max of number boxes, phone format), so when it found something else it never reached the server and the server's messages waited for the second save. *Rejected:* removing the early return, because sending a form the device already knows is wrong could create a record when a view check is device-only, and does not work offline. The fix has three parts. (1) **The device pass reads the server's field-type rules from the field itself,** after the view's own check has run (so a view's own wording always wins), for every filled, visible, enabled box: money (`must be a valid amount`, `cannot be negative`, and `must be greater than zero` on a box marked positive), number boxes (whole number, at least min, at most max) and phone boxes (`Enter a valid phone number.`). A money field opts in with `positive: true` where the server says positive (Record payment, Add expense, Add unit rent). (2) **Views add their own check only for cross-field or business rules** the type rules cannot see: the Settings invite (a manager or cleaner needs a property) and New discount code (no spaces, 3 to 20 letters, numbers or dashes, a percent or an amount but not both, end date after start date). Bulk changes is not built on `makeForm`, so it marks its own boxes and focuses the first wrong one. (3) **Servers answer with every field problem in one 400:** a route that runs extra rules after `validate()` puts them in `validate(body, spec, { check })` instead of throwing one at a time. This round: `POST /discount-codes`. Other routes are fixed when a sweep finds them.
> - **A form built without `makeForm` clears a field's message on edit.** D-104's guest-form rule (a field's message, its red mark and `aria-invalid` clear as soon as that field changes, and the banner goes when no field is still marked) now covers every form. Confirm your card and Get started are built by hand; **each keeps its own small copy, no shared helper this round** (the two pages have different shapes, a helper that fits both would be bigger than two six-line copies, and it would make both lanes wait for `ui.js`; Book, Quote and Check-in already keep their own copies).
> - **Internal notes show a picked teammate as `@Name`,** not `@[Name](id)`. The composer keeps a map from each picked name to its user ID; on Save note each `@Name` still present becomes the coded form, so the stored text, the highlight and the notification are unchanged. A saved draft stores the coded form and restoring it shows `@Name` again. Two teammates with the same full name: the last one picked wins (rare; accepted). A message to a guest is never coded. *Not a founder question:* only what the host sees while typing changed.
> - **New group booking:** when any room row's listing or dates change, the "These rooms are taken" list and its banner line go (the same way the reservation form's date clash clears); the next save checks again and lists only rooms that still clash.
> - **The "Void this charge?" dialog names the line as the list does:** `<description>: ₱<amount>`, adding `(<category>)` when the description is not just the category's own name; a charge with no description keeps the category.
>
> *Layout and access.*
> - **Reports, Performance: under 560 px the four headline cards stack one per row** (a modifier on that row only), so the Revenue detail no longer wraps into about eight short lines. Dashboard and other stat rows keep two per row on phones.
> - **My account shows the Team line to owners only (PHN-1).** BACKEND-SCHEMA section 6 is the rule and the code was wrong: the server sends `members` only to owners (and does not run the count for anyone else), and the page shows the line only when `members` is present.
>
> *Rulings: not bugs, not filed.*
> - **ONB-3, a Tagalog sign-up reaches an English-only Confirm your card page: works as decided.** Setup and Confirm your card are host screens, so they are English under D-09. The language choice covers the public pages, Get started, Check your email and the verify step. Translating the card page (about 20 short lines) is offered in BLOCKERS (b), not built.
> - **N-3** (a scripted select on the assignment box leaves the body focused when focus was not on it first): a tool artifact; the real keyboard path keeps focus. **N-4** (onboarding units have `monthly_rent 0.00` in the database although Add unit refuses 0): not a bug. A blank optional monthly rent is stored as 0.00 (`BLANK_TO_ZERO` in `src/routes/properties.js`); "greater than zero" applies only to a rent someone typed. **N-5** ("‹ Reservations" once returned to the saved view instead of the view the person came from): seen once, not reproduced in two more runs; watch next round. **N-6** (Cleaner pay's 196 px scroll case): cannot be set up on today's data; held at 73 and 149 px, the deepest scroll the data allows. Closed. **N-7** (the support tickets table was empty at the start): expected, the dev database was restored between rounds.
>
> *QA tooling (correction to D-104).* D-104 said the harness `mark.sh unreachable` gap was fixed before round 23's card lane. It was not: the fake PayMongo checked `unreachable` only on checkout sessions, not on customers, payment intents, attach or the key check. Fixed in round 23 (tooling, not product): while `unreachable` is on, every server-side call to PayMongo fails with `fetch failed`. The browser's card call to the local 3-D Secure server is unchanged (that is the browser reaching PayMongo, a different state). The server needs a restart with the preload before the card lane.
>
> *Why:* a button that signs the owner out must never sit under a link, a keyboard user should never be left on the page body, a form should say every problem once and stop saying it when it is fixed, a discounted payment should convert for what was paid, and an owner should see only what the access matrix says.

> **D-106 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 24 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-fourth QA sweep (9 browser lanes: bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone) found 20 findings: 1 severe (SEV1: a New reservation that was closed and reopened on another listing kept the first listing's suggested price as if the host had typed it, and a booking was saved at 9,200 where 6,200 was right), 5 significant (SEV2) and 14 minor (SEV3). Two were ruled "works as decided" (below). The rest came down to 17 real causes, fixed in 18 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane. The inbox lane was clean, and every round 23 fix held by effect on every surface. The round is classed hard: the same classes came back (forms that report one problem at a time, now for dates and cross-field rules) and the SEV1 is a state-boundary bug in a helper that eight forms share. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-103, D-104 and D-105. No migration.
>
> *Money and saved drafts.*
> - **A saved form draft keeps only what the person typed (SEV1, BKG-1).** A field is saved only while it still holds the value the person last gave it (an `input` or `change` on that field; a value restored from the draft counts as the person's). A value the app writes later (a quote's suggested price, a tenant's outstanding balance, a default date or guest count) drops out of the draft and is never restored as typed. Passwords and files are still never stored. The fix is in the shared draft helper, so it covers every form that keeps a draft, not only New reservation. *Why:* a booking was saved at another listing's price. A typed amount restored onto another listing stays the host's number, with the existing "Use rate" offer showing, as before.
> - **Every money line on a receipt is as of that payment (BKG-2).** The charge lines, the extras total, the booking total and the remaining balance all describe the stay as it stood when the payment was recorded: a charge added later, or a refund recorded later, no longer changes a re-printed receipt. This extends the rule that the remaining balance is as of the payment (annotated "see D-106" on that line). *Known limit:* a change to the stay's own price (accommodation, cleaning or other fees) after the payment still shows at today's price, because the old price is not stored. A stored snapshot would need a migration and five places that record a payment; not built, carried as a watch item.
> - **Rows of the same amount and date get different button names (BKG-3).** On payments, refunds, owner payouts and withdrawals, the Void, Receipt and Email buttons of a row say the row's own identifier when it has one (the receipt number for a payment, for example `Void payment AR-000100 of ₱3,000.00 on Oct 20, 2026`), else `(n of m)` among rows of the same amount and date. A row with no twin keeps its old name. This extends D-105's rule for charges.
>
> *Forms.*
> - **Every form reports all its problems in one save, now including dates and cross-field rules (extends D-105 (a)).** A date box checks its own earliest and latest day on the first save (a future date where none is allowed, a date outside the allowed window). Rules a single box cannot say are checked in the same pass: a lease end before its start, a recurring job's next-due window, Cleaner pay's per-clean and per-hour ceilings and a zero amount, and a Channels calendar link (empty or not a link). Add repair cost and Add rate must be above zero.
> - **The booking page's Guests message clears as a valid count is typed (GST-1, N-8; extends D-103 and D-105 (b)).** The message, the red mark and the greyed Continue clear as soon as the count is valid, and the price quote follows 500 ms after typing stops. An invalid count is still said when the person leaves the box, not while typing. The fallback maximum is 50 everywhere (the server's ceiling), where the box used 20 and its own check 50.
> - **A dialog never opens on a disabled control (MNY-1).** Dialogs skip disabled fields when picking where focus starts, and Edit pricing rule disables Rule and Applies to before it opens, so it opens on Days.
> - **A reason box left empty is marked invalid as well as described (MNY-3).** This covers every confirm dialog with a reason box (for example Void payout).
> - **Edit listing shows a blank Maximum guests when none is stored, and blank means no limit (ONB-1).** The default of 2 is for new listings only. Before, opening Edit listing and saving without a change stored 2 and made quotes for 3 or more guests refuse.
> - **Add unit on a workspace that takes stays asks the cleaning fee and maximum guests, like Add listing (ONB-3).** One owner, one listing form (D-85): the guest limit protects a stay. Not a founder question; told to the founder for information in BLOCKERS (b).
> - **Bulk changes: a negative price says "cannot be negative"** (MNY-2), like every other money box.
>
> *Shell, access and wording.*
> - **The shell rebuilds as soon as the signed-in person's menu changes (CARD-2).** When a refresh of the signed-in person changes anything the menu is built from (a card passing), the sidebar and top bar are rebuilt around the page on screen, with no route change and no redraw of the page. Focus that was inside the page is put back on the same control. A refresh that ends signed out does not rebuild; the router handles it.
> - **While the card is due, the top bar shows only Help and the account menu (CARD-1).** No Search, Bookmarks, Notifications or "/" key. The full top bar returns on the success panel.
> - **A PayMongo outage says the same sentence on one-tap pay and Pay another way (CARD-3):** "We couldn't reach the payment service. Nothing was charged. Please try again in a moment."
> - **My account, Preferences follows the monthly-rental rule already decided (CARRY-N1, carried since round 5).** DECISIONS and APP-FLOW already say a monthly-rental owner sees a note that alerts aren't available yet, with no Turn on or Send a test button; the My account row did not follow it. Fixed. This closes the carry item; it was never a founder question.
>
> *Layout.*
> - **Narrow phone tabs wrap their label instead of clipping it (OPS-2):** "Today & overdue (6)" no longer loses its ")" at 320 px.
> - **A table cell that holds only an action button centres the button on the row's first line (MNY-4):** the Turn off / Turn on button on Discount codes sat about 8 px low.
> - **REV24 (landed before the sweep reports were adjudicated):** the Revenue card detail on Performance is three lines at 390 px, the note having moved to a footnote. This closes item 1 added at the round 23 gate.
>
> *Rulings: not bugs, not filed.*
> - **PHN-1, calendar Earlier, Later and Today *(named Previous week and Next week since D-122)* pressed by a script with focus on the body leave focus on the body: works as decided.** D-105 (c) says redraws from in-page controls (filters, saves, `rerender()`, `navigate()`) fire no `hashchange` and are unchanged. The week buttons are in-page controls, and focus was on the body before the press, so the app lost nothing. A real pointer click and the real keyboard path (Tab to Later, Enter) keep focus on the button. Moving focus to the heading after every in-page redraw would break the keyboard user's place. Same ruling as round 23 N-3. No router change.
> - **BKG-4, focus after Remove block lands on the redrawn day cell: works as decided.** The decision on calendar reloads (Block dates, Set price, New reservation from a cell, Remove block, drag-move) already says focus returns to the redrawn cell that opened the menu, else the page heading.
> - **BANNER24 (the form banner "Please check the highlighted fields." read twice): not a bug.** The accessibility tree holds one `role="alert"` node; its wrapper has no role. This closes item 2 added at the round 23 gate.
> - **SESSION24 (a saved owner session ended after other lanes signed in as the same owner): intended.** A session ends after 12 hours idle (`SESSION_IDLE_HOURS`) or 7 days (`SESSION_TTL_HOURS`), or by a sign-out action; there is no cap on sessions per person. Saved lane states older than 12 hours must sign in again. Written in BACKEND-SCHEMA (sessions) and already in TRD security. This closes item 3 added at the round 23 gate.
> - **N-1** (`PATCH /api/push/settings` with stay alert types for a long-term workspace answers 200 and stores nothing): not a bug. The route refuses a known alert type the person cannot receive with a 400 and ignores keys that are not alert types, as every PATCH ignores unknown fields. **N-2** (staff have no Reports tab while the access table says staff view the dashboard and financial report for assigned properties): the screen and the server agree (staff get the money summary on Home); the table wording is ambiguous and is asked in BLOCKERS (a), no code change. **N-3** (the PayMongo key reads "Saved, not checked yet" after Connect): not filed; most likely the card lane had the harness `unreachable` switch on at that moment (one shared harness); re-verify connects a key with it off. **N-4** (an English host's booking page once drew its calendar in Tagalog): explained, the inbox lane switched the guest language during the run. **N-5** (Mara's Profit and loss once showed a refund under Rio 2210 that belongs to Loft A): not reproduced, another lane moved the booking between reads; watch next round. **N-6** (picking a teammate inserts a trailing space): cosmetic, the space is deliberate so typing can continue. **N-7** (the email message reads "Enter a valid email address." on Booking and "Please enter a valid email address." on Quote): copy only, carried for a copy pass.
>
> *Why:* a saved price must be a price the host typed or accepted, a re-printed receipt has to add up, two buttons that do different things must not have the same name, a form should say every problem once, and the menu a person sees should match their account the moment it changes.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 24 re-check):* three more fixes landed before the commit, on the same rules: the printed receipt shows the itemised charges and extras total exactly as the emailed receipt (both as of the payment); the gated top bar hides the desktop search box from the first paint, not only after a route change; and Record payment's amount, being an app suggestion, follows the tenant picked in the open dialog unless the person typed their own (the D-106 draft rule applied inside one dialog). Ruled not a bug: a channel the host picks by hand is kept even when the pasted link is from another site. Carried to round 25: the Ask assistant button in the phone gated top bar, and the missing balance line on a restored Record payment draft.

> **D-107 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 25 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-fifth QA sweep opened with two carry fixes already landed: the Ask assistant button is hidden while the card check is due (ASSIST-GATE), and a restored Record payment draft draws its Current balance line while a restored Add expense keeps its property and unit (RENT-DRAFT). Nine browser lanes ran (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone); the ops lane was partial because its server hit the time limit. They found 23 findings: none severe (SEV1), 2 significant (SEV2: End lease with no confirmation; focus jumping to Filters after Back) and 21 minor (SEV3). 18 were real causes, fixed in 17 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane; 1 was a doc fix only; 3 were ruled "works as decided" or "not a product bug"; 1 was a duplicate of an open founder question. The phone lane was clean and every round 24 fix held by effect. The round is classed hard: the "all problems in one save" class came back on six forms, and the draft class came back as a link-filled value overwritten by an old draft. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes) and D-103 to D-106. No migration.
>
> - **Ending a lease asks a question that names the tenant, the unit and the move-out date,** and starts on Keep lease. An ended lease still can't be reopened in the app; asked in BLOCKERS (a). *Why:* one press with defaults ended a live tenancy.
> - **A rebuilt filter bar puts focus back on the same control, including a page's own control in the bar** (Inbox Show, Reservations Saved view, Profit and loss Month, Cleaner pay Month, Help and Housekeeping tabs). Only a control that no longer exists (a removed chip) falls back to Filters. Extends D-104 and D-105 (c).
> - **Every form reports all its problems in one save (extends D-105 (a) and D-106):** New group booking (guest and every room), Add charge (a zero price), Record payout (a zero amount), guest Check-in (email and mobile shape), Get started step 2 (the summary line), and a receipt file's type and size on Record payment and Add expense, checked before anything is saved.
> - **Messages read as sentences:** a number box that can't go below zero says "cannot be negative"; a field whose label is a question or a phrase uses a short name in messages (Supplies: Low-stock level, On hand, Quantity).
> - **A value the page fills from the link it was opened with wins over an older saved draft** (Help's follow-up subject and topic). Defaults the page fills by itself are still replaced by a typed draft (D-106). **A value the person picks from a menu counts as theirs, like typing** (a template inserted into a guest message is kept in that conversation's draft).
> - **A choice the form insists on shows the required mark** even when the form's own sentence is used for the message (Record payment and Record payout Method).
> - **A discount code that has already ended is asked about before saving, not refused;** the toast says when a code has ended or has not started yet.
> - **While the card check is due, My account says "Waiting for your card check"** with one Confirm your card button.
> - **A heading the app focuses on arrival shows no focus ring;** controls keep theirs (D-104 unchanged: focus still goes to the heading).
> - **Reports tabs are one Tab stop** (the selected tab).
> - **Bulk changes shows about six listings before its list scrolls, and shows a shadow where more are hidden.**
> - **Guest pages use one wording per rule:** "This place allows up to N guests." and "Enter a valid email address." (closes the round 24 copy carry). Cleaner pay's rate select is labelled "Rate type".
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* a template inserted into a guest message is restored after a reload (the person's choice, per-conversation draft); the abandoned hosted checkout seen by the card lane was the shared harness's state (the app expires an open checkout before a new charge, and the dev row reads expired); `#/receipts/<id>` on a monthly-rental workspace goes home with the module toast (stays-only page); "Pay ₱999.00 with Visa ending ..." is right and APP-FLOW is corrected (two decimals everywhere); "Save listing" on Add and "Save changes" on Edit; a zero deposit or a calendar block ending before it starts is checked by the server alone when nothing else is wrong; iPhone Safari tap focus asked as a device check. A shorter first Add listing stays the open founder question from round 21 (a). Carried for a copy pass: the verify link's second-visit wording, Mara's "Select..." listing picker.
>
> *Why:* a step that cannot be undone must ask first and name what it ends, a keyboard user should stay on the control they were using, a form should say every problem once, and a value the person chose, from a menu or a link, should not be overwritten by something older.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 25 re-check):* every fix held in the browser. One late fix landed before the commit: the card page's own stylesheet drew a focus ring on "Your card is confirmed", overriding the D-107 heading rule; that rule now wins there too. Ruled not bugs: Record payment Cancel discards a typed draft while Escape keeps it, and a failed Pay another way stays "waiting for payment" until the next one-tap pay expires it.

> **D-108 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 26 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-sixth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The areas the round 25 ops lane did not reach (Maintenance, Daily close, Supplies edit and archive, the manager mara@, the staff member sam@, the top bar) are now covered and clean. The lanes filed 16 findings: 1 severe as filed (a double charge from two tabs), 4 significant and 11 minor. Reading the code while tracing the severe one found 1 more significant money bug (BIL-1, below). 13 real causes were fixed in 13 code lanes, plus a pins lane and a docs lane. The severe finding was ruled **not a product bug**: the test script pressed the Pay button twice, and the stale-page guard held. 1 finding was ruled works as decided (Type starts on Studio) and 1 was a duplicate. Every round 25 fix held, except the draft on "Change it", whose cause turned out to be shared by every dialog. The round is classed hard: money concurrency was in play, and the one-save, required-mark, tab-stop and draft classes all came back. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-98 (card on file) and D-103 to D-107. No migration.
>
> - **The two-tab "double charge" was a test-script press, not an app bug.** The card lane's tester saw two charges within a fifth of a second (₱899.50 with a coupon, then ₱1,799.00). We read the script and the database. The script clicked tab 2's one-tap Pay button once as a "check" (that was the ₱899.50 charge), and later its second click waited for the page to redraw and then pressed the new Pay button (an early renewal, which adds a month, as the page says). The stale tab was refused with "That payment was just received. Check your plan before paying again." So the stale-page guard held, and nothing was taken twice by one press. Whether one-tap pay should also ask before paying again right after a payment is a founder question (BLOCKERS round 26 (a)); built as it was, with no question.
> - **BIL-1, a real gap, now closed: one open plan payment per workspace now includes a free coupon activation.** A free-coupon activation in one tab and a card payment in another, started in the same moment, could both go through: the owner got the free month and was also charged for a month. It was found by reading the code, not seen in the sweep. While a free activation is being applied (and for at most 2 minutes if one is left pending by a crash), a second plan payment is refused with "A payment is already in progress. Try again in a moment." *Why:* money must never be taken twice for one intent. Tests now run two payments at once.
> - **A coupon the owner applied stays applied when they change the plan after the trial, automatic payment or the card.** It is checked again for the current plan, and the server's refusal is shown if it no longer fits. A redraw after a payment, or after another tab paid, does not carry it (the money changed, so the person starts from what the server says). Extends D-106: what the person typed is kept.
> - **After a two-tab race the losing tab says what is true now:** "That payment was just received. Check your plan before paying again." when the other payment finished, nothing when it failed (the person may pay), and the waiting view while it is still waiting.
> - **A dialog that backs out of a question keeps its draft, in every dialog, not only Add expense.** The shared dialog code now passes on "nothing was saved". Cancel still discards a typed draft (the D-107 gate ruling stands); Escape keeps it.
> - **A receipt file that is empty is refused before anything is saved** ("This file is empty. Choose the receipt file again."). A receipt that fails to upload after the expense or payment is saved says so in one message ("Expense saved, but the receipt didn't upload: ..." or "Payment saved, but the receipt didn't upload: ..."), never under a plain success message. *Wording superseded 2026-10-21 by D-110: "This file is empty. Choose the file again." on every control.*
> - **When the router removes a dialog, focus goes to the page heading; Back or Forward always closes a dialog the address no longer asks for.** This covers the `?new=1` dialogs on every page and extends D-105.
> - **Every tab list has one Tab stop (the selected tab),** set in one place for the whole app: Rent, Housekeeping, Listings, Maintenance, Help, Home and any future tab list. Arrow keys, Home and End still move between tabs. Extends D-107 (Reports).
> - **Every form reports all its problems in one save (extends D-105 (a), D-106, D-107):** Edit reservation (the date order shows together with the guest count) and Add a season (a zero price shows together with a bad minimum stay).
> - **Required marks:** the reservation Record payment Method (direct bookings) and Record refund Method.
> - **The active-filter chips sit under the bar, above the Filters panel, at every width** (on desktop they had been below the open panel, off screen).
> - **A manager on Cleaner pay is told to ask the owner about rates.** Rates stay owner-only; showing them to managers read-only would change the access table, so it is asked in BLOCKERS round 26 (d).
> - **Guest pages, one sentence per rule (continues D-107):** "Please enter your full name.", "Please enter your mobile number.", "Please read and agree to the house rules.", "Please agree to continue." Check-in keeps its own name ("as on your ID") and consent ("so your host can register you with the building") sentences, because those rules are different. Tagalog follows each.
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* Add listing's **Type starts on Studio on purpose** (the shortest first listing, which is what the open question in round 21 (a) asks for; a host-only field that can be changed in Edit listing; a studio is the most common short-stay unit for 1 to 3 property hosts; the monthly-rental Add unit keeps "Choose..." because its type matters to the rent roll; the round 20 rule about Method selects is about money and does not apply); "Please fill in the highlighted fields." (every highlighted field is only empty) and "Please check the highlighted fields." (some value is wrong) are two different messages by design; `#/receipts/<id>` on a monthly-rental workspace stays the round 25 ruling (the page gives the toast "That page isn't available for your role or workspace." in the toast area, which the lane's script did not read); add-ons with the same name (two archived rows are seed data; carried); the English "Loading BalayHub..." line before a Tagalog guest page (the round 6 carry); Escape on "Check the amount" closing the form once (not reproducible; a watch item).
>
> *Why:* money must never be taken twice for one press or one intent, a message must be true at the moment it is read, what a person typed should survive a step they did not mean as "discard", and a keyboard user should never be left on nothing.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 26 re-check):* every fix held in the browser except Back from a `?new=1` dialog, which was fixed before the commit (the router's last address now follows a page's own address rewrite) together with the security review's performance note (the tab-row watcher reacts only to tab-row changes). Group C ran 11 payment races (stale against fresh, two fresh tabs, a free coupon against a card, card first) and each left exactly one paid plan row. Found at the gate and carried to round 27 as its top item: expense receipts are stored but never linked to their expense, so owner statements show no Receipt link (older than this round).

> **D-109 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 27 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-seventh QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). Two carry fixes landed before the round began: expense receipts are now linked to their expense when uploaded, and migration 040 links the ones already stored, so owner statements and the owner portal show the Receipt link; and the quote page's empty email says "Email is required.", while a payment refused because another one just finished says "That payment was just received. Check your plan before paying again." The lanes filed 12 findings: no severe, 5 significant as filed and 7 minor. The group guest name is ruled minor once fixed, so 4 significant and 8 minor as ruled. Eight real causes were fixed in eight code lanes, plus a pins lane (the old source-text checks the changes broke) and a docs lane. Two findings were ruled **not product bugs**: the two Filters "jumps" on Rent > Payments and Reports (the test pressed a button hidden under the 64 px top bar). Two were ruled works as decided and go to the founder or are folded into a question (Calendar Back; the three "no channel fee" lines). Inbox and phone were clean, and every round 26 fix held. The round is classed hard: the Back-to-the-list finding sits in the shared router, so it touches every page, and the "all problems in one save" class came back on Return deposit (with the one-rule class on a repair cost). Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-97 (Back keeps your place), D-99 S1 (a typed name joins a saved guest with the same email and first name), and D-103 to D-108. Migration 040 came with the carry fix; this round adds no migration.
>
> - **Back keeps your place, even after a link to the page already on screen.** The browser forgets which page entry is which when a link or an address points at the page on screen. The app now puts the place back, so Back and the "‹" links return to the same place on every page. *Why:* opening a reservation from its date link, then "‹ Reservations", went to the top of the list. (Extends D-97 and D-108 (d3).)
> - **Every screen that saves an expense asks "Check the amount" above ₱50,000,** including a repair cost added on a maintenance request. The rule lives in one place (the Rent and Expenses screen code) and every expense screen uses it.
> - **A group booking that joins a saved guest says so,** with the same sentence as a single booking: "Group booking created. Saved under the existing guest {name} (same email and first name)." (D-99 S1.) Keeping the typed name instead is still the open founder question.
> - **One outage sentence on Confirm your card,** also when an earlier ₱20 attempt is still open: "We couldn't reach the payment service. Nothing was charged. Please try again in a moment." (Completes D-107 CARD-3.)
> - **Daily close names the day it closed:** "Oct 17, 2026 was closed on Oct 20, 2026, 7:51 PM by {name}…" (the day first, then when and who).
> - **An empty receipt or attachment is refused before upload on the expense and payment pages too,** with "This file is empty. Choose the receipt file again." (Extends D-108.) *Wording superseded 2026-10-21 by D-110: "This file is empty. Choose the file again." on every control.*
> - **Toasts are read once.** The toast area is the one live region; a toast carries no role of its own. Error toasts keep their red style and are read politely with the others. A form's own error summary keeps its `role="alert"`. *(Supersedes the role on error toasts in D-95; see the note there.)*
> - **Return deposit reports a missing reason before it asks about early return,** and marks Reason as required while the deduction is above 0 (extends D-105 (a), D-106 and D-107).
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the two Filters "jumps" on Rent > Payments and Reports (the test pressed a button hidden under the top bar; the page does not move when Filters is pressed where it can be seen, which the re-verify confirms in group A.3); the three "no channel fee" lines stay one per channel (each link opens exactly the bookings it counts; folded into the small-host question, BLOCKERS round 27 (d)); Back from a window opened by a button leaves the page everywhere, Calendar included (asked in BLOCKERS round 27 (a)); the group merge by email and first name is D-99 S1; the verify link's second visit, the gated My account sections, the demo house rules text, an inverted calendar block that the server refuses (D-107), and the deposit return method starting on the method the deposit was taken by are as decided or carried.
> - *Why:* a person who goes back expects to land where they were, one money rule should read the same everywhere it applies, and a message should be true and said once, for sighted and screen-reader users alike.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 27 re-check):* every fix held in the browser; Back returned to the saved place on all 60 measured tries on paths that open the reservation. The security review's notes were fixed before the commit: an assigned staff member's upload fills an expense's receipt link only when it is empty (owners and managers still replace it), a voided expense is never linked, the file and its link save together, and migration 040 matches receipts within each workspace. Left as is: the general PayMongo network message (also used for refunds and payouts, where "Nothing was charged" would not always be true). Found at the gate and carried to round 28 as its top item: on a phone, tapping a reservation card's guest name jumps to the end of the list and opens nothing (older than this round).

> **D-110 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 28 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-eighth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). One carry fix landed before the round began (PHONE-TAP, below). The lanes filed 12 findings: no severe, 5 significant and 7 minor, and all 12 are real. They come down to 6 root causes in 9 files, fixed in 9 code lanes plus a pins lane (the old source-text checks the changes broke) and a docs lane. Card, longterm and onboarding were clean. Onboarding's one "We couldn't reach the payment service" came from another test lane switching the fake payment service off for 4.5 seconds (the timestamps show it), not from a product bug. Every round 27 fix held where it was tested, except the empty-file rule: D-109 had scoped it to two pages, and four more upload controls lacked it. The round is classed hard: Back keeping your place came back in the shared router, on guest pages, the owner portal and after the sidebar link; the empty-file message was missing on four more controls; and focus could hide under the sticky top bar (WCAG 2.2 2.4.11), a new class. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-97 (Back keeps your place), D-99 O4 (a payment on an Airbnb booking starts on Cash), and D-105 to D-109. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **A phone tap on a reservation card's guest name opens the reservation (PHONE-TAP, landed before the round).** The bottom-bar focus guard acts only on keyboard focus, so a tap is left to the page. When it does scroll a box, it stops at the same line as the browser's own focus scroll, never past the top bar. *Why:* on a phone, a tap on a card's guest name jumped the list to its end and opened nothing (found at the round 27 gate).
> - **Back keeps your place on guest pages and the owner portal too, and after a link that opens a new view of the page already showing** (the sidebar Reservations link). *Why:* a guest who scrolled the booking home and opened a listing came back to the top, and so did a host who used the sidebar link before opening a booking. (Extends D-97 and D-109.)
> - **Only the page on screen can change the address.** A page that finishes loading after you moved on no longer writes its own address over the one you are on. *Why:* a slow Calendar rewrote the address to Calendar while Inbox was showing, so a refresh opened the wrong page.
> - **Keyboard focus is never hidden under the top bar** (WCAG 2.2 2.4.11): the page keeps a focused control clear of the top bar at every width, as it already did for the phone bottom bar. A table box is a Tab stop only while it really scrolls sideways; the stacked phone cards are not. *Why:* on a phone the reservations list box was a Tab stop nobody could see whole, and on Help at 1280 a control sat fully under the bar.
> - **One sentence for an empty file, everywhere: "This file is empty. Choose the file again."** It is said before anything is sent on every upload control (deposit damage photo, housekeeping photos, maintenance attachment, Help screenshot and reply, tenant documents, expense and payment receipts), and the server says the same if an empty file ever reaches it. Forms that save a record and then attach a file (Contact support, Report damage, Found an item, Inspect cleaning) check the file first, so nothing is saved. Housekeeping says it to cleaners in Taglish. *Supersedes the wording in D-108 and D-109 ("Choose the receipt file again."), which was wrong on a photo or screenshot.*
> - **Group booking room money boxes, Bulk change by %, Supplies used:** each problem is said on its own box before anything is sent. A room's Accommodation total and Cleaning fee are checked with the server's money rule (a value such as 2125.005 is refused on its own box, and Cleaning fee has a box of its own). Bulk change by % checks the range -90 to 300, whole numbers and "not 0". Supplies used with every quantity at 0 puts its message on the first quantity row and focuses it.
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* onboarding's one "couldn't reach" (above); a sidebar link to the page already open keeps the scroll while it redraws (D-97; focus goes to the heading, D-105); an Airbnb booking's balance takes the channel fee off (D-85) and its payment starts on Cash (D-99 O4); host message templates stay one body (D-09; for information in BLOCKERS round 28 (a)); the gated My account sections (round 27); the English "Loading BalayHub…" before a Tagalog guest page (round 6 carry); watches closed this round: Escape on "Check the amount", the coupon line " Remove" with no code, and the room price "2125.005000".
>
> *Why:* going back should land where you were on every page a guest or host sees, the address should always name the page on screen, a keyboard user must always see where they are, and a message should be true for the control it is said on.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 28 re-check):* every fix held in the browser: Back returns to the saved place on guest pages and after the sidebar link, a page that loads late no longer rewrites the address, nothing the keyboard reaches sits under the top bar, and every upload control refuses an empty file with the one sentence (in Taglish for cleaners). One older gap found at the gate was fixed before the commit: in a group booking, "Existing guest" with nobody picked now marks the guest search box with its sentence instead of an empty "Please check the highlighted fields." Security PASS with no findings. Carried to round 29: a brief top-of-list frame when a phone tap opens a reservation far down the list, and six card checks the gate could not set up.

> **D-111 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 29 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The twenty-ninth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). One carry fix landed before the round began (GUEST-EMPTY, below). The lanes filed 9 findings: no severe, 1 significant and 8 minor. 6 are real bugs, with 7 causes in 8 files, fixed in 7 code lanes plus a pins lane (the old source-text checks the changes broke) and a docs lane. 3 need no code: the admin's Skip card check leaving its ₱20 row pending is how it was built; Back from a `?new=1` window opened from another page is the open founder question in BLOCKERS round 27 (a); and the Gate pass button on Listings is a founder question (BLOCKERS round 29 (b)). Guest and longterm were clean. Every round 28 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the "every problem in one save" class came back on discount codes; the focus guard left focus on the page body after an upload's redraw (one cause, covering every upload-on-pick control); and the card check's limit touched the card gate, where the SEV2 sits. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-97 (Back keeps your place), D-101 (the in-progress wait names the real time), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), and D-106 to D-110. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **GUEST-EMPTY (landed before the round): the guest check-in ID photo, the direct-booking photo and the guest payment screenshot refuse an empty file with the one sentence, and so does the host's listing photo.** The guest pages say it in Taglish ("Walang laman ang file. Piliin ulit ang file."), and the page refuses before anything is sent. The server answers an empty upload with 400 `EMPTY_FILE` after the access checks, so a person who cannot reach the record still gets the access answer. The host's listing photo refuses in English, as host screens are (D-09). *Why:* D-110's empty-file rule did not reach these routes; the guest check-in photo has its own route (a round 28 watch item).
> - **The card check says how long to wait, and a press that failed because PayMongo couldn't be reached doesn't count.** After too many tries the page says "Too many tries. Please try again in N minutes." (or "in about an hour", or "in about N hours"), from the server's own wait: every 429 now carries that wait as `retry_after`. Paid, declined and refused presses still count. *Why:* an owner was told "a few minutes" and was locked out for an hour; the declined-card limit lasts a day. The limits themselves (10 starts an hour, 8 declined cards a day) are unchanged and are asked in BLOCKERS round 29 (a).
> - **Focus is never left on the page body after an upload redraws its card.** A file dropped onto an upload control (or set by assistive technology) is remembered the way a clicked control is, so the control keeps focus after its card is redrawn. File inputs only: a text box is not remembered on change, so a redraw cannot pull focus back into it. *Why:* on the deposit's Add damage photo, a valid PNG dropped onto the control left focus on the page body after the photo was added. (Extends D-89 and D-105.)
> - **Discount codes report both problems in one save:** with Percent off and the amount both filled, and Percent off wrong on its own (150, 2.5 or 0), the range message stays on Percent off and "Choose one: a percent off or an amount off, not both." shows on the amount box. A whole percent from 1 to 90 keeps the not-both sentence on Percent off, as before. (Extends D-105 (a) and D-110 (a).)
> - **Contact support checks the screenshot's content before the ticket is created:** a file whose first bytes are not those of its type (PDF, PNG, JPEG or WEBP) is refused on the Screenshot field ("This file isn't a real PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP. Choose the file again."), and no ticket is created. The server still checks the whole file. *Extends D-110's rule that a form which saves a record and then attaches a file checks the file first, to the file's content.*
> - **Wording:** a season overlap names the other season once ("Overlaps A, which wins the shared nights."); Daily close says "That date isn't valid, so this shows today."
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the admin's Skip card check leaves the last ₱20 check row pending on purpose (a check that finishes at the bank still saves the card; the reconcile job in `src/lib/cardcheck.js` expires any check still pending after 24 hours; nothing reads the row); Back from a `?new=1` window opened by a link from another page closes the window and returns to that page, because the link was one step (BLOCKERS round 27 (a) covers it); the Gate pass button on Listings stays (founder question, BLOCKERS round 29 (b)); the blank frame of about 50 to 100 ms before a phone row opens is a page swap (closed); the housekeeping photo forms' "Saved, but the photo did not upload" for a renamed file (watch).
> *Why:* a person who is told to wait should be told how long, a keyboard or screen-reader user should never be left on the page body, and a form should say every problem once, before it saves anything.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 29 re-check):* every fix held in the browser. The security review's low note was fixed before the commit: because a start that failed at PayMongo is now given back, a second limit of 30 card-check starts an hour per workspace counts every press, so an outage can never make starts unlimited. A new test was found wiping the local dev database (it loaded app code before the test helper chose the test database); it was fixed before the commit and the full suite was re-run with the dev database checked untouched. Ruled not a bug: Back to a list shows its top for 1 to 2 frames (under 40 ms) before the saved place returns, like the page swap.

> **D-112 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 30 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirtieth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). One carry fix landed before the round began (RATE-COPY, below). The lanes filed 8 findings, all minor, and one more was found by reading the code while checking another (the guest payment proof form). All 9 are real, with 6 causes in 9 files, fixed in 9 code lanes plus a pins lane (the old source-text checks the changes broke) and a docs lane. Bookings, card, money and onboarding were clean, and every round 29 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: forms that save a record and then attach a file were still saving before the file's content was checked (housekeeping, long-term expenses and payments, and the guest proof; the Help reply said it in a toast after uploading), the fourth round running for the files class; and the guest proof form left focus on its button and said a file problem only after the amount was fixed. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), and D-106 to D-111. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **Every "too many tries" answer says how long to wait (RATE-COPY, landed before the round).** Every limited answer from the server (sign-in, sign-up, password, email links, quotes, the owner portal, the guest pay pages, the card check) now reads "Too many tries. Please try again in N minutes.", "…in about an hour." or "…in about N hours.", from the same wait it sends as `retry_after`. Tagalog guest and sign-in pages get it in Taglish ("Masyadong maraming subok. Subukan ulit pagkalipas ng N minuto."). *Why:* round 29 fixed the card page's sentence, but API callers and every other limited page still said "a few minutes" when the wait could be 15 minutes or an hour. *Closes the round 29 carry for the 429 body and the other limiters' copy.*
> - **A file is checked by its first bytes before anything is sent or saved, on every upload.** A file that is not really the PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP it claims to be is refused with "This file isn't a real PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP. Choose the file again." (Taglish for cleaners and on guest pages: "Hindi totoong PDF, JPG, PNG o WEBP ang file na ito. Piliin ulit ang file."), before any upload request. The forms that save a record first (Report damage, Found an item, Inspect cleaning, Add expense, Record payment, Contact support, the guest payment proof) say it in the same save as their other problems, before anything is saved. The server still checks the whole file. *Why:* a text file renamed `.png` saved a damage report and an expense without their photo or receipt, and Add photo blamed the file's type or size. *Extends D-110 and D-111.* The empty-file sentence and every type and size sentence are unchanged.
> - **A file refused on a ticket reply is said on the Attach a file box,** not in a toast, with focus on the box; the reply text stays.
> - **The guest payment proof form says every problem in one press and puts focus on the first:** the amount and the screenshot are checked together, the screenshot's message sits on its own box, and focus goes to the first wrong box. After a screenshot is refused once the proof was saved, focus goes to the screenshot box. "Add selected to my bill" with nothing chosen puts focus on the first extra.
> - **Wording and small fixes:** an empty internal note says "Write a note first."; Daily close removes its "That date isn't valid" warning when the date box is emptied, and a box left empty shows the page's day again; the phone search box is 48 px tall (the 44 px phone rule).
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the Refund and nightly-rate questions focus their first button (a safe default); Help's empty search shows its empty-state title and line; the onboarding helper read no card field messages (a harness lookup; the re-verify reads those lines by id); the guest check-in ID photo and the direct-booking photo do not yet run the first-bytes check (watch); closed watches: the list top for 1 to 3 frames before a Back restore, Add expense's sentence shown twice, the skip link at 390.
> *Why:* a form should refuse a bad file before it saves anything and say so on the file's own box, a guest should land on the thing to fix, and a person told to wait should be told how long.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 30 re-check): groups A, B and C re-checked in the browser. One fail, A.3: Daily close went to a new page while the year was still being typed (e.g. 0002), redrew and lost the typing. Late fix DAILYCLOSE2: the date box moves only on a complete real date from 2000 to 2100; a complete out-of-range date still gets "That date isn't valid, so this shows today." on blur or Enter. Confirmed in the browser. C.1b (the login page's 429 in English for a Taglish guest) is not a bug: host screens are English (D-09), and the Taglish 429 covers guest pages and pages that use tServer. Security PASS. Full suite 3870/3870.*

> **D-113 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 31 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-first QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 9 findings: no severe, 2 significant (Save note lost its first press while the @ list was open; the whole app was one live region for screen readers) and 7 minor. Five more were found by reading the code and the lanes' notes while checking, and one carried item was fixed. All are real, with 11 causes in 16 files, fixed in 14 code lanes plus a pins lane (the old source-text checks the changes broke) and a docs lane. Card, money and ops were clean, and every round 30 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: D-112's "every upload" did not reach the listing photos or the guest check-in ID photo, and stand-alone upload controls still said a refused file only in a toast (the fifth round running for the files class); and a new class, pickers that start on a record nobody chose, moved a booking on one untouched press. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), D-109 (toasts are read once through `#toasts`), and D-110 to D-112. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **Every upload control says a refused file on its own line under the control.** It is marked invalid and keeps focus; the next pick clears the line. This replaces the toast for a refused file on Add photo (housekeeping), Upload receipt and Upload attachment (rent), Add damage photo (deposits), Upload photo or document (maintenance) and Listing photos. Other failures keep their toast. *Why:* a message in a toast is gone in seconds and is not where the person is looking; the forms already said it on the box (D-112). *Extends D-112's Help reply and guest proof rule to every upload control.*
> - **The first-bytes check reaches the uploads that post raw files:** Listing photos, the guest check-in ID photo (in the form's one pass, before the answers are saved, in Taglish on Tagalog pages) and the guest pay page's "Add the screenshot". A check-in photo the server still refuses after the answers were saved is said on the ID photo box with focus there. *Why:* a text file renamed `.png` reached the server from these three because they bypass `uploadFile`; the check-in photo's re-encode falls back to the original when it cannot decode. *Supersedes the round 30 watch on the check-in ID photo and the direct-booking (listing) photo.*
> - **A picker that names a record starts on "Select…":** Move to another listing, and a manager's Add a season and Add a pricing rule. An untouched save says "Move to is required." or "Listing is required." with focus on the box. An owner's pricing pickers still start on "All listings". *Why:* one untouched press moved a booking to a listing nobody chose. *Extends round 14's rule on money methods to any record.*
> - **Save note works on the first press while the @ list is open,** and a note with a typed but unpicked @name says "Note saved. Nobody was notified: pick a name from the @ list to mention someone." The list still sits under the box and never covers the tabs (round 17 O10). *Why:* the list closed under the pointer, so the first press landed on empty space.
> - **The app is not one big live region:** only `#toasts` and small status areas (filter chips, search status, the assistant list, help suggestions) are live; a page change is said by focus on its heading, including the "Can't reach BalayHub" page. The `#app` attribute came with the first build and was never a decision. *Larger page-section live regions are a round 32 audit.*
> - **Dialog actions stay in view:** in every dialog Save and Cancel stick to the bottom of the scrolling body, and a focused field scrolls clear of them.
> - **The card check says its longest wait first:** while the day's card tries are used up, the start press answers with that wait ("about N hours") and starts nothing, instead of "about an hour" from the hourly limit. *Extends D-112.*
> - **Wording:** an empty saved view names the view ("No check-ins today", "Choose another saved view to see your other reservations."); "Nothing here yet" stays for All reservations.
> *Not bugs / not changed:* "Select…" is the app's one placeholder (the "Choose a listing…" copy carry is closed); the reservation money dialogs (Record payment, Record refund and Return deposit) keep no draft after Escape (D-106); a malformed file-name header now gives 415, not 500 (closed); add-on duplicate names are refused (closed); Mara's P&L refund sits under the right listing (closed); the phone carries for the Modules select, stacked labels, skip link, late-load address check and Cleaner pay table no longer reproduce (closed).
> *Why:* a refused file should be said where the person is looking, a press should never be lost, a screen reader should hear what changed and not the whole page, and an action should never apply to a record nobody chose.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 31 re-check): groups A, B and C re-checked in the browser. Every round 31 fix held (Save note on the first press; #app no longer a live region; dialog Save and Cancel in view; the upload line under every upload control; Listing photos, the check-in ID photo and the guest screenshot checked by first bytes; Move to and the manager's pickers start on "Select…"; the saved-view empty titles; the card check's day wait said first, with no payment started). Re-verify A found an older check-in banner bug: a refused replacement photo on a submitted check-in said "Add a photo of your ID to finish registering", and on a draft page the same note showed twice. Late fixes CHECKIN2 and CHECKIN3: the note shows only on a first save with no saved record; a draft page keeps its top banner only; a submitted page shows none. Browser-confirmed. Ruled not a bug: an unfinished 3-D Secure return shows the waiting copy, as card-on-file.md says for a still-pending check. Watch, carried: after a reload the card page shows an empty form instead of the refusal (nothing charged; the next press says the same wait). Security PASS. Full suite 3929/3929.*

> **D-114 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 32 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-second QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 12 findings: no severe, 5 significant as filed and 7 minor. Two of the significant ones are ruled minor (the New reservation price line, which is wording, and the support screenshot's name, which still starts with its visible word), so 3 stay significant as ruled: the selected saved view unreadable in dark mode, a refused lease document not announced, and Supplies, Cleaner pay and Lost and found reading their whole list on every save. Three more were found by reading the code (five more dark-mode text colours, the lease document box's name, and Pricing's two whole-list live regions), and the round 32 audit of page-section live regions is closed. 10 of the 12 filed findings are real; the Help placeholder is decided wording (D-101), and the staff phone bar is a doc line. The real findings come down to 10 causes in 13 files, fixed in 13 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane. Money and card were clean, and every round 31 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the upload line missed the lease documents box (the sixth round running for the files class), whole-list live regions were found in six files, and two new classes came in with two or more items each (dark-mode text on a tinted fill; a control named without its visible label). Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-76 (staff may view pricing for their assigned properties), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-101 (the Help Ask placeholders), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), D-109 (toasts are read once through `#toasts`), and D-110 to D-113. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **A box that holds a whole list, table, grid or conversation is never a live region.** Removed from Supplies (the list and an item's history), Cleaner pay (the owner's table and the cleaner's My pay), Lost and found, Profit and loss, the inbox conversation, and Pricing's price grid; the bulk-change preview keeps only its summary line live. My pay's month name is now the live line. What changed is said by the toast, by focus on the page heading, by the pressed tab or filter chip, or by a short status line. *Why:* a screen reader heard the whole table again after every save. *Closes the round 32 audit D-113 set.*
> - **The lease documents box says a refused file on its own line,** marked invalid, with focus on the file box, like every other upload control; "Choose a file first." says it there too. Other failures show under the box and are read out. *Why:* D-113's "every upload control" missed it.
> - **Text on a tinted fill uses the ink colour.** The selected saved view, the Direct badge, the selected @ name, listing tags and the owner portal's feed kind label use `--teal-ink`; the owner portal's current tab uses `--accent`. `--teal-2` and `--forest` are fill and border colours only. *Why:* in dark mode they stay dark green, and the selected saved view read at 1.84:1.
> - **A control's name includes its visible label.** The support screenshot box and the lease document box are named by their labels ("Screenshot (optional)", "Upload signed lease or tenant document"). `aria-label` stays only where nothing visible names the control. *Why:* WCAG 2.5.3; a voice user says the words they see.
> - **The tenant's Monthly rent charges table is full width** under the two columns, so all five columns fit at 1280.
> - **Wording:** the New reservation price line starts "From your rates:" (the Accommodation total box is what saves, and "Use ₱X from your rates" offers the difference); "What happened is required."; a mention of someone not on the team says "That person can't be mentioned. Pick a name from the @ list."; the guest's "Choose at least one extra." clears when an extra is picked; a notification save with no property picked puts focus on the first property.
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the Help Ask placeholders "e.g. Paano mag-record?" and "e.g. I-connect Airbnb?" stay as decided (D-101); the staff phone bottom bar stays as built, with Help in More (APP-FLOW 28.7 names the short labels); staff open Pricing read-only (D-76; the APP-FLOW section 14 line is corrected); the 502 on Reyes Staycations' booking page is a placeholder key in the local copy, not a product state; a coupon the workspace already holds keeps its months after it is switched off (by design); while a payment waits, "Pay another way" shows the waiting line with its minutes, not the server's sentence (closed); the card carries for the coupon before one-tap, the outage presses and "Set up my workspace" held (closed).
> *Why:* a screen reader should hear what changed and not the whole list, every upload control should behave the same, text must be readable in dark mode, and a control should answer to the words on screen.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 32 re-check): groups A, B and C re-checked in the browser. Every round 32 fix held: dark-mode text on tinted fills (saved view, Direct badge, @ list, tag chip) passes contrast with axe clean; the support screenshot and the lease document are named by their visible labels; the notifications refusal focuses the first property; no whole list or table is a live region (supplies, cleaner pay, lost and found, P&L, pricing grid and bulk table, inbox thread); the lease upload says a refused file on its line with focus; the rent charges card is full width; New reservation says "From your rates:"; the guest extras message clears on a pick; "What happened is required."; a mention of someone off the team says to pick from the @ list; staff see Pricing read only and Help under More. Group C: a switched-off coupon refuses one-tap pay (400 COUPON_INVALID, nothing charged), and a direct booking reaches the fake checkout with a key saved through Settings. Not run: the owner portal tab (needs a sign-in email) and "PayMongo says:" (harness). Security PASS. Full suite 3974/3974.*

> **D-115 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 33 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-third QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 7 findings: no severe, 1 significant (the deposit damage photo box was named "Add damage photo" instead of its visible label "Damage / condition photos") and 6 minor, and all 7 are real. Three more were found by reading the code while checking: the inbox's Send later box had the same wrong name; housekeeping's Set pay and Edit pay closed onto the page heading; and the other nine reason dialogs dropped the typed reason when the server refused. Two items the lanes flagged for a decision are real too: the assistant chat was a whole conversation in a live region, and a free coupon activation let a refused payment say "just received". That makes 12 real items, with 9 causes in 18 files, fixed in 17 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane. Card, inbox, longterm and phone were clean, and every round 32 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the visible-label rule was missed on two controls the round after it was set; ten reason dialogs share one cause (the dialog closed before the server answered); two "keep focus" promises have been broken since round 26 by the router's dialog sweep; and D-114's own list of live areas named the assistant chat against its rule. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; the router owns page changes), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), D-109 (toasts are read once through `#toasts`), and D-110 to D-114. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **A reason dialog keeps the typed reason when the server refuses.** Cancel reservation, Void payment (on a stay and on long-term rent), Void expense, Void payout, Void withdrawal, Remove room, Cancel group, Void refund and Void charge send their request from inside the dialog. A refusal is said under the reason box, the reason stays and focus stays on the button; only success closes the dialog. *Why:* the dialog closed before the server answered, so a refused void lost the reason the host had typed.
> - **"Void the refund first" names the refund.** With one live refund: "Void the ₱800.00 refund from Oct 21, 2026 first, then this payment." With several: "Void a refund first, then this payment. Refunds on this booking: ₱100.00 from Oct 22, 2026 and ₱800.00 from Oct 21, 2026.", newest first. *Why:* a small host had to hunt for the row.
> - **A control's name includes its visible label (audit closed).** The deposit damage photo box is named "Damage / condition photos" and the inbox's Send later box "Send later (Philippine time, optional)". Every file input and every control with an `aria-label` was checked against its visible text; these two were the only misses. Where nothing visible names a control, its `aria-label` stays. *Why:* D-114's rule missed two controls.
> - **Close the day / Update close and Set pay / Edit pay keep focus on their button after the dialog saves.** The page redraws after the dialog has closed, so focus goes back to the button or its replacement. Other dialog saves still close onto the page heading; round 34 audits them. *Why:* since round 26 the router put focus on the heading whenever a page redrew while a dialog still held focus.
> - **The assistant chat is not a live region.** Only its newest answer, or "Couldn't answer. Try again or contact support.", is read out, from a hidden line beside the chat. *Corrects D-114's list of live areas, which named "the assistant list".* *Why:* the whole chat, up to 40 messages, was read again after every answer.
> - **The booking page's discount code box is marked when a code is refused.** The box is marked invalid and points at its message; typing in the box clears both.
> - **Wording:** Home's daily tiles show "0 Arrivals" and "0 Departures" when nothing is due, not "0/0"; a discount code suggestion keeps only letters, numbers and dashes ("Try BAD-CODE."), or says "Use 3–20 letters, numbers or dashes, e.g. BALIK10." when nothing usable is left; a refused one-tap payment says "just received" only when a real payment finished, never after a free coupon activation.
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the help suggestion box stays a small live list; the guest proof is refused on Send proof, not on pick (round 32); the 502 on Reyes Staycations' checkout is the local placeholder key (D-114); top search not covering inbox messages and a mid-month lease's first due date are founder questions (BLOCKERS round 33 (a) and (b)), kept as built until answered.
> *Why:* a refusal should never cost what the person typed, a control should answer to the words on screen, focus should stay where the person was working, and a screen reader should hear what changed, not the whole chat.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 33 re-check): groups A, B and C re-checked in the browser. Every round 33 fix held: the deposit damage photo and the Send later box are named by their visible labels; every reason dialog keeps the typed reason and shows the server's refusal inside it (a real refused void named "Void the ₱100.00 refund from Oct 21, 2026 first, then this payment."), and a refusal after the dialog closed comes as a toast; Close the day lands on Update close and Set pay on its button; the assistant has one short live line; the booking form marks a wrong discount code on its box (English and Taglish); the code suggestion keeps only letters, numbers and dashes; Home shows "0 Arrivals" on an empty day; after a free coupon a second one-tap says "A payment is already in progress", never "just received". "PayMongo says:" was run and shows. Carried to round 34: the coupon box still says "applied" after a pay refusal (filed), and a refused card number leaves a pending card-check row (decide). Security PASS. Full suite 4024/4024.*

> **D-116 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 34 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-fourth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 7 findings: 1 severe (a workspace scheduled for deletion still took two plan payments, and the deletion stayed scheduled), 1 significant (opening a conversation with the keyboard put focus on the Inbox heading, 25 Tab presses from the reply box) and 5 minor, and all 7 are real. One more was filed at the round 33 gate (the coupon box still said "applied" after a pay refusal). Four more were found by reading the code (the card check could start on a workspace being deleted; Plan & billing called a deletion "overdue"; six more file boxes were named only by a hidden label; Record deposit closed onto the heading, against APP-FLOW section 26's focus rule), and two items the lanes flagged are real (the dialog-save audit, and Settings in a gated owner's menu leading nowhere). 11 causes in 15 files, fixed in 14 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane. Bookings, longterm, money, ops and phone were clean, and every round 33 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the severe bug takes money on a state boundary; the visible-label class came back for the third round running; focus after opening a conversation and after a dialog save broke written rules; and D-108's dialog rule conflicted with D-89. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-103, D-105, D-108, D-109, D-110 and D-111 to D-115. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **A workspace scheduled for deletion takes no plan payment and no card check.** *Round 41 (D-123): the admin's Skip card check too.* One-tap pay, checkout, a free coupon activation and the card check are refused with "This workspace is scheduled for deletion, so plan payments are turned off. Cancel the deletion first, then pay." (the card check: "This workspace is scheduled for deletion, so the card check is turned off. Cancel the deletion first."). Plan & billing shows that sentence in place of the plan picker, the Pay buttons and the coupon box. The owner cancels the deletion from the banner at the top of every page, also when BalayHub scheduled it. *Why:* money was taken for a workspace the deletion job was about to remove. A payment already waiting at the bank when the deletion is scheduled is a founder question (BLOCKERS round 34 (a)).
> - **Plan & billing names a scheduled deletion,** not "Your payment is overdue."
> - **A refused coupon leaves no discount behind.** After the server refuses a coupon at pay time, the "applied" line goes, the total returns to the list price, and the next press pays without the code; the code stays in the box, marked, with the message. A refused code is never sent with a payment.
> - **Opening a conversation puts focus on its heading (the guest's name),** at every width. A page may mark the heading of what it just opened; otherwise the page heading takes focus as before. *Why:* `/inbox` and `/inbox/<id>` are one page (D-110), but focus went to "Inbox", 25 Tab presses from the reply box.
> - **A dialog save closes onto the button that opened it.** When a dialog saves and redraws its own page, focus returns to the control that opened it, or its replacement. The page heading takes focus only when that control is gone (after Void or Delete), when the dialog opened with no button (`?new=1`), or when the save opens another page. The More sheet and the top bar's panels still hand focus to the heading. *Corrects D-108's dialog rule, which sent focus to the heading even when the page did not change, against D-89.* *Why:* about twenty dialogs, including Record deposit, closed onto the heading, against APP-FLOW section 26's focus rule (after a save, focus goes back to the control the person used).
> - **Every file box is named by its own visible label (class closed).** Each file box has a label on screen linked to it, and no hidden name: check-in "ID photos" ("Litrato ng ID"), "Listing photos", "Attach a file", "Add photo", "Photo (optional)", "Upload photo or document", "Screenshot or receipt", "Choose a receipt or proof file". A scan test fails if a file box is ever named by a hidden label again. *Supersedes D-115's allowance of a hidden name under a card heading, for file boxes.* *Why:* the third round running for this class.
> - **The Listing photos dialog opens on its file box,** not on ×.
> - **Two controls on one screen never share a name:** the conversation's select is "Conversation assigned to"; the list filter stays "Assigned to".
> - **Phones:** the bottom bar is exactly 64 px tall and the assistant panel sits flush on it (no page text shows between them); the booking page's "Have a discount code?" is 44 px tall.
> - **While the card check is due, the account menu shows My account, Help & support and Log out,** not Settings (it only led back to Confirm your card). *Wording corrected by D-117: on desktop, Help & support is the top bar's help button, not a menu item; on phones it is in the account menu.*
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the 3-D Secure Fail sentence on the fake bank page (harness limit; *Round 43: raised again; still a harness limit (BLOCKERS 'Live stage for card on file').*); no "Check the amount" on a maintenance estimate (an estimate is not money spent; Add cost asks, APP-FLOW 30.6); Daily close's out-of-range date shows today with its warning (APP-FLOW section 42); the Reports date boxes' names (they exist); Remove block with no question; the 13 px empty alert line (as built); a refused card number's pending ₱20 card-check row (never charged, never listed, retired on the next start; D-111 family); the floating Ask button over a row (round 18), with a measured focus check carried.
> *Why:* money must never be taken for a workspace being deleted, a refusal must not leave a discount on screen, focus should land where the person was working or on what they opened, and every file box should answer to the words on screen.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 34 re-check): full suite 4081 of 4081 pass. Browser re-verify (groups A, B and C) pass, no new finding. Security PASS; its Low card-check item was fixed in the round (`src/lib/cardcheck.js`), its other Low item is BLOCKERS round 34 (a).*

> **D-117 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 35 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-fifth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 8 findings (two more reports were the same two found twice): 2 significant (in dark mode the assistant's header line was too faint to read, 4.3:1; Reports' Download CSV stayed live for a range over two years and downloaded nothing) and 6 minor, and all 8 are real.
