Three more were found by reading the code (the booking page's Connect PayMongo and Turn on landed on the heading; four more phone toggles were under 44 px; the card page's hourly wait had the same empty-form gap), and two items the lanes flagged are real (focus after cancelling a scheduled message went to "Inbox" instead of the conversation; Add charge's pay-link question could open with no button behind it). 10 causes in 13 files, fixed in 9 code lanes plus a pins lane and a docs lane. Bookings, guest, inbox and longterm were clean, and every round 34 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: focus after a save broke for the third round running (five files), the 44 px toggle rule and the dark-mode contrast rule each came back, and D-116 had no rule for a replacement that cannot be used. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-103, D-105, D-109, D-110 and D-111 to D-116. No migration, no new env var, no schema change.
>
> - **A replacement takes focus, even when it can't be used yet.** After a save, focus goes to the control that takes the place of the one used, found by the same focus key even when its words change (Mark 1 paid to Mark paid, Reopen day to Close the day, Connect PayMongo to Turn on, Turn on to Turn off). When that control can't be used right now, it stays focusable: it is dimmed and announced as unavailable (`aria-disabled`), and pressing it does nothing. The page heading takes focus only when nothing takes the place. *Extends D-116.* *Why:* Cleaner pay, Daily close and the booking page sent focus to the heading with the replacement on screen, the third round running for this class.
> - **The page's own focus heading is used everywhere.** When nothing else can take focus, the open conversation's heading (the guest's name) takes it, not "Inbox", for the router, the focus guard and a dialog with no opener alike. *Why:* cancelling a scheduled message left the person 11 Tab presses from the reply box.
> - **A question after a save opens from the button that was used.** Add charge closes its dialog before the page redraws, so "Send the guest a pay link?" returns focus to Add charge, whatever the answer. *Why:* the order made the result depend on timing (heading one run, Add charge the next).
> - **A question about what is being saved stays over its form** (Refund leaves money owed?, Pay more than the statement?), so Cancel goes back to the form with everything typed. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Dark mode: the assistant header's small line is full white** (5.3:1 on the dark green). Text on a fill is never made lighter with opacity. *Why:* it was 4.3:1, under the 4.5:1 rule (D-114).
> - **Every tappable toggle is 44 px on a phone:** the landing FAQ, Help articles (also in the assistant), "Show the accepted text" and "Automatic welcome codes".
> - **Reports: Download CSV is off for a range over two years,** with "Choose up to two years to export.", the export's own limit, so a click never leads to an empty download.
> - **Confirm your card says the wait when it opens.** When the day's or the hour's card tries are used up, the page shows "Too many tries. Please try again in about 24 hours." (or the hour's wait) above the form on load; Pay still answers the same and starts nothing.
> - **Calendar links: the refusal names the reason.** An IP address: "This link is an IP address, not a channel's website, so BalayHub can't read it. Copy the calendar (iCal) link from the channel." A port or a password: "BalayHub can't read links with a port number or a password in them. Copy the calendar (iCal) link exactly as the channel shows it." Inside the Channels dialog a failed sync ends "Check the link above, then press Sync now."; alerts and Daily close keep "Check the link on the listing's Channels page."
> - **An owner whose card is not confirmed and whose workspace is scheduled for deletion sees the deletion state first** (Home with **Cancel deletion**), not Confirm your card: no money can move while a deletion is scheduled (D-116), so the card page would only refuse. After the deletion is cancelled, the card page comes back.
> - **Wording correction to D-116:** on desktop the gated account menu shows My account and Log out; Help & support is the top bar's help button. On phones the account menu also holds Help & support.
>
> *Not bugs / not changed:* the Taglish booking page's discount box named "Discount code" (the Taglish dictionary keeps the noun, as "May discount code ka?" does); the phone @ list not showing in one run (the script typed in the guest message box, where there is no list); a control partly under the floating Ask button (WCAG 2.4.11 is met: none is fully hidden); both custom date boxes marked before anything is typed on Reports (the status line says why).
> *Why:* focus should land where the person was working, a page should say what it already knows, and text and toggles should meet the house rules in both themes and on phones.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 35 gate): full suite 4114 of 4114 pass. Browser re-verify (groups A, B and C) pass, no new finding. Security PASS. Suite runtime 11.8 min. BLOCKERS round 35 (a) is open, for information.*

> **D-118 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 36 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-sixth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 10 reports; one was a duplicate (the maintenance half of the same problem, filed by two lanes), so 9 findings. 7 are real and minor, and 2 are not bugs (the long-term Reports page has no two-year limit, and the Tenants "top frames" were the tenant page still on screen while the list loaded). Two were filed as significant (focus going to the page heading after saving an edited automatic message or template, and after maintenance status, property Archive and Restore); they are ruled minor, as round 35 ruled the same kind of problem, because focus is never lost. An audit found 13 more buttons in 10 files with the same cause, and one more rule in the nightly price dialog (its one-year limit) that only the server checked. 6 causes in 15 files, fixed in 15 code lanes plus a focus-key test lane, a pins lane and a docs lane. Card and phone were clean, and every round 35 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: focus after a press broke for the fourth round running (eleven files), an export link again ignored its page's rule, and two forms again waited for the server to name a problem. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws), D-97 (Back keeps a list's place), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), D-109 (toasts are read once through `#toasts`) and D-110 to D-117. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change.
>
> - **A button whose words can change keeps a fixed focus key.** When a button's words depend on the record (Archive or Restore, Start work or Back to open, Mark clean or Mark dirty, Send now or Send again, Email or Email again, Reply and reopen or Send reply) or on a value its own dialog can change (the name in "Edit template: …"), it carries a focus key that stays the same. So after the press or the save, focus comes back to that button or to the button that took its place. Buttons that take each other's place share one key; a button repeated per row has the record in its key. A new button that breaks this rule fails a test. *Extends D-89, D-116 and D-117.* *Why:* saving a renamed template or automatic message, every maintenance status button, and Archive and Restore sent focus to the heading with the button still on screen, the fourth round running for this class.
> - **Maintenance: every status button shares one key,** so after Start work focus is on Back to open, after Back to open on Start work, and after Mark resolved or Cancel request on Reopen. *Supersedes the per-status key in D-99's focus table (DESIGN-BRIEF section 26).*
> - **Turn off portal access hands its key to "Invite again"** before the page redraws, so focus lands there.
> - **Every rule the device can know is said in the same press** (D-105). The Channels form names a link's problem (an IP address, a port or a password) together with a missing channel, in the server's own words. The nightly price dialog names a To night before the From night, a range over one year and a price of 0 together. The server keeps the same checks.
> - **Stay reports: Download CSV is off while a typed date is refused,** with "Fix the dates to export.", because the page says that date isn't used. *Why:* the link kept the last good range while the box showed 1900.
> - **Long-term Reports has no range limit, and neither does its export.** The two-year limit belongs to Stay reports, whose report also refuses longer ranges. Every export obeys exactly the rules its own page states. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Booking form: a guest count the place can't take clears the total and turns Continue off at once,** with "This place allows up to {n} guests." in the price summary; the box's own message still comes when the guest leaves the box.
> - **PayMongo key with the right start but other characters:** "A PayMongo key has only letters and numbers after sk_live_ or sk_test_. Copy the Secret key again from your PayMongo dashboard (Developers, API keys)."
> - **Inspect starts with every item ticked Pass, by design:** the inspector unticks what was not done well, and saving with nothing unticked is a passing inspection (D-50 scores it 100). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Two ways to add a listing are both right:** Listings, then Add listing, and the "Add unit" form that opens after saving a property. Both save the same record.
> - **Guest payments are tested on a QA workspace** that saves the fake PayMongo key through Settings (as rounds 32 and 35 did), because the demo workspace has no working key on purpose.
> - **Back to a list: a "top frame" counts only when the list itself is on screen.** While the list loads after Back, the page you came from stays on screen; that wait is not a jump.
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the Name box on the booking form stopping at 120 characters; the proxy refusing PayMongo's hosted checkout address in the test sandbox (nothing is sent); focus going to the heading when the pressed button leaves with its row (Delete, Mark returned, Mark as solved, Void).
> *Why:* focus should come back to where the person was working whatever the button now says, a form should say everything wrong at once, and a page should never show a price or a link that no longer matches what is typed.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 36 gate): full suite 4192 of 4192 pass. Browser re-verify (groups A, B and C) pass after three late fixes (supplies More focus, Mark as solved focus and its read-before-await follow-up). Security PASS. Suite runtime 11.9 min. BLOCKERS round 36 (a) is open, for information.*

> **D-119 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 37 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-seventh QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 7 findings; card, guest, longterm, ops and phone filed none. 4 are real: 2 significant (on Stay reports, a dimmed Download CSV could not be reached with the keyboard and a click on it left focus on the page body; a conversation opened at its oldest message and stayed there after a note was saved) and 2 minor (a report over two years kept October's figures on screen with nothing saying so; a support screenshot that only looked like a PNG was refused after the ticket was created). 3 are not bugs (the filter chip strip appearing under a filter just ticked; the sidebar's last item, Admin, partly shown at rest because the menu scrolls; the two ways to add a listing, already decided in D-118). One more was found by reading (long-term Reports' dimmed Export, the same gap), and one item flagged by the card lane is real (an owner whose workspace is scheduled for deletion started on the Launchpad, against D-117). 5 causes in 5 files, fixed in 5 code lanes plus a test that keeps handlers reading the pressed button before they wait, a test-database guard, a pins lane and a docs lane. Every round 36 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: a dimmed control left the tab order again (two files), a file the server refuses still passed the device before the record was saved (the files class, sixth round), a view again worked on its page before the page was on screen, and two docs disagreed about where a read-only owner lands. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws), D-97 (Back keeps a list's place), D-103 (the first wrong field gets focus), D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure), D-109 (toasts are read once through `#toasts`), and D-110 to D-118. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A dimmed link stays in the tab order.** A link with no address while it can't be used (Download CSV on Stay reports, Export on long-term Reports) is still reached with Tab, keeps focus when clicked, is announced as unavailable with its reason, and does nothing. *Extends D-117 ("unavailable, not gone") to links.* *Why:* the dimmed Download CSV had no place in the tab order, and a click on it left focus on the page body.
> - **Stay reports: a custom range over two years is not sent.** The page shows this month, the status line reads "Choose up to two years. Showing this month for now.", the To box is marked, and Download CSV stays off with "Choose up to two years to export.". *Supersedes the "keeps the last report" line in APP-FLOW 29.11 and the "report itself is unchanged" sentence in 48.2, for this rule.* *Why:* October's figures stayed on screen under a two-year range with nothing saying which dates they were for.
> - **A conversation opens at its newest message, and stays there after a note or a message is saved.** A view never scrolls, measures or focuses its page before the router attaches it. It waits until the page is on screen, and it does not hold the view up while it waits. *Why:* the thread was scrolled while its page was still detached, so it opened at the oldest message. *Round 39 (D-121): a support ticket too.*
> - **The device runs the server's whole check on an image before anything is saved.** A file that only starts like a PNG, JPG or WEBP but is cut short or has no picture in it gets "This file isn't a real PDF, JPG, PNG or WEBP. Choose the file again." in the same press as the form's other problems, on every form that uploads (Contact support, Add expense, Record payment, Report damage, Found an item, Inspect, check-in ID photos, listing photos, the guest payment proof). The server keeps the same check. *Extends D-112.* *Why:* a support ticket was created and then its screenshot was refused.
> - **An owner whose workspace is read-only starts on its home page,** with its banner (scheduled for deletion, trial ended or payment overdue), not on the Launchpad, whose steps it can't save. *Settles APP-FLOW section 1 against D-117 and APP-FLOW 48.2: D-117 wins.*
> - **Handlers read the pressed button before they wait.** A handler that waits (for a question or a request) reads `e.currentTarget` first, and a test checks every script in `public/js`. *Why:* round 36's Mark as solved read it after a question and stopped working.
> - **Tests never run on the dev database.** Every test file loads the test helpers before the app's own code, and the helpers refuse the `balayhub` database.
> - **The filter chip strip is as designed** (D-108): it appears under the bar when a filter is turned on, on every list. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **The sidebar menu scrolls,** with a fade along its bottom edge while more links are below; at 1280 by 900 every host item fits, and a platform admin's Admin item may sit under the fade until the menu is scrolled or Tab reaches it. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the two ways to add a listing (D-118); the hidden "You're offline" banner text in a focus read; the check-in guest row's remove sending focus to the heading; a short blank frame on a phone bottom-bar tap (watch); Back to Tenants takes about 30 ms (watch closed).
> *Why:* a person using the keyboard should reach every control on screen, a report should only show figures for the dates it names, a conversation should open where the guest last wrote, and a form should never save a record whose file the server will then refuse.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 37 gate): full suite 4253/4253 on the test database. Browser re-verify pass (groups A to D) after one late fix: below 960 px the inbox thread is not a scroll box, so the page itself brings the newest message into view, and only when it sits below the area above the bottom bar (`inbox.js` `scrollToNewest`). Security first BLOCK (the test helpers' dev-database guard was a text match a URL could slip past), then PASS after one late fix: the helpers resolve the database pg will really open (`pg-connection-string`, then `PGDATABASE`, then the user name) and refuse `balayhub` in any case. Suite runtime 13.1 min. BLOCKERS round 37 (a) is open, for information.*

> **D-120 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 38 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-eighth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 3 findings; bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money and ops filed none. 1 is real and significant: on a slow connection, saving an inbox note left focus on the page body for as long as the save took. 2 are not bugs: calendar booking bars on a phone are 26 px, above the 24 px the calendar is allowed (D-89, D-95), and the booking-page tick is in a closed section once a listing is already on the booking page, as D-104 decided. Checking the code for the same cause found 38 places in 20 files, the filed one included: a button (or a file box, a check box or a select) that had just been used was switched off with `disabled` while it waited for the server, and a switched-off control can't keep focus. On the local server each wait lasts 20 to 50 ms, under the 300 ms read, which is why the D-99 audit closed; on a slow connection every one of them reads the page body. One more place was found by reading, in a 21st file: the cleaner's My pay Next month button switched itself off when it reached this month, while it had focus. The round is classed hard: the busy-button rule of D-97 and D-105 was broken again in many files (the D-99 audit had closed it, but only fast saves were checked), and a dimmed control again lost focus (D-117). Every round 37 fix held where it was tested. One cause in two shapes, fixed in 21 code lanes (one per file), plus a scan test that keeps the class closed, a pins lane and a docs lane. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws), D-95 (calendar tiles stay 24 px), D-97 (a form's Save is marked busy with `aria-disabled`), D-99 (its audit, S7), D-104, D-105 (every form reports all its problems in one save; a read of BODY is a failure) and D-116 to D-119. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A control that is waiting for its own save keeps focus.** While a button, a file box, a check box or a select waits for the server after it was used, it is dimmed with `aria-disabled` and keeps focus. Pressing it again does nothing, and it is usable again when the wait ends. It is never switched off with `disabled`, which drops focus to the page. This covers every page: the inbox (Send, Save note, Draft reply with AI), the assistant, Help (Send, Send rating and the staff buttons), My account (notification Save, Sign out, Sign out other devices), Settings (Guest experience, the plan renewal and every Plan & billing pay button), Pricing bulk changes (Preview, Apply), Listings (photos, Sync now, Connect calendar), Rent (receipt Upload), Tenants (lease document Upload), the Maintenance and deposit photo boxes, Housekeeping (the assignment select and the checklist boxes), Admin (Delete), and the guest and public pages (sign-up, Try the live demo, Resend, Continue to payment, quote pay, Pay now, extras, payment proof, check-in and review). *Round 39 (D-121): it did not cover buttons that were never marked; those are now marked by the shared button builder.* *Extends D-97's focus rule 2 and D-105's OPS-2 to every control.* *Reopens D-99 S7 ("The `.disabled` audit is closed"), which is closed again here by a test.* *Why:* with a slow connection, saving a note left focus on the page body for the whole wait.
> - **A switch-off before anyone presses stays a plain switch-off.** A control that is off because the form isn't ready (Continue to payment while the dates are being priced or the guest count is too high, and fields locked after they are saved) keeps `disabled`. Focus is in the box being typed in, and the reason is on screen. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed (the booking page's Continue, D-118).*
> - **The cleaner's Next month is dimmed on this month,** keeps focus after the press that reached it, and does nothing. *Extends D-117.*
> - **A test keeps the class closed:** `test/qa-r38-busyscan.test.js` fails on any script that switches a control off with `disabled` and then waits, apart from two listed form states.
> - **Calendar booking bars stay at least 24 px on a phone** (D-89, D-95). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **After a calendar drag, focus is on the moved booking,** the control used (D-116, D-117, D-118). *Round 45 (D-127): the bar takes focus as the drag starts, so this holds on every browser.* *Annotates D-103's calendar line ("the redrawn cell that opened the menu") for a drag, which opens no menu.*
> - **Managers don't change pricing rules for every listing.** Rules and seasons that apply to the whole workspace are owner-only to change (BACKEND-SCHEMA section 7), so a manager sees them without Edit or Remove. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Tab can leave a dialog for the browser's own toolbar for one stop** before it comes back to the dialog's first control. The browser's modal dialog keeps the page behind it inert. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the booking-page tick in a closed section once a listing is on the page *(any listing in the workspace: D-121)* (D-104; the listing-card switch is still BLOCKERS round 22 (c)); the dimmed automation toggle that can't be produced (a template in use can't be archived); Taglish messages with English dates (a copy watch); links inside a sentence under 44 px (D-95).
> *Why:* a person using the keyboard or a screen reader should never be dropped on the page body, however slow the connection.
>
> *Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 38 gate): full suite 4378/4378 on the test database. Browser re-verify (groups A, B and C) pass; not run: Plan & billing renewal and pay buttons (the test owner is on a complimentary plan; covered by the unit tests), payment proof Send (manual payments off), guest review (no review link), Sync now (no channel on a QA unit). Three late fixes during the gate: a refusal with no field messages now shows the server's own words on Bulk changes, the Guest experience save and the booking page quote (each had read `fields ?`, which is always set); and the guest pay page's Add the screenshot is marked busy before its file check, so a quick second press cannot upload twice (security finding, Low, existed before this round). Security PASS. Suite runtime 14.1 min. BLOCKERS round 38 (a) is open, for information.*

> **D-121 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 39 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The thirty-ninth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 20 findings; every lane filed at least one. 16 are real: 2 significant (pressing "Email booking confirmation" twice on a slow connection emailed the guest twice; the "Invitation created" link box had no name for screen readers) and 14 minor. 14 of the 16 have one cause: a button that waited for the server without being dimmed, so in most cases a second press sent a second request. D-120's test only looked for buttons switched off with `disabled`, so it could not see a button that was never marked at all. Checking the code found the same cause behind about 90 buttons in 38 files. It is fixed once, in the helper that builds every button, plus 16 places that dropped their request or were a select or a check box. 4 findings are not bugs, and 1 note is a question for the founder. Every round 38 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: D-120's rule, said to cover every page, was broken again in a second form. Calls follow D-85 (problems for owners of 1 to 3 properties first, then money and guests), D-09 (host screens are in English), D-89 (one focus guard for redraws; on phones every button, tab, chip, back link and standalone link is at least 44px tall), D-95 (links inside a sentence are exempt), D-97 (a busy Save is marked with `aria-disabled`), D-104 (the booking-page section opens by itself only while no listing in the workspace is on the booking page), D-105 (every form reports its problems in its own banner and fields; a read of BODY is a failure), D-112 and D-113 (a refused file is said on the line under its box), D-115 (a dialog that sends a request says the server's refusal inside the dialog), and D-116 to D-120. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A button that waits for the server is dimmed and refuses a second press, on every page.** Any button whose press waits for a request (Email booking confirmation, Email receipt, Copy guidebook link, every Void and Cancel, Publish, Unpublish and Send on a statement, Mark clean, Start, Mark done, Reopen, Snooze, Archive, automatic message on and off, discount code on and off, coupon Apply and about 70 more) is marked busy with `aria-disabled` until the answer comes, keeps focus, and a second press does nothing. This is done once, by the shared button builder (`h()` in `public/js/ui.js`), for every button whose press hands back its request; a button dimmed before the press (D-117) keeps its own state. Every confirm button (Void, Cancel reservation, Remove room, Cancel group) is marked the same way while it sends. *Extends D-120's first bullet: D-120 covered buttons that were switched off with `disabled`; this covers buttons that were never marked.* *Round 42 (D-124): and a press that closed a dialog swallows its own second press.* *Round 44 (D-126): and a press whose answer came back fast swallows its own second press, on whatever button is drawn there.* *Why:* on a slow connection a second press emailed a guest twice and sent status changes twice.
> - **A press always hands its request back to the button.** A view never starts a request from a press and walks away from it. A select that saves on change marks itself and puts back a second change; a check box that saves on change refuses a second click. *Why:* a dropped request is invisible to the shared builder, which is how Publish, Mark done, Reopen, Snooze and the booking page's Turn on stayed clickable while they waited.
> - **A second test keeps the class closed:** `test/qa-r39-busyscan2.test.js` fails on a dropped request, on a select or check box that waits unmarked, on a waiting click handler that is not on a button, and if the shared guard is removed. Five places that are not presses are listed with their reasons.
> - **A support ticket opens at its newest entry,** like a conversation (D-119), on the owner's ticket and the admin's. *Extends D-119.*
> - **The invitation link box is named "Invitation link".** *Why:* it is the control that takes focus when the "Invitation created" dialog opens, and it had no name for screen readers.
> - **A dialog or a form says the server's refusal in its own alert, not a toast** (the lease Documents dialog, the check-in form's banner after the answers saved): D-115 and D-105. A refused file is still said under its box (D-113). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Phone standalone links are at least 44px tall** (D-89); width is not part of the rule (the inbox "Open" link is 44 px tall and 38 px wide, which is above the 24 px minimum in WCAG 2.5.8). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **The booking-page section of Edit listing stays closed once any listing in the workspace is on the booking page,** even when the listing being edited is not (D-104 as written). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* Send 12 px below the window at 1280x900 in a conversation (D-119's layout; measured again at 1366x768 in round 40). *Superseded by D-122: the reply box and Send now fit at 1280x900.* The paid-ahead coupon sentence (decided text, DECISIONS 1069; copy watch); Add listing opening after the first property (APP-FLOW 1834).
> - *Open:* online payment on a finished stay's pay link while money is owed (BLOCKERS round 39 (a)).
>
> *Why:* one press should do one thing, and a person should see that the app is working on it.

*Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 39 gate): full suite 4453/4453 on the test database (13 old tests re-pinned to the new text or given the stand-in methods the guard reads). Browser re-verify (groups A and B) pass, no step failed; not run because the accounts were not in that state: the lost-and-found select, the assignee select (needs a second person), the coupon box (absent on a live plan; covered by its test) and the booking page Turn on (already on). Security PASS (two informational notes: a rejected button handler no longer leaves a console trace, and a request the server never answers keeps its button refusing presses until the page changes; both carried). Suite runtime 13.4 min. BLOCKERS round 39 (a) is open, a founder question.*

> **D-122 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 40 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The fortieth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 8 findings; guest, phone and longterm filed none. 4 are real: 1 severe (pressing the saved-card Pay button twice, or Enter twice, could charge the card twice when the first payment answered quickly) and 3 minor (a payout could be recorded for a removed unit owner; a conversation's reply box started below the window on laptop screens; the calendar's week buttons were named "Earlier" and "Later"). 4 are not bugs: two were test-script timing, and two are D-121 as written. The double charge was not the button's guard failing: the button was dimmed and refused the second press while the first one waited. The first payment finished, the page redrew with a new Pay button in the same place and focus on it, and the next press was taken as paying for another month, which a fresh page was allowed to do (DECISIONS 1312). The server now refuses that press for a minute. The live site runs the same code until this round is deployed. The round is classed hard: a severe money bug with a gap on the server. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-97, D-98, D-102, D-108, D-115 and D-116 to D-121. No migration, no new env var, no schema change; two new refusal rules and one new field. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A payment is never taken twice for one press.** For one minute after any plan payment is paid, a one-tap payment with the saved card is refused with "That payment was just received. Check your plan before paying again." (409 `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`), checked under the workspace lock. Nothing is charged, written or held. After a minute, paying ahead works as before. The hosted checkout is unchanged: it charges nothing until the person pays on PayMongo's own page. After a paid one-tap payment or a free activation, focus goes to the "Plan & billing" heading, not the new Pay button. *Narrows DECISIONS 1312's "Paying ahead from a fresh page stays allowed" to "after a minute".* *Why:* a double click or a double Enter on a fast answer charged the card twice, because the second press landed on the redrawn page's new Pay button. Whether to also ask before a second payment within 10 minutes is still the founder's question (BLOCKERS round 26 (a)).
> - **A removed unit owner takes no new payout.** A payout for an owner who was removed is refused with "{name} was removed, so a payout can't be recorded. Payouts already recorded stay on their statements." (409 `OWNER_REMOVED`). The statement says "This owner was removed, so new payouts can't be recorded here." in place of Record payout. Past payouts stay listed and can be voided. Reserve withdrawals are unchanged on purpose. The Remove question adds "Record any payout you still owe them first." *Why:* removing an owner frees their units, so nothing could be checked as due, and a ₱100.00 payout was recorded for an owner with no units.
> - **A conversation's reply box and Send are on screen when it opens, on a laptop screen.** At 960 px and wider the thread gives up height (it keeps at least 160 px and still opens at its newest message), and Send sits on the same row as the Send-later field. At 1280x900 and taller Send is on screen; at 1366x768 the reply box is, and Send is a short scroll away. *Supersedes D-121's not-bug line about Send 12 px under the window (measured this round: Send was 40 to 280 px under it, and the reply box itself was under it at 768 and 900).*
> - **The calendar's week buttons are named "Previous week" and "Next week",** as on Housekeeping. *Why:* the buttons move the calendar seven days, and a screen reader user heard "Earlier" and "Later", which did not say so.
> - **A question that only asks closes when it is answered; its opener carries the wait.** Remove card, Replace link, Publish, Unpublish and Send close at the press, and the button that opened them is dimmed until the answer (D-121). A dialog that sends its own request (Void, Cancel reservation, Remove room, Cancel group, every reason dialog) stays open and dims its confirm (D-115, D-121). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **Starting an unassigned cleaning task names the person who started it** (DECISIONS 230: the person who works a job nobody was assigned to is assigned to it). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the keyboard-only skip link is 43 px tall (it is not a tap target, D-89); a click on plain page content after a dialog closed moves focus to the page (the person clicked elsewhere) *(measured again in round 41)* *Round 42 (D-124): a press within 600 ms and 30 px of the press that closed the dialog now does nothing.*; the housekeeping select's second change after its save finished (a new choice).
> - *For the testers:* a read at 300 ms is taken from the page directly. A Playwright locator, `selectOption` or `scrollIntoViewIfNeeded` waits for its match or for the control to stop being dimmed, which is how this round's double charge and second housekeeping save were first misread.
>
> *Why:* one press should take one payment, and a person should see where to type.

*Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 40 gate): full suite 4486/4486 on the test database (pins run: 4484/4486 first, the two expected billing pins, then 4486/4486); browser re-verify groups A and B all steps pass (one-tap double click and double Enter each saved exactly one paid row; a second pay within the minute answered 409 with no second provider call; removed-owner payout refused; inbox Send on screen at 1280x900, 1440x900 and 1920x1080, reply box top 679 of 768 at 1366x768); security PASS with one Low (a payout racing the owner's removal), fixed in the same round by lane OWNERLOCK: the payout route re-reads `archived_at` under the owner row lock and refuses with `OWNER_REMOVED` (test qa-r40-ownerlock); suite runtime 14.1 min.*

> **D-123 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 41 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-first QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 9 findings; onboarding, phone and longterm filed none. 3 are real and minor: Stay reports' stays count did not say it counts check-ins, while the owner statement and Profit and loss count stays that check out; the Supplies used dialog's row messages were not announced or tied to their quantity box; and the platform admin could skip the card check on a workspace scheduled for deletion. 6 are not bugs: two were round 40 rulings filed again (the Replace link second click, which this round measured as landing on plain text; Publish and Send), the season Save was a broken test read (the dialog stays open and dimmed while it saves), and three are the decision record as written (the booking page's greyed Continue, D-120; an owner's season starting on All listings, DECISIONS 1577; a bank return focusing its notice, DECISIONS 1262), plus the phone's long conversation (D-119: the bottom bar's Inbox tab is the way back). Every round 40 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed routine: three small causes in three places, no repeat bug, no decision conflict. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-105, D-116 and D-119 to D-122. No migration, no new env var, no schema change; one new refusal on an admin route. Then the bullets:
>
> - **The stays count on Stay reports says it counts check-ins.** The Revenue tile reads "{n} check-ins · {m} nights". Reports keep counting stays by check-in date (DECISIONS 1275), and owner statements and Profit and loss keep counting stays that checked out (D-37); each number now names its basis where it is read. *Why:* September showed 34 stays on Reports and 32 on the statement with nothing on the tile to say why.
> - **Every error line a view builds itself is an alert tied to its box,** as `field()` does: the Supplies used dialog's row messages ("Quantity can't be negative.", "Quantity must be a whole number.", "Enter a quantity for at least one item.", "Nothing on hand. Restock it first.") have `role="alert"` and the quantity box's `aria-describedby` points to them. *Extends D-121's "a dialog or a form says a refusal in its own alert".* *Why:* a screen reader heard "invalid" with no reason.
> - **The admin can't skip the card check on a workspace scheduled for deletion.** The panel shows "This workspace is scheduled for deletion, so the card check can't be skipped. Cancel the deletion first." in place of Skip card check, and the route refuses with "This workspace is scheduled for deletion, so the card check is turned off. Cancel the deletion first." (409 `DELETION_SCHEDULED`); nothing is written. After Cancel deletion the button is back. The admin's plan, paid-until and trial edits are unchanged. *Extends D-116 to the admin's route.* *Why:* skipping started a trial clock on a workspace about to be deleted.
> - **On a phone the bottom bar's Inbox tab is the way back from a long conversation.** A conversation opens at its newest message (D-119), so its "‹ Inbox" link is at the top of the page; the bottom bar is always on screen. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - **A form dialog stays open and dims its Save until the answer** (Save season and every form in a dialog); only a question that only asks closes at the press (D-122). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* a second click after a question closed lands on plain page content and moves focus to the page (measured: the spot under Replace link is the reservation's "No channel fee" line; without the second click focus stays on New link) *Round 42 (D-124): within 600 ms of the first press, at the same spot, it does nothing.* *Round 47 (D-129): after a form's Save too.*; a click on a button that was off before anyone pressed it (the booking page's Continue with no dates, D-120); an owner's season and pricing-rule pickers starting on "All listings" (DECISIONS 1577); a bank return focusing its "Payment received" notice (DECISIONS 1262). The channel table's and the CSV's "Stays" headers keep their names (copy watch).
> - *For the testers:* a lane that files again an item already ruled not a bug cites the fact that changed; a read is not trusted after a step that timed out (the season read came after a 30 s option-label timeout that ended the session).
>
> *Why:* a host should be able to match one report to another, and hear why a box is wrong.

> **D-124 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 42 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-second QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 5 findings; guest, inbox, phone, bookings and card filed none. 1 is real and minor, and it is a class: a second press after the press that closed a dialog landed on whatever was under it. On the cleaner's task page that was the "Add photo" label, which opened the file chooser (the camera on a phone); on a phone the same double tap opened the bottom bar's More sheet, on any page. The money tester's report about the statement's Send was the same double press (a single press keeps focus on Send again). 3 are not bugs: the first PayMongo key save focusing the dimmed Turn on (D-117 names that pair), a Sign out button partly under the floating Ask button (D-117), and the statement Send read after the tester's own second click (D-122). 1 goes to the founder (archived listings are found only under Filters). Every round 41 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the second press after a dialog closed was filed for the third round running and twice this round, and the fix narrows a D-122 not-bug line. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-117, D-121 and D-122. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A press that closes a dialog swallows its own second press.** After a mouse, pen or touch press inside a dialog closes it (a question's answer, a form that saved quickly, the ×, a tap outside), a second press within 600 ms and 30 px of it does nothing: no click, no focus change, no file chooser, no menu. Keyboard presses are unchanged (a second Enter lands on the dimmed opener, D-121). *Extends D-121 ("a second press does nothing") to the button that left with its dialog; narrows D-122's not-bug line to presses after 600 ms or away from the spot.* *Why:* a double click on "Mark done anyway" opened the cleaner's file chooser on a laptop-sized screen, and a double tap opened the More sheet on a phone, and the same press had been filed in rounds 40, 41 and 42. *Round 43 (D-125): the guard starts at the closing press itself.*
> - **The first PayMongo key save focuses the dimmed Turn on.** Connect PayMongo, Turn on and Turn off take each other's place (D-117 names Connect PayMongo to Turn on); a dimmed Turn on says why ("Tick at least one listing for your page first …"); Change key is a separate button after it. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* a control partly under the floating Ask button, at rest as well as while scrolling (D-117: none is fully hidden); Outstanding rent counting all unpaid rent with the overdue part on its sub-line (PRD 399; copy watch); Tagalog sign-ups seeing English host screens (D-09, BLOCKERS "Host app language" still open).
> - *For the testers:* a report about a second press says so in its steps, and reads focus both before and after the second press (round 42 carry item 2); a refiling of a ruled item names the changed fact (as OPS42-1 did, which is why it was taken).
>
> *Why:* a stray double tap should never open a file chooser (the camera on a phone) or a menu the person did not ask for.

Browser re-verify group A pass (A.4's one aria-disabled read did not reproduce in 6 runs; on the old ui.js the same double press dropped focus, the D-124 bug), group B pass. > **D-125 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 43 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-third QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 8 findings; guest, longterm, card and ops filed none. 4 are real and minor, from three causes: round 42's second-press guard started only when the browser ran the dialog's close event, a moment later, so a second press sent right after the first (5 ms later, by a test script) still fell on the page and focus dropped to the page body on two questions (Delete automatic message, Mark as solved); opening another page from a button inside a page left focus on the page body while the next page loaded (up to 273 ms in a busy run); and the Launchpad step and the help article did not mention the address the first listing needs. 4 are not bugs: the phone More sheet's hidden rows were a test script tapping rows that are not drawn; the 1366x768 reply box is D-122 as written; the Record payout messages do show together (the tester's own results say so); and the shorter first listing is the open founder question from round 21. Every round 42 fix held where it was tested at a person's speed. The round is classed hard: two findings this round in one shared helper, and the second-press class for the fourth round running. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-28, D-105, D-116, D-122 and D-124. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **The second-press guard starts at the press that closed the dialog.** D-124's rule is unchanged (a mouse, pen or touch press within 600 ms and 30 px of the press that closed a dialog does nothing); it now starts at that press, not when the browser later runs the dialog's close event, so even a second press sent a few milliseconds after the first is stopped. A dialog that closes later (a form whose answer comes back) starts it when it closes, as before. *Completes D-124.* *Why:* a back-to-back double click on "Delete" (automatic message) and on "Yes, it's solved" dropped focus to the page body about half the time; with 100 ms between the presses it was already stopped.
> - **Opening another page from inside a page never leaves focus on the page body.** When a button or link inside the page opens another page, focus waits on the page area while the next page loads (no ring), and the new page's heading takes it when the page is drawn. *Narrows round 29's "page-swap blank frame under about 100 ms" to the paint frame: a body read during a load is a bug.* *Round 44 (D-126): guest pages too.* *Why:* Message guest showed the page body for up to 273 ms in a busy run, and on a slow phone network it would be the whole load.
> - **The Launchpad and the help article name the address the first listing needs.** The step reads "Enter the listing name (for example Tower 1 - 1201) and the nightly rate, then choose the building, or add it with its street address, city and province (guests see only the city and province)."; the help article says an owner adds a new building right in the form, and a manager asks the owner. Whether the first listing should ask for less is still BLOCKERS round 21 (a).
> - **The phone More sheet's closed "More tools" rows are not drawn.** Only the visible rows can be tapped; a tap lands on what is on screen. *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* Send a short scroll away at 1366x768 with the reply box on screen (D-122); Record payout's amount and Method messages in one save (they show together); the 3-D Secure Fail sentence on the fake bank page (D-116, harness limit; the live answer is BLOCKERS "Live stage for card on file"); a date that is Manila's today while the UTC date is the day before; a manager opening Discount codes by its address (D-28).
> - *For the testers:* a row is tapped only when it is drawn: check `checkVisibility()` and that `elementFromPoint` at its centre is the row itself; `getBoundingClientRect` and `offsetParent` say nothing about a row inside a closed fold. A finding quotes the lane's own result file for the text it reports.
>
> *Why:* one press should count once however fast the second comes, focus should never sit on nothing while a page loads, and the first step should say what the form will ask.

*Gate addendum (2026-10-21, round 43 gate): full suite 4520/4520 on the test database (PINS first run 4518/4520: two stale pins, the Launchpad step line in `qa-r12-c13` and, not predicted, `qa-r13-f01` line 345, whose regex now also allows the focus hold between the page swap and the scroll). Browser re-verify groups A, B, C and D pass (back-to-back double press 10 of 10 on the heading; Message guest never on the page body in 5 of 5; PHN43-1 confirmed a tool artifact). Security PASS, no findings. Suite runtime 13.8 min. The clean-round counter stays at 0.*

> **D-126 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 44 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-fourth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 9 findings; longterm, phone and money filed none. 6 are real, from six causes: a double tap on a button whose answer came back fast landed on the button drawn in its place and undid the first press (Start work went back to open; Check in checked the guest out; Archive, Mark clean and every on and off button the same); guest pages left focus on the page body while the next page loaded (D-125 covered only the signed-in pages); the New quote and owner-portal listing pickers showed bare unit names; a refusal moved the guest Pay and Continue buttons so a quick second press landed on the message or unticked the consent box; a group with two rooms on one listing was refused with a banner and no room marked; and sign-up, reset password and the listing discounts said some problems only on the second save. 3 are not bugs: the card page's Pay button follows its fields; the phone conversation scrolls under the bottom bar like every page (D-119; D-122 is a laptop rule); and the two supply quantity messages name two different rules. Every round 43 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: one cause behind about 25 buttons in 15 files, the second-press class for the fifth round running, and a gap in D-125's own fix. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-105, D-117, D-119, D-121, D-122, D-124 and D-125. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **A press that waits for the server swallows its own second press.** After a mouse, pen or touch press on a button whose press waits for the server, any press within 600 ms and 30 px of it does nothing, whatever is drawn there by then (Start work and Back to open, Check in and Check out, Archive and Unarchive, Mark clean and Mark dirty, Turn on and Turn off, Publish, Snooze and the rest). Keyboard presses are unchanged. The guest pages' Continue to payment, Pay and Add selected to my bill do the same. A press 600 ms or more later, or a press on a button away from the spot, works as before. *Completes D-121 (refused while waiting) and D-124 (after a dialog closed).* *Round 45 (D-127): a change the person makes after the press ends the guard.* *Round 47 (D-129): every form's Save and the guest check-in Submit registration too.* *Why:* each answer came back in 5 to 20 ms, so a double tap's second press landed on the new button: a repair went back to open, and a guest was checked in and straight out.
> - **Guest pages hold focus while the next page loads.** When a link inside a guest page (a listing card, a link back to the host page) opens another guest page, a placeholder holds focus while it loads (no ring), and the new page's heading takes it. *Completes D-125, which covered the signed-in pages.* *Why:* the host page's listing card left focus on the page body for the whole load (3 s with a slow answer).
> - **Listing pickers name the building.** New quote and the owner portal's Block dates show "Unit — Building", like the reservation, group and Move-to pickers.
> - **A group's two rooms on one listing are marked on the room.** The later room's Listing box says "Room N already has this listing on nights that overlap. Choose another listing or change the dates." in the same save as every other problem; nothing is sent.
> - **Checks that need no server are said in the first save.** Sign-up's password length, match and Terms box, reset password's match, and a listing's discount order show with the required boxes, in one save (D-105). *Round 43's Record payout ruling stands: that form already did this.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the card page's Pay button below the window at 1280x900 (a form's button follows its fields; the last field is on screen); the phone conversation's buttons under the bottom bar at rest (the page scrolls, D-119; D-122 is a laptop rule); the owner's Record use "at least 1" and the cleaner's "can't be negative" (two rules); at 1366x768 the reply box counts as on screen with its border's last 7 px below the window (D-122's gate measured its top at 679); the gated Account page's Edit profile, Change password and Sign out (APP-FLOW line 57: Account stays reachable); a second Enter on a replacement (keyboard presses are unchanged, D-124); check boxes that save on change (a second tap is a second change).
> - *Open:* a payout to an owner with no units skips the over-payment question too (BLOCKERS "Payouts to an owner with no units", raised again).
> - *For the testers:* a double press on a button that waits for the server now counts once at any speed; a lane that tests "a press after a press" waits at least 1 s.
>
> *Why:* one press should do one thing however fast the next comes, focus should never sit on nothing while any page loads, and a form should say everything it knows in one save.

> **D-127 · 2026-10-21 · QA round 45 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-fifth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 5 findings; phone, onboarding, card, inbox and ops filed none. 4 are real, from four causes: round 44's double-press guard also swallowed a deliberate press that came within 600 ms of a press the page had refused on its own (a Void payment pressed with no reason, then pressed again just after a reason was typed, did nothing); after a calendar drag-move, focus landed on the page heading instead of the moved booking on browsers that do not focus a button on a mouse press; the discount code date message named boxes that are not on the form and said "after" where the same day is allowed; and the pricing rule Days label read like a blank to fill in, and every message built from it repeated it. 1 is not a bug: a refusal on the guest booking page still moves Continue to payment (round 44 ruled this; the press guard makes the second press safe). Every round 44 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed hard: the second-press class for the sixth round running, and a gap in D-126's own fix. Calls follow D-85, D-09, D-89, D-103, D-105, D-117, D-120, D-121, D-124, D-125 and D-126. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API shape change (one refusal message's words change). Then the bullets:
>
> - **A change ends the press guard.** After a press, the 600 ms guard of D-124 and D-126 ends as soon as the person changes something on the page (types, pastes, ticks a box, picks an option): the next press is a new one. A double press with nothing changed between still counts once. Only the person's own input counts; a box the page fills itself keeps the guard. *Completes D-126.* *Why:* a Void payment pressed before its reason was typed swallowed the press that came just after the reason, and nothing said why.
> - **After a calendar drag, focus is on the moved booking, on every browser.** The bar takes focus when its drag starts, so the move question closes back onto it, and the reload puts focus on the moved bar (after a refused move, on the bar in its old row). *Completes D-120, which held only where a mouse press focuses a button (Chromium).*
> - **The discount code dates say their own names.** Valid until before Valid from: "Valid until must be on or after Valid from." (the same day is allowed, and always was). The server's refusal uses the same words.
> - **Pricing rule labels are names, not blanks.** The Days box reads "Stay starts within this many days", "Stay starts more than this many days away" or "Gap is up to this many nights", and its messages say "Days" ("Days is required.").
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the guest booking page's Continue to payment moving under a refusal (round 44's ruling and D-126's guard stand; the balance page's button moves the same way: the lane measured it after centring it); a quick second press with nothing changed after a refused press (counts once, D-126); the automation Turn off that sent nothing once (a test script clicked an old position); the manager's 403 on guest settings (BACKEND-SCHEMA: owners only); the daily close line naming the date twice (round 27's format); a one-frame (17 ms) page-body read after the one-tap pay press (below the three-frame threshold).
> - *For the testers:* Playwright's mouse drag (down, move in steps, up) starts a calendar bar's drag in Chromium; use it rather than built DragEvents. Read a button's position after scrolling it into view, and check `elementFromPoint` before every press.
>
> *Why:* a press that the person meant should never be lost, focus should land on the thing that was moved, and a message should use the words on the screen.

PINS first run 4,578/6, exactly the expected 6 failing pins in 5 old files. > **D-128 · 2026-10-27 · QA round 46 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-sixth QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 4 findings, all minor; money, phone, longterm, bookings, ops and inbox filed none. 2 are real: the building admin's gate pass page for a wrong or expired link still showed the footer of a live pass ("Private gate pass. Please don't forward…"), and Plan & billing said "try again in 1 minutes" in a waiting payment's last minute. 2 are not bugs: the shorter first Add listing and the booking page tick are open founder questions (BLOCKERS round 21 (a) and round 22 (c)). Of six things the testers noticed but did not file, one is real: Change password checked nothing on the device, so a person with two mistakes (a short or mismatched new password, a blank current one) needed two saves to see both; four are as designed (the admin's Delete needs the workspace name typed, D-100; a hint and a message both under Percent off; blank checklist lines are dropped; a new owner sets up the workspace before confirming the card, D-98), and one is a new founder question (removing the card while a bank payment waits). A sign-out seen on the phone was the test script pressing Log out, not a session problem. Every round 45 fix held where it was tested. The round is classed routine. Calls follow D-09, D-98, D-100, D-104, D-105, D-125 and D-126. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API change, no server change. Then the bullets:
>
> - **The gate pass page shows its footer only with a pass.** A link that is not valid, has expired or could not be read shows "Gate pass not available" and the reason, without "Private gate pass. Please don't forward. The link stops working after check-out." *Why:* the footer spoke about a pass that was not there.
> - **A waiting payment's minutes count right.** "…or try again in 1 minute." (and "2 minutes", as before), the same as every other wait in BalayHub.
> - **Change password reports every problem it can see in one save.** A new password shorter than 8 characters and a confirmation that does not match are shown together with any empty box, before anything is sent, in the server's words ("New password must be at least 8 characters.", "The passwords do not match."). Only a wrong current password needs the server. *Extends D-105 and round 44's sign-up and reset fix to Change password (Settings and My account).*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* the shorter first Add listing (open, BLOCKERS round 21 (a)); new listings off the booking page with the tick folded in Add listing (as D-104 decided; open, BLOCKERS round 22 (c)); the admin's Delete now and Delete in 14 days need only the typed workspace name (D-100); "Fill in this or the amount." (the hint) and "Set either a percent off or an amount off." (the message) under Percent off; Edit checklist dropping blank lines ("One item per line"); Setup before Confirm your card (D-98); a sign-out during a phone test (the script pressed Log out: it read the position of a link hidden in the closed "More tools" fold, which sits under Log out; for a person the fold shows only Log out there).
> - *For the testers:* a link inside a closed fold still reports a position in Chromium, under whatever is drawn there; check `checkVisibility()` and `elementFromPoint` before every coordinate press. Never change the password of, or sign out other devices of, a shared test login.
>
> *Why:* a page should say only what is true of what it shows, and a form should name every problem it can see at once.

> **D-129 · 2026-10-27 · QA round 47 calls (alpha with small hosts).** The forty-seventh QA sweep ran nine browser lanes (bookings, card, guest, inbox, longterm, money, onboarding, ops, phone). The lanes filed 7 findings, one marked significant and six minor; card, longterm and ops filed none. 3 are real, from one class: a quick second press after a press that waited for the server still did something on forms. Every form's Save (Record payout, Send to support and every other form) had never been given round 44's guard, so a second press closed the "Pay more than the statement?" question that the first press's answer had just opened, and after Send to support opened the new ticket, a second press moved focus to the page body. On the guest check-in page a second press after a quick failure unticked the consent box. (The significant report, a slow failed save that moved focus to the consent box, was the test script pressing again late at the button's old place; the quick case is the real one.) 2 more are real and small: the owner's New quote price check refused dates more than 18 months ahead, and stays over 60 nights, with the guest booking page's words ("Please contact the host"), although the host may quote and book those dates; and Setup said the next step was the first listing when it is confirming the payment card (D-98). Profit and loss and the emailed owner statement said "1 nights". 2 are not bugs: Pricing and Discount codes are opened from Calendar and Guests, not from the menu; "Match device" sits on one line under its icon in the More sheet. The round is classed hard: the second-press class is back, in the most common button in the app. Every round 46 fix held where it was tested. Calls follow D-85, D-98, D-103, D-104, D-120 and D-123 to D-128. No migration, no new env var, no schema change, no API shape change; one server change (the host's quote price check, which now prices any future dates) and one emailed-statement word; the dev server restarts once. Then the bullets:
>
> - **Every form's Save swallows its own second press.** After a mouse, pen or touch press on a form's Save (Save payout, Send to support, every form in a dialog or on a page) any press within 600 ms and 30 px of it does nothing, whatever the answer drew there: it does not answer or close a question that just opened, and it does not move focus off the page that just opened. A change the person makes ends it (D-127); Enter is unchanged; a press 600 ms later or on another control works. The guest check-in page's Submit registration does the same, so a quick second press after a failed save cannot untick the consent box. *Completes D-126, which reached buttons with their own press handler and the guest Pay and Continue buttons, not a form's Save.* *Narrows D-123's not-bug line again: a press on plain content within 600 ms and 30 px of a Save does nothing.* *Why:* a double click on Save payout lost the over-payment question, and a double click on Send to support left focus on the page body.
> - **The host's quote price check takes any dates the host may quote.** The owner's or manager's New quote prices a stay more than 18 months ahead or longer than 60 nights; those two limits stay on the guest booking page, where they tell the guest to contact the host. *Why:* the check told the host to contact the host, and made them type the price, for dates the quote and the reservation accept. *Security pass (round 47, Low): the host path still refuses a stay over one year (366 nights), "Stays are limited to one year.", the same limit New reservation uses (stays.js).*
> - **Setup names the next screen.** While the card check is on (D-98), Setup says "Next, you'll confirm your payment card, then add your first listing or property." A platform admin, and a Setup page whose plan read fails, keep the old sentence: "Next, you'll add your first listing or property."
> - **Count words agree with the count:** Profit and loss's Gross line "1 stay · 1 night" and the emailed owner statement "(1 night, …)". *Extends D-128's "1 minute".*
> - **A slow failed check-in save keeps focus on Submit registration** and shows the message in the alert at the top (D-120, measured to 7.5 s). *Not a bug; recorded so it is not re-filed.*
> - *Not bugs / not changed:* Pricing and Discount codes are not menu entries: they open from Calendar's **₱ Pricing rules** and Guests' **% Discount codes** (owners and managers); staff, who only view pricing, find it in the top bar search; "Match device" in the More sheet is an icon over a one-line label, the same height as Light and Dark (round 18); "Cancel this cleaning task?" is asked on the task's own page, whose heading names it; the 3-D Secure Fail sentence at the fake bank (a harness limit, DECISIONS 1630); a More sheet pick leaves focus on More until the new heading is drawn (never the page body; D-103, D-125); an unknown address such as `#/expenses` goes to the home page (APP-FLOW 21); Remove block is the button of the "Remove this block?" question.
> - *For the testers:* read a button's state through an element handle, not a locator named by its label (a waiting button says "Saving…", and the locator waits until the label comes back); a press is reported in the steps when the script makes it; never write a password into a result file; never call a remote-session tool, even a read-only one.
>
> *Why:* one press should do one thing on every button, a rule written for guests should not stop the host, and a sentence should say what comes next.
